“I’ve got every line,” said the nurse enthusiastically. “My brother is a reporter on the *Megaphone*, and he says this is the best story they’ve had for years﻿—”

===Chinese===
「我可是每一行都收着呢。」护士起劲地说。「我哥哥是《传声筒报》的记者，他说这是他们多年来经手的最精彩的一桩新闻——」

===Original===
Mirabelle interrupted this somewhat gruesome conversation to make inquiries about luncheon. Her head was steady now and she had developed an appetite.

===Chinese===
米拉贝尔打断了这段多少有些瘆人的谈话，问起午饭来。她的头此刻已经不昏了，胃口也开了。

===Original===
The front door stood open, and as she turned to go into the dining-room to get her writing materials, she heard an altercation at the gate. A third man had appeared: a grimy-looking pedlar who carried a tray before him, packed with all manner of cheap buttons and laces. He was a middle-aged man with a ragged beard, and despite the warmth of the day, was wearing a long overcoat that almost reached to his heels.

===Chinese===
前门敞着，她转身正要进饭厅去取纸笔，忽听得院门口起了口角。来了第三个男人：一个邋里邋遢模样的货郎，胸前挂着一副托盘，塞满了各式各样的廉价纽扣和花边。他是个中年人，蓄着一部蓬乱的胡须，虽是天热，却罩着一件几乎垂到脚跟的长外套。

===Original===
“You may or you may not be,” the man with the pipe was saying, “but you’re not going in here.”

===Chinese===
「你或许是、或许不是，」抽烟斗的男人正说着，「反正这儿你进不得。」

===Original===
“I’ve served this house for years,” snarled the pedlar. “What do you mean by interfering with me? You’re not a policeman.”

===Chinese===
「这户人家的买卖我做了好些年。」货郎咆哮道。「你从中作梗是什么意思？你又不是警察。」

===Original===
“Whether I’m a policeman or a dustman or a postman,” said the patient guard, “you don’t pass through this gate﻿—do you understand that?”

===Chinese===
「管我是警察、是垃圾夫、还是邮差，」那看守好性儿地说，「这道门你就是过不去——这话你可明白？」

===Original===
At this moment the pedlar caught sight of the girl at the door and raised his battered hat with a grin. He was unknown to the girl; she did not remember having seen him at the house before. Nor did Alma, who came out at that moment.

===Chinese===
就在这时，货郎一眼瞥见门口的姑娘，咧嘴一笑，掀了掀那顶破帽子。姑娘不认识他，不记得先前在这宅子里见过这个人。此刻走出来的阿尔玛也不认识。

===Original===
“He’s a stranger here, but we’re always getting new people up from Gloucester,” she said. “What does he want to sell?”

She stalked out into the garden, and at the sight of her the grin left the pedlar’s face.

===Chinese===
「他在这一带是个生面孔，不过格洛斯特上来的人里，我们常常见到新的。」她说。「他要卖什么？」

她大步流星走进园子；货郎一看见她，脸上的嬉笑顿时敛了。

===Original===
“I’ve got some things I’d like to sell to the young lady, ma’am,” he said.

“I’m not so old, and I’m a lady,” replied Alma sharply. “And how long is it since you started picking and choosing your customers?”

===Chinese===
「我有几样东西，想卖与这位年轻小姐，太太。」他说。

「我没那么老，我正当是个小姐。」阿尔玛尖刻地回敬。「再说，你从几时起，居然挑拣起主顾来了？」

===Original===
The man grumbled something under his breath, and without waiting even to display his wares, shuffled off along the dusty road, and they watched him until he was out of sight.

===Chinese===
那人嘴里低低咕哝了句什么，连货也懒得摆，便拖着脚步顺着尘土飞扬的大路走远了；他们望着他的背影，直到望不见为止。

===Original===
Heavytree Farm was rather grandly named for so small a property. The little estate followed the road to Heavytree Lane, which formed the southern boundary of the property. The lane itself ran at an angle to behind the house, where the third boundary was formed by a hedge dividing the farmland from the more pretentious estate of a local magnate. It was down the lane the pedlar turned.

===Chinese===
赫维特里农场这块产业不大，名字倒起得挺堂皇。这一小片产业沿着大路延展到赫维特里巷，巷子便是产业的南界。巷子本身斜斜折到宅子背后，第三面的界是一道树篱，把这片农田同当地一位乡绅更有气派的庄园林地隔开。货郎拐进去的，正是这条巷子。

===Original===
“Excuse me, ma’am,” said the companion of the man with the pipe.

===Chinese===
「失陪片刻，太太。」抽烟斗男人的同伴说。

===Original===
He opened the gate, walked in, and, making a circuit of the house, reached the orchard behind. Here a few outhouses were scattered, and, clearing these, he came to the meadow, where Mirabelle’s one cow ruminated in the lazy manner of her kind. Half hidden by a thick-boled apple-tree, the watcher waited, and presently, as he expected, he saw a head appear through the boundary hedge. After an observation the pedlar sprang into the meadow and stood, taking stock of his ground. He had left his tray and his bag, and, running with surprising swiftness for a man of his age, he gained a little wooden barn, and, pulling open the door, disappeared into its interior. By this time the guard had been joined by his companion and they had a short consultation, the man with the pipe going back to his post before the house, whilst the other walked slowly across the meadow until he came to the closed door of the barn.

===Chinese===
他推开院门走进来，绕着宅子兜了半圈，到了后面的果园。这里疏疏落落散布着几间杂屋；绕过这些，便到了草场——米拉贝尔家独一的那头奶牛，正以它同类的慵懒姿态反刍。看守把身子半掩在一株粗干苹果树后守候着，不多时，果然如他所料，一颗脑袋从界篱里钻了出来。观察一阵之后，货郎纵身跳进草场，站定，四下打量地势。他的托盘和提包已然撂下；以他这把年纪而论，跑得快得出奇，三两下便抢到一座小木棚跟前，拉开门，闪身进了棚里。这时看守的同伴也赶了过来，两人略作商议：抽烟斗的回宅子正面的岗位上去了，另一个则缓缓穿过草场，走到木棚紧闭的门前。

===Original===
Wise in his generation, he first made a circuit of the building, and discovered there were no exits through the blackened gates. Then, pulling both doors open wide:

“Come out, bo’!” he said.

===Chinese===
他老于此道，先绕着棚子转了一圈，查明几扇发黑的板门后头并无别的出口。随后他把两扇门一齐拉敞：

「出来，小子！」他说。

===Original===
The barn was empty, except for a heap of hay that lay in one corner and some old and wheelless farm-wagons propped up on three trestles awaiting the wheelwright’s attention.

===Chinese===
棚里空空如也，只有墙角堆着一垛干草，另有几辆没了轮子的旧农用大车，架在三脚木架上，等着车轮匠来拾掇。

===Original===
A ladder led to a loft, and the guard climbed slowly. His head was on a level with the dark opening, when:

“Put up your hands!”

He was looking into the adequate muzzle of an automatic pistol.

===Chinese===
一架梯子通向阁楼，看守慢慢地往上爬。他的头刚齐那个黑黢黢的洞口，忽听——

「举起手来！」

他正对着一支自动手枪那口径宽裕的枪口。

===Original===
“Come down, bo’!”

“Put up your hands,” hissed the voice in the darkness, “or you’re a dead man!”

The watcher obeyed, cursing his folly that he had come alone.

===Chinese===
「下来，小子！」

「举起手来，」黑暗里那声音嘶嘶地说，「不然你就成了死人！」

看守依言照办，一面暗骂自己糊涂，竟独自一个闯了进来。

===Original===
“Now climb up.”

With some difficulty the guard brought himself up to the floor level.

===Chinese===
「现在，爬上来。」

看守费了些气力，才把自己弄上阁楼的地面。

===Original===
“Step this way, and step lively,” said the pedlar. “Hold your hands out.”

He felt the touch of cold steel on his wrist, heard a click.

===Chinese===
「这边走，走麻利点。」货郎说。「手伸出来。」

他只觉手腕上一片冰凉的钢铁挨上来，又听得咔哒一声。

===Original===
“Now the other hand.”

The moment he was manacled, the pedlar began a rapid search.

===Chinese===
「换另一只手。」

他一上铐，货郎便即刻动手搜身。

===Original===
“Carry a gun, do you?” he sneered, as he drew a pistol from the man’s hip pocket. “Now sit down.”

In a few seconds the discomfited guard was bound and gagged. The pedlar, crawling to the entrance of the loft, looked out between a crevice in the boards. He was watching not the house, but the hedge through which he had climbed. Two other men had appeared there, and he grunted his satisfaction. Descending into the barn, he pulled away the ladder and let it fall on the floor, before he came out into the open and made a signal.

===Chinese===
「还带着枪，是么？」他从那人裤后袋里抽出一支手枪，讥诮道。「坐下。」

不多几秒，吃了亏的看守已被捆了个结实，嘴里也塞上了东西。货郎爬到阁楼入口处，从木板缝里向外张望。他望的不是宅子，而是他翻过来的那道界篱。那儿又出现了两条汉子，他满意地哼了一声。他下到棚里，抽走梯子撂在地上，然后走到明处，打了个信号。

===Original===
The second guard had made his way back by the shortest cut to the front of the house, passing through the garden and in through the kitchen door. He stopped to shoot the bolts, and the girl, coming into the kitchen, saw him.

===Chinese===
另一名看守抄最短的道赶回宅子正面，穿过园子，打厨房门进了屋。他停下来插门闩；姑娘走进厨房，正看见他。

===Original===
“Is anything wrong?” she asked anxiously.

“I don’t know, miss.” He was looking at the kitchen windows: they were heavily barred. “My mate has just seen that pedlar go into the barn.”

===Chinese===
「出了什么事吗？」她焦灼地问。

「说不准，小姐。」他正打量厨房的窗户：窗上装着粗重的铁栏。「我的搭档方才看见那货郎钻进木棚里去了。」

===Original===
She followed him to the front door. He had turned to go, but, changing his mind, came back, and she saw him put his hand into his hip pocket and was staggered to see him produce a long-barrelled Browning.

===Chinese===
她跟着他走到前门。他本已转身要走，又变了主意折回来；她瞧见他把手伸进裤后袋，竟掏出一支长管的勃朗宁手枪来，不由得愣住了。

===Original===
“Can you use a pistol, miss?”

She nodded, too surprised to speak, and watched him as he jerked back the jacket and put up the safety catch.

===Chinese===
「手枪您会使么，小姐？」

她点了点头，惊得说不出话来，只瞧着他猛地把衣襟向后一撩，推上了保险。

===Original===
“I want to be on the safe side, and I’d feel happier if you were armed.”

===Chinese===
「我求个万全，您手里也添件家伙，我心里更踏实些。」

===Original===
There was a gun hanging on the wall and he took it down.

“Have you any shells for this?” he asked.

She pulled open the drawer of the hall-stand and took out a cardboard carton.

“They may be useful,” he said.

===Chinese===
墙上挂着一杆枪，他把它取了下来。

「这枪有子弹吗？」他问。

她拉开门厅衣帽柜的抽屉，取出一只纸板盒来。

「兴许用得上。」他说。

===Original===
“But surely, Mr.﻿—”

“Digby.” He supplied his name.

“Surely you’re exaggerating? I don’t mean that you’re doing it with any intention of frightening me, but there isn’t any danger to us?”

===Chinese===
「可是说真的，您是——」

「迪格比。」他自报了名姓。

「您总不至于言过其实吧？我倒不是说您存心吓唬我，只是我们并没有什么危险，是吧？」

===Original===
“I don’t know. I’ve got a queer feeling﻿—had it all morning. How far is the nearest house from here?”

===Chinese===
「说不准。我心里有一种古怪的预感——打一早上就有。这儿离最近的人家有多远？」

===Original===
“Not half a mile away,” she said.

“You’re on the phone?”

She nodded.

===Chinese===
「不到半英里。」她说。

「家里通着电话吧？」

她点了点头。

===Original===
“I’m scared, maybe. I’ll just go out into the road and have a look round. I wish that fellow would come back,” he added fretfully.

===Chinese===
「兴许是我自己胆怯。我出去到大路上张望一圈。巴不得那家伙早点回来才好。」他又烦躁地添了一句。

===Original===
He walked slowly up the garden path and stood for a moment leaning over the gate. As he did so, he heard the rattle and asthmatic wheezing of an ancient car, and saw a tradesman’s trolley come round a corner of Heavytree Lane. Its pace grew slower as it got nearer to the house, and opposite the gate it stopped altogether. The driver getting down with a curse, lifted up the battered tin bonnet, and, groping under the seat, brought out a long spanner. Then, swift as thought, he half turned and struck at Digby’s head. The girl heard the sickening impact, saw the watcher drop limply to the path, and in another second she had slammed the door and thrust home the bolts.

===Chinese===
他缓步走上园中小径，倚着院门站了片刻。正在这时，他听得见一辆旧车哐啷作响、喘得像个哮喘病人似的，又看见一辆商贩的送货篷车从赫维特里巷的拐角转了出来。离宅子越近它走得越慢，到了院门对面，索性停住了。司机骂骂咧咧地下了车，掀起那凹瘪的铁皮车头盖，又在座位底下摸索一通，掏出一把长扳手来。说时迟那时快，他半转过身，朝迪格比的头顶抡去。姑娘听见那叫人心惊肉跳的一声闷响，看见看守软软地瘫倒在石径上；下一秒钟，她已经砰地摔上门，把门闩死死推进了槽里。

===Original===
She was calm; the hand that took the revolver from the hall-table did not tremble.

===Chinese===
她很镇定；从门厅桌上拿起那支左轮枪的手，一丝也不曾发抖。

===Original===
“Alma!” she called, and Alma came running downstairs.

“What on earth﻿—?” she began, and then saw the pistol in Mirabelle’s hands.

===Chinese===
「阿尔玛！」她喊道，阿尔玛奔下楼来。

「这到底是——」她刚开口，就看见了米拉贝尔手里的手枪。

===Original===
“They are attacking the house,” said the girl quickly. “I don’t know who ‘they’ are, but they’ve just struck down one of the men who was protecting us. Take the gun, Alma.”

===Chinese===
「有人在攻打这宅子。」姑娘急急地说。「我不知道『他们』是谁，可他们刚打倒了保护我们的人里的一个。拿着枪，阿尔玛。」

===Original===
Alma’s face was contorted, and might have expressed fear or anger or both. Mirabelle afterwards learnt that the dominant emotion was one of satisfaction to find herself in so warlike an environment.

===Chinese===
阿尔玛的脸扭曲了，那神情或许是恐惧，或许是愤怒，或许两者都有。米拉贝尔事后才知道，其中占上风的情绪原来是心满意足——发现自己置身于这样一个剑拔弩张的境地，她正中下怀。

===Original===
Running into the drawing-room, the girl pushed open the window, which commanded a view of the road. The gate was unfastened and two men, who had evidently been concealed inside the trolley, were lifting the unconscious man, and she watched, with a calm she could not understand in herself, as they threw him into the interior and fastened the tailboard. She counted four in all, including the driver, who was climbing back to his seat. One of the newcomers, evidently the leader, was pointing down the road towards the lane, and she guessed that he was giving directions as to where the car should wait, for it began to go backwards almost immediately and with surprising smoothness, remembering the exhibition it had given of decrepitude a few minutes before.

===Chinese===
姑娘奔进客厅，推开那扇望得见大路的窗子。院门开着，两个先前分明藏在篷车里的男人，正抬起那不省人事的人；她怀着连自己都莫名其妙的一团镇定，看着他们把他扔进车厢，扣上后挡板。她数了数，连司机在内一共四个人；司机正爬回自己的座位。新来的人里有一个显然是头领，正朝大路那头的巷子指指点点；她猜他是在吩咐车该在哪里等候，因为那车几乎立刻就倒退起来，而且顺溜得出奇——想想它几分钟之前还表演过一番老朽衰迈呢。

===Original===
The man who had given instructions came striding down the path towards the door.

“Stop!”

He looked round with a start into the levelled muzzle of a Browning, and his surprise would, in any other circumstances, have been comical.

“It’s all right, miss﻿—” he began.

“Put yourself outside that gate,” said Mirabelle coolly.

“I wanted to see you﻿ ﻿… very important﻿—”

===Chinese===
发号施令的那人顺着小径大步朝宅门走来。

「站住！」

他吃了一惊，回头正对上一支平举的勃朗宁枪口；换在任何别的场合，他那份错愕本该叫人发笑。

「不要紧的，小姐——」他开口道。

「你给我退到院门外面去。」米拉贝尔冷冷地说。

「我要见你……有顶要紧的事——」

===Original===
*Bang!*

Mirabelle fired a shot, aimed above his head, towards the old poplar. The man ducked and ran. Clear of the gate he dropped to the cover of a hedge, where his men already were, and she heard the murmur of their voices distinctly, for the day was still, and the far-off chugging of the trolley’s engine sounded close at hand. Presently she saw a head peep round the hedge.

===Chinese===
*砰！*

米拉贝尔开了一枪，瞄着他头顶上方、朝着那棵老白杨打去。那人一缩脖子，撒腿便跑。一出院门，便扑进一道树篱后藏身，他的手下早已躲在那儿；这天四野静得很，他们的低语声她听得清清楚楚，连远处篷车引擎的突突声也仿佛近在耳边。不多时，她看见一颗脑袋从树篱后探出来。

===Original===
“Can I have five minutes’ talk with you?” asked the leader loudly.

He was a thickset, bronzed man, with a patch of lint plastered to his face, and she noted unconsciously that he wore gold earrings.

===Chinese===
「能同你说五分钟的话么？」那头领高声问道。

他是个矮壮汉子，古铜色的脸，脸上贴着一块纱布；她还下意识地留意到，他耳朵上挂着金耳环。

===Original===
“There’s no trouble coming to you,” he said, opening the gate as he spoke. “You oughtn’t to have fired, anyway. Nobody’s going to hurt you﻿—”

He had advanced a yard into the garden as he spoke.

===Chinese===
「没有祸事要落到你头上。」他一边说，一边推开了院门。「反正你不该开枪。谁也不会伤害你——」

说话间，他已朝园子里跨进了一码。

===Original===
*Bang, bang!*

In her haste she had pressed butt and trigger just a fraction too long, and, startled by the knowledge that another shot was coming, her hand jerked round, and the second shot missed his head by the fraction of an inch. He disappeared in a flash, and a second later she saw their hats moving swiftly above the box. They were running towards the waiting car.

===Chinese===
*砰！砰！*

她慌忙之中，握着枪柄扣扳机的工夫稍稍长了一瞬；惊觉又有一发将出，手上一歪，第二枪擦着他的脑袋，只差毫厘。他一闪身便没了影，一秒钟后，她看见他们的帽子在黄杨树篱上方飞快地移动——他们正朝那辆候着的车奔去。

===Original===
“Stay here, Alma!”

Alma Goddard nodded grimly, and the girl flew up the stairs to her room. From this elevation she commanded a better view. She saw them climb into the van, and in another second the limp body of the guard was thrown out into the hedge; then, after a brief space of time, the machine began moving and, gathering speed, disappeared in a cloud of dust on the Highcombe Road.

===Chinese===
「你守在这儿，阿尔玛！」

阿尔玛·戈达德狠狠点了点头，姑娘飞奔上楼进了自己的房间。从这高处望下去，视野更清楚。她看见他们爬进篷车，转瞬间，看守那软瘫的身子便被扔出车外，丢进了树篱；隔了一小会儿，车开动起来，越走越快，在海科姆路（Highcombe Road）上拖着一股尘土，消失不见了。

===Original===
Mirabelle came down the stairs at a run, pulled back the bolts and flew out and along the road towards the still figure of the detective. He was lying by the side of the ditch, his head a mass of blood, and she saw that he was still breathing. She tried to lift him, but it was too great a task. She ran back to the house. The telephone was in the hall: an old-fashioned instrument with a handle that had to be turned, and she had not made two revolutions before she realized that the wire had been cut.

===Chinese===
米拉贝尔飞奔下楼，拉开门闩，冲出去顺着大路奔向那暗探僵卧不动的人影。他躺在沟边，满头是血，她看得出他还有气息。她想把人抱起来，可这活计太重，她力不能胜。她跑回宅子。电话机在门厅里：老式的机子，带一只要摇的柄；她还没摇上两圈，便明白过来——线早叫人剪断了。

===Original===
Alma was still in the parlour, the gun gripped tight in her hand, a look of fiendish resolution on her face.

===Chinese===
阿尔玛还在客厅里，枪攥得死紧，脸上是一副凶狠决绝的神情。

===Original===
“You must help me to get Digby into the house,” she said. “Where is he?”

===Chinese===
「你得帮我抬迪格比进屋。」她说。「他在哪儿？」

===Original===
Mirabelle pointed, and the two women, returning to the man, half lifted, half dragged him back to the hall. Laying him down on the brick floor, the girl went in search of clean linen. The kitchen, which was also the drying place for Alma’s more intimate laundry, supplied all that she needed. Whilst Alma watched unmoved the destruction of her wardrobe, the girl bathed the wound and the frightened nurse (who had disappeared at the first shot) applied a rough dressing. The wound was an ugly one, and the man showed no signs of recovering consciousness.

===Chinese===
米拉贝尔一指，两个女人回到那人跟前，半抬半拖，把他弄回了门厅。姑娘让他躺在砖地上，转身去找干净的布巾。厨房——那里同时也是阿尔玛晾晒贴身衣物的地方——把她需要的一应都供上了。阿尔玛冷眼看着自己的衣物惨遭「征用」，不动声色；姑娘替伤者洗净创口，吓坏了的护士（头一声枪响就没影了）给他草草敷了绷带。伤势不轻，那人毫无转醒的迹象。

===Original===
“We shall have to send Mary into Gloucester for an ambulance,” said Mirabelle. “We can’t send nurse﻿—she doesn’t know the way.”

“Mary,” said Alma calmly, “is at this moment having hysterics in the larder. I’ll harness the dogcart and go myself. But where is the other man?”

Mirabelle shook her head.

“I don’t like to think what has happened to him,” she said. “Now, Alma, do you think we can get him into the drawing-room?”

===Chinese===
「只好打发玛丽去格洛斯特叫救护车了。」米拉贝尔说。「护士去不得——她不认得路。」

「玛丽么，」阿尔玛不动声色地说，「这会儿正在食品间里发歇斯底里呢。我来套双轮轻便马车，亲自去一趟。只是另一个人哪儿去了？」

米拉贝尔摇了摇头。

「他遭了什么，我不敢往下想。」她说。「好了，阿尔玛，你看咱们能把他抬进客厅去么？」

===Original===
Together they lifted the heavy figure and staggered with it into the pretty little room, laying him at last upon the settee under the window.

===Chinese===
两人合力抬起那沉重的身躯，踉踉跄跄抬进那间漂亮的小房间，末了把他安放在窗下的长沙发上。

===Original===
“He can rest there till we get the ambulance,” began Mirabelle, and a chuckle behind her made her turn with a gasp.

It was the pedlar, and in his hand he held the pistol which she had discarded.

===Chinese===
「救护车来之前，他可以先在这儿歇着。」米拉贝尔话刚起头，身后一声低笑，惊得她倒抽一口气，回过头去。

是那货郎。他手里握着的，正是她撂下的那支手枪。

===Original===
“I only want you”﻿—he nodded to the girl. “You other two women can come out here.” He jerked his head to the passage. Under the stairs was a big cupboard and he pulled the door open invitingly. “Get in here. If you make a noise, you’ll be sorry for yourselves.”

===Chinese===
「我要的只是你」——他朝姑娘点了点头。「你们另外两个女人家，出来，到这边来。」他朝过道一摆头。楼梯底下有一口大柜，他殷勤地拉开柜门。「进去。要是出声，有你们受的。」

===Original===
Alma’s eyes wandered longingly to the gun she had left in the corner, but before she could make a move he had placed himself between her and the weapon.

“Inside,” said the pedlar, and Mirabelle was not much surprised when Aunt Alma meekly obeyed.

===Chinese===
阿尔玛的眼睛恋恋地瞟向她撂在角落里的那杆枪，可她还没来得及挪步，他已抢身挡在她与枪中间。

「进去。」货郎说。阿尔玛姨妈乖乖从命，米拉贝尔倒也不怎么惊奇。

===Original===
He shut the door on the two women and fastened the hatch.

“Now, young lady, put on your hat and be lively!”

===Chinese===
他把两个女人关进柜里，扣上了柜门的闩。

「好了，年轻小姐，戴上帽子，麻利点！」

===Original===
He followed her up the stairs into her room and watched her while she found a hat and a cloak. She knew only too well that it was a waste of time even to temporize with him. He, for his part, was so exultant at his success that he grew almost loquacious.

===Chinese===
他跟着她上楼进了她的房间，看着她找出一顶帽子、一件斗篷。她心里再明白不过：跟这号人敷衍周旋，纯粹是白费光阴。至于他呢，得手之余得意忘形，话竟多起来了。

===Original===
“I suppose you saw the boys driving away and you didn’t remember that I was somewhere around? Was that you doing the shooting?”

She did not answer.

===Chinese===
「我猜你眼看着那帮小子开车跑了，就没想起来我还在左近什么地方转悠？放枪的是不是你？」

她不答话。

===Original===
“It couldn’t have been Lew, or you’d have been dead,” he said. He was examining the muzzle of the pistol. “It was you all right.” He chuckled. “Ain’t you the game one! Sister, you ought to be﻿—”

===Chinese===
「不可能是卢，要是卢，你早没命了。」他说。他正察看手枪的枪口。「千真万确是你。」他咯咯一笑。「你可真有种！大妹子，你本该——」

===Original===
He stopped dead, staring through the window. He was paralysed with amazement at the sight of a bareheaded Aunt Alma flying along the Gloucester Road. With an oath he turned to the girl.

“How did she get out? Have you got anybody here? Now speak up.”

===Chinese===
他猛地顿住，直勾勾望着窗外。只见阿尔玛姨妈光着脑袋，正顺着通往格洛斯特的大道飞奔——他惊得整个人都僵住了。他咒骂一声，转向姑娘。

「她怎么出去的？屋里还藏着人？快说。」

===Original===
“The cupboard under the stairs leads to the wine cellar,” said Mirabelle coolly, “and there are two ways out of the wine cellar. I think Aunt Alma found one of them.”

===Chinese===
「楼梯底下那口柜子通着酒窖，」米拉贝尔冷冷地说，「酒窖有两个出口。我料想阿尔玛姨妈找着了其中一个。」

===Original===
With an oath, he took a step towards her, gripped her by the arm and jerked her towards the door.

“Lively!” he said, and dragged her down the stairs through the hall, into the kitchen.

He shot back the bolts, but the lock of the kitchen door had been turned.

===Chinese===
他又咒骂一声，抢上一步，攥住她的胳膊，把她朝门口猛地一拽。

「麻利点！」他说着，拖她下楼穿过门厅，进了厨房。

他拉开了门闩，可厨房门上的锁却已经拧上了。

===Original===
“This way.” He swore cold-bloodedly, and, her arm still in his powerful grip, he hurried along the passage and pulled open the door.

===Chinese===
「打这边走。」他冷口冷面地咒了一声，那只铁钳般的手仍攥着她的胳膊，匆匆沿过道走去，拉开了宅门。

===Original===
It was an unpropitious moment. A man was walking down the path, a half-smile on his face, as though he was thinking over a remembered jest. At the sight of him the pedlar dropped the girl’s arm and his hand went like lightning to his pocket.

===Chinese===
这个时辰实在不巧。一个男人正顺着小径走来，唇边噙着半个微笑，仿佛在心里回味一句记得的俏皮话。货郎一看见他，便撒开姑娘的胳膊，那只手闪电般探向口袋。

===Original===
“When will you die?” said Leon Gonsalez softly. “Make a choice, and make it quick!”

And the gun in his hand seemed to quiver with homicidal eagerness.

===Chinese===
「你几时死？」莱昂·冈萨雷斯柔声说道。「挑一个日子吧，快些挑！」

他手里那支枪，仿佛正怀着杀人的急切，微微颤动。

## XV Two “Accidents” / 十五 两场「意外」

===Original===

The pedlar, his face twitching, put up his shaking hands.

Leon walked to him, took the Browning from his moist grip and dropped it into his pocket.

===Chinese===

小贩脸皮抽搐着，颤巍巍地举起了双手。

莱昂走上前去，从他汗津津的手里拈下那把勃朗宁手枪，随手揣进了自己口袋。

===Original===

“Your friends are waiting, of course?” he said pleasantly.

The pedlar did not answer.

“Cuccini too? I thought I had incapacitated him for a long time.”

“They’ve gone,” growled the pedlar.

===Chinese===

「你那几位朋友，自然都在外头候着吧？」他语声和悦地说。

小贩不作声。

「库奇尼也来了？我还当我已经叫他好一阵子动弹不得了呢。」

「他们走了。」小贩恶狠狠地说。

===Original===

Gonsalez looked round in perplexity.

“I don’t want to take you into the house. At the same time, I don’t want to leave you here,” he said. “I almost wish you’d drawn that gun of yours,” he added regretfully. “It would have solved so many immediate problems.”

This particular problem was solved by the return of the dishevelled Alma and the restoration to her of her gun.

===Chinese===

冈萨雷斯困惑地四下张望。

「我不想把你带进宅子去。可与此同时，我又不想把你撂在这儿。」他说。「我倒几乎希望，你方才把那杆枪拔出来过，」他不无遗憾地补了一句，「那该省掉多少眼面前的难题。」

这一桩具体的难题，终以披头散发的阿尔玛去而复返、手枪物归原主而告解决。

===Original===

“I would so much rather you shot him than I,” said Leon earnestly. “The police are very suspicious of my shootings, and they never wholly believe that they are done in self-defence.”

===Chinese===

「我宁可开枪的是您，不是我。」莱昂恳切地说。「警方对我开枪的案子疑心极重，从来不肯全信那是出于自卫。」

===Original===

With a rope he tied the man, and tied him uncomfortably, wrists to ankles. That done, he made a few inquiries and went swiftly out to the barn, returning in a few minutes with the unhappy guard.

“It can’t be helped,” said Leon, cutting short the man’s apologies. “The question is, where are the rest of the brethren?”

===Chinese===

他用一根绳子把那人捆住，而且捆得极不舒服——手腕拴着脚踝。捆完，他略略盘问了几句，便快步出门到谷仓那边去，几分钟后带着那个倒霉的看守回来了。

「没法子的事。」莱昂截住那人连声的道歉。「问题是，其余那几位弟兄都在哪儿？」

===Original===

Something zipped past him: it had the intensified hum of an angry wasp, and a second later he heard a muffled “Plop!” In a second he was lying flat on the ground, his Browning covering the hedge that hid Heavytree Lane.

“Run to the house,” he called urgently. “They won’t bother about you.” And the guard, nothing loth, sprinted for the cover of walls.

===Chinese===

有什么东西嗖地从他身边掠过：那声息像一只狂怒的黄蜂把嗡鸣拔到了极处；一秒钟后，他又听见一声闷闷的「噗！」转瞬间他已伏倒在地，勃朗宁手枪对准了遮着赫维特里巷的那道树篱。

「往宅子里跑。」他急声喊道。「他们不会来纠缠你。」那看守正求之不得，撒腿朝围墙的掩蔽奔去。

===Original===

Presently Leon located the enemy, and at a little distance off he saw the flat top of the covered trolley. A man walked slowly and invitingly across the gap in the hedge, but Gonsalez held his fire, and presently the manoeuvre was repeated. Obviously they were trying to concentrate his mind upon the gap whilst they were moving elsewhere. His eyes swept the meadow boundary﻿—running parallel, he guessed, was a brook or ditch which would make excellent cover.

===Chinese===

不多时，莱昂便摸清了敌人的位置；隔着不远，他望见那辆带篷板车的平顶。一个男人慢吞吞地、招人上钩似的从树篱的豁口前走过，冈萨雷斯却按枪不发；不多时，这套把戏又演了一遍。明摆着，他们是要把他的心思拴在豁口上，人手却在别处挪动。他的目光扫过草场的边界——他猜，与边界平行处有一条小溪或沟渠，正是上好的掩蔽。

===Original===

Again the man passed leisurely across the gap. Leon steadied his elbow, and glanced along the sight. As he did so, the man reappeared.

*Crack!*

===Chinese===

那人又从从容容地从豁口前踱过。莱昂稳住臂肘，顺着准星望去。就在这时，那人再度现身。

「砰！」

===Original===

Gonsalez aimed a foot behind him. The man saw the flash and jumped back, as he had expected. In another second he was writhing on the ground with a bullet through his leg.

===Chinese===

冈萨雷斯瞄准他身后一英尺的地方开了一枪。那人望见枪火，向后跳开——正如他所料。再一秒钟，他已腿上中弹，在地上打起滚来。

===Original===

Leon showed his teeth in a smile and switched his body round to face the new point of attack. It came from the spot that he had expected: a little rise of ground that commanded his position.

===Chinese===

莱昂龇牙一笑，霍地拧转身子，面向新的攻击点。攻击果然来自他料定的那处——一座小土坡，居高临下，正扼着他所在的位置。

===Original===

The first bullet struck the turf to his right with an angry buzz, sent a divot flying heavenward, and ricochetted with a smack against a tree. Before the raised head could drop to cover, Gonsalez fired; fired another shot to left and right, then, rising, raced for the shelter of the tree, and reached it in time to see three heads bobbing back to the road. He waited, covering the gap, but the people who drew the wounded man out of sight did not show themselves, and a minute later he saw the trolley moving swiftly down the byroad, and knew that danger was past.

===Chinese===

头一颗子弹带着怒蜂似的嗡声打在他右侧的草皮上，掀得一块草皮飞上了天，又「啪」地一声弹到一棵树上。不等那颗探起的脑袋缩回掩体，冈萨雷斯便开了枪；又朝左右各补一发，随即起身，飞奔去投那棵大树的庇护，赶到时正看见三颗脑袋一路起伏着缩回大路上去。他守在那里，枪口罩住豁口，可那些把伤者拖出视线的人始终没有露面；一分钟后，他望见那辆带篷板车顺着岔路疾驶而去，知道危险已经过去。

===Original===

The firing had attracted attention. He had not been back in the house a few minutes before a mounted policeman, his horse in a lather, came galloping up to the gate and dismounted. A neighbouring farm had heard the shots and telephoned to constabulary headquarters. For half an hour the mounted policeman took notes, and by this time half the farmers in the neighbourhood, their guns under their arms, had assembled in Mirabelle’s parlour.

===Chinese===

枪声惊动了四邻。他回到宅子里没有几分钟，一名骑警已经马汗淋漓地疾驰到大门前，翻身下马。邻近一家农场听见了枪声，打电话报了警察总部。那骑警足足记了半个钟头的笔录；到这时，左近半数的农户都已把猎枪夹在腋下，聚拢到米拉贝尔的客厅里来了。

===Original===

She had not seen as much of the redoubtable Leon as she could have wished, and when they had a few moments to themselves she seized the opportunity to tell him of the call which Lee had made that morning. Apparently he knew all about it, for he expressed no surprise, and was only embarrassed when she showed a personal interest in himself and his friends.

It was not a very usual experience for him, and he was rather annoyed with himself at this unexpected glimpse of enthusiasm and hero-worship, sane as it was, and based, as he realized, upon her keen sense of justice.

===Chinese===

那位威名赫赫的莱昂，她没能如愿多见上几面；待两人得着片刻独处，她便抓住机会，把李先生当天上午来访的事讲给他听。他对这一切显然了如指掌，因为他毫不见讶异；唯独当她对他本人和他的朋友们流露出私人的关切时，他才显出几分窘态。

这在他绝非惯常的经历；这份不期而至的热忱与英雄崇拜，叫他对自己颇为着恼——虽说那热忱自有分寸，而且他明白，根基是她那敏锐的正义感。

===Original===

“I’m not so sure that we’ve been very admirable really,” he said. “But the difficulty is to produce at the moment a judgment which would be given from a distance of years. We have sacrificed everything which to most men would make life worth living, in our desire to see the scales held fairly.”

“You are not married, Mr. Gonsalez?”

===Chinese===

「说实在的，我不敢肯定我们就真有那么可敬。」他说。「难就难在，要在此刻下一个须隔着许多年才下得了的判语。为了看着天平端得平正，我们牺牲了一切——对多数人而言，那本是教人生值得过活的东西。」

「您没有成家吧，冈萨雷斯先生？」

===Original===

He stared into the frank eyes. “Married? Why, no,” he said, and she laughed.

“You talk as though that were a possibility that had never occurred to you.”

===Chinese===

他直直望进那双坦率的眼睛。「成家？这个嘛，没有。」他说。她笑了。

「您说话的口气，倒仿佛这种可能性从没在您脑子里闪过似的。」

===Original===

“It hasn’t,” he admitted. “By the very nature of our work we are debarred from that experience. And is it an offensive thing to say that I have never felt my singleness to be a deprivation?”

“It is very rude,” she said severely, and Leon was laughing to himself all the way back to town as at a great joke that improved upon repetition.

===Chinese===

「确实没有闪过。」他承认道。「凭我们这份营生的本性，我们就与那种际遇无缘。还有——倘若我说，我从不曾觉得独身是一种缺憾，这话算不得冒犯吧？」

「冒犯极了。」她板起脸说。莱昂一路回城，独自笑个不住，只当想着一个越品越有味的绝妙笑话。

===Original===

“I think we can safely leave her for a week,” he reported, on his return to Curzon Street. “No, nothing happened. I was held up in a police trap near Newbury for exceeding the speed limit. They said I was doing fifty, but I should imagine it was nearer eighty. Meadows will get me out of that. Otherwise, I must send the inevitable letter to the magistrate and pay the inevitable fine. Have you done anything about Johnson Lee?”

===Chinese===

「我想，把她独自撂下一个星期，也尽可放心。」回到柯曾街，他这样汇报。「不，没出什么事。我在纽伯里（Newbury）附近一头撞进警方设的埋伏，罪名是超速。他们说我开到了五十英里，可依我想，怕是接近八十英里。米多斯会替我把这事了结。不然的话，我就得给治安法官照例去一封信、照例缴一笔罚金。约翰逊·李那边，你们可有举措？」

===Original===

Manfred nodded. “Meadows and the enthusiastic Mr. Washington have gone round to see him. I have asked Washington to go because”﻿—he hesitated﻿—“the snake is a real danger, so far as he is concerned. Elijah Washington promises to be a very real help. He is afraid of nothing, and has undertaken to stay with Lee and to apply such remedies for snakebites as he knows.”

===Chinese===

曼弗雷德点了点头。「米多斯和那位热心十足的华盛顿先生已经过去看他了。我之所以请华盛顿同去，是因为」——他迟疑了一下——「就他而论，『毒蛇』是一桩实实在在的危险。伊莱贾·华盛顿看来会是一大助力。他什么都不怕，而且已经应承留下陪李先生，把他所知的蛇伤救治法子尽数施为。」

===Original===

He was putting on his gloves as he spoke, and Leon Gonsalez looked at him with a critical admiration.

“Are you being presented at Court, or are you taking tea with a duchess?”

===Chinese===

他说这话时正戴着手套；莱昂·冈萨雷斯望着他，目光里带着品评式的钦佩。

「您这是要进宫觐见呢，还是要同哪位公爵夫人共进茶点？」

===Original===

“Neither. I’m calling upon friend Oberzohn.”

“The devil you are!” said Leon, his eyebrows rising.

===Chinese===

「都不是。我去拜访朋友欧伯佐恩。」

「你竟要去！」莱昂说，两道眉毛扬了起来。

===Original===

“I have taken the precaution of sending him a note, asking him to keep his snakes locked up,” said Manfred, “and as I have pointedly forwarded the carbon copy of the letter, to impress the fact that another exists and may be brought in evidence against him, I think I shall leave Oberzohn & Smitts’ main office without hurt. If you are not too tired, Leon, I would rather prefer the Buick to the Spanz.”

===Chinese===

「我已未雨绸缪，先给他递了张短简，请他把他的蛇都锁好。」曼弗雷德说，「而且我特意把那封信的复写副本转送了一份，好教他明白另有一份存在、日后可充作指证他的凭据——我想，我定能毫发无伤地走出欧伯佐恩-斯米茨商行的总办公室。你要是不太累，莱昂，我宁可坐别克，也不坐那辆『斯潘兹』。」

===Original===

“Give me a quarter of an hour,” said Leon, and went up to his room to make himself tidy.

===Chinese===

「给我一刻钟。」莱昂说罢，上楼回房把自己收拾齐整。

===Original===

It was fifteen minutes exactly when the Buick stopped at the door, and Manfred got into the saloon. There was no partition between driver and passenger, and conversation was possible.

“It would have been as well if you’d had Brother Newton there,” he suggested.

===Chinese===

一刻钟分毫不差，别克在门口停住，曼弗雷德坐进车厢。司机与乘客之间没有隔板，说话不成问题。

「方才要是牛顿弟兄也在场，事情倒更圆满。」他提了一句。

===Original===

“Brother Newton will be on the spot: I took the precaution of sending him a similar note,” said Manfred. “I shouldn’t imagine they’ll bring out their gunmen.”

===Chinese===

「牛顿弟兄自会到场：我未雨绸缪，给他也送去了一封同样的短简。」曼弗雷德说。「料想他们还不至于把那帮枪手搬出来。」

===Original===

“I know two, and possibly three, they won’t bring out.” Gonsalez grinned at the traffic policeman who waved him into Oxford Street. “That Browning of mine throws high, Manfred: I’ve always had a suspicion it did. Pistols are queer things, but this may wear into my hand.” He talked arms and ammunition until the square block of Oberzohn & Smitts came into sight. “Good hunting!” he said, as he got out, opened the saloon door and touched his hat to Manfred as he alighted.

===Chinese===

「有两个，说不定三个，他们是搬不出来了。」冈萨雷斯朝那位挥手放他拐进牛津街（Oxford Street）的交通警察咧嘴一笑。「曼弗雷德，我那把勃朗宁打出去偏高：我一直疑心它有这毛病。手枪是种古怪的玩意儿，不过它也许会渐渐磨合得合手。」他一路大谈枪械弹药，直到欧伯佐恩-斯米茨商行那方方正正的一座大楼进入视野。「祝猎运亨通！」他说着，跳下车拉开厢门，待曼弗雷德下车，又抬手碰了碰帽檐。

===Original===

He got back into his seat, swung the little car round in a circle, and sat on the opposite side of the road, his eyes alternately on the entrance and on the mirror which gave him a view of the traffic approaching him from the rear.

===Chinese===

他重新坐回驾驶座，让小车兜了个圈，停到马路对面，两眼轮番盯住大门入口和那面映出后方来车的后视镜。

===Original===

Manfred was not kept in the waiting-room for more than two minutes. At the end of that time, a solemn youth in spectacles, with a little bow, led him across the incurious office into the presence of the illustrious doctor.

===Chinese===

曼弗雷德在接待室里没有多坐两分钟。时间一到，一个神情严肃的戴眼镜青年微微躬身，领着他穿过那间谁也不抬头看一眼的办公大厅，来到大名鼎鼎的博士面前。

===Original===

The old man was at his desk. Behind him, his debonair self, Monty Newton, a large yellow flower in his buttonhole, a smile on his face. Oberzohn got up like a man standing to attention.

===Chinese===

老头儿坐在书案后面；他身后站着的是那副潇洒做派的蒙蒂·牛顿，扣眼里簪一朵大黄花，脸上挂着笑。欧伯佐恩站起身来，像应声立正的军人。

===Original===

“Mr. Manfred, this is a great honour,” he said, and held out his hand stiffly.

An additional chair had been placed for the visitor: a rich-looking tapestried chair, to which the doctor waved the hand which Manfred did not take.

===Chinese===

「曼弗雷德先生，这是莫大的荣幸。」他说着，僵硬地伸出手来。

为来客添置了一把椅子：一把看上去很华贵的织锦椅子，博士朝它挥了挥手——挥的正是曼弗雷德没有去握的那只手。

===Original===

“Good morning, Manfred.” Newton removed his cigar and nodded genially. “Were you at the dance last night?”

“I was there, but I didn’t come in,” said Manfred, seating himself. “You did not turn up till late, they tell me?”

===Chinese===

「早安，曼弗雷德。」牛顿取下衔着的雪茄，和气地点头。「昨晚的舞会，你在场吧？」

「到场了，只是没进去。」曼弗雷德说着落座。「听说你到得很晚？」

===Original===

“It was of all occurrences the most unfortunate,” said Dr. Oberzohn, and Newton laughed.

“I’ve lost his laboratory secretary and he hasn’t forgiven me,” he said almost jovially. “The girl he took on yesterday. Rather a stunner in the way of looks. She didn’t wish to go back to the country where she came from, so my sister offered to put her up for the night in Chester Square. I’m blessed if she didn’t lose herself at the dance, and we haven’t seen her since!”

===Chinese===

「诸般变故之中，这要数最不巧的一桩。」欧伯佐恩博士说。牛顿笑了。

「我把他实验室的秘书弄丢了，他到如今还不肯饶我。」他说得几乎有几分快活。「就是他昨天雇下的那个姑娘。论模样，真是个出挑的人物。她不愿回她乡下的老家，我妹妹便做主留她在切斯特广场过夜。你说怪不怪，她竟在舞会上走丢了，打那以后我们就再没见着她的人影！」

===Original===

“It was a terrible thing,” said Oberzohn sadly. “I regard her as in my charge. For her safety I am responsible. You, I trust, Mr. Newton﻿—”

“I don’t think I should have another uneasy moment if I were you, doctor,” said Manfred easily. “The young lady is back at Heavytree Farm. I thought that would surprise you. And she is still there: that will surprise you more, if you have not already heard by telephone that your Old Guard failed dismally to﻿—er﻿—bring her back to work. I presume that was their object?”

===Chinese===

「这是一件可怕的事。」欧伯佐恩悲切切地说。「我视她为我照管之下的人。她的安全，由我负责。您，我信得过，牛顿先生——」

「我要是您，大夫，往后就再不必有片刻提心吊胆了。」曼弗雷德轻描淡写地说。「那位年轻小姐已经回赫维特里农场了。我想这消息准叫您吃一惊。而且她如今还在那儿：这更要叫您吃惊了——除非您已从电话里听说，您的『老近卫军』一败涂地，没能——呃——把她接回来上工。我料想，这就是他们的差事吧？」

===Original===

“My old guard, Mr. Manfred?” Oberzohn shook his head in bewilderment. “This is beyond my comprehension.”

“Is your sister well?” asked Manfred blandly.

===Chinese===

「我的老近卫军，曼弗雷德先生？」欧伯佐恩莫名其妙地摇头。「这实在超出了我的理解。」

「令妹安好？」曼弗雷德淡淡地问。

===Original===

Newton shrugged his shoulders.

“She is naturally upset. And who wouldn’t be? Joan is a very tenderhearted girl.”

“She has been that way for years,” said Manfred offensively. “May I smoke?”

===Chinese===

牛顿耸了耸肩。

「她自然心绪不宁。换了谁能不这样？琼是个心肠极软的姑娘。」

「她这副软心肠已经软了好些年了。」曼弗雷德存心冒犯地说。「我可以抽烟吗？」

===Original===

“Will you have one of my cigarettes?” Manfred’s grave eyes fixed the doctor in a stare that held the older man against his will.

“I have had just one too many of your cigarettes,” he said. His words came like a cold wind. “I do not want any more, Herr Doktor, or there will be vacancies in your family circle. Who knows that, long before you compound your wonderful elixir, you may be called to normal immortality?”

===Chinese===

「您可愿取用一支我的香烟？」欧伯佐恩博士问。曼弗雷德那双凝重的眼睛直勾勾盯住博士，那盯视不由分说，把老人钉在了原地。

「您敬的香烟，我恰恰已经多领教过一支。」他说。他的话字字像一阵冷风。「我不要再要了，博士先生，否则您那个家族圈子里就要空出位子来了。谁知道呢——没等您调配成那奇妙的长生灵药，您说不定先蒙了召唤，去享凡人寻常的那种不朽了？」

===Original===

The yellow face of Oberzohn had turned to a dull red.

“You seem to know so much about me, Mr. Manfred, as myself,” he said in a husky whisper.

===Chinese===

欧伯佐恩那张蜡黄的脸已经涨成了暗红色。

「您似乎知道关于我的那么多事，曼弗雷德先生，正如我自己知道的那样多。」他声音沙哑地低语道。

===Original===

Manfred nodded.

“More. For whilst you are racing against time to avoid the end of a life which does not seem especially worthy of preservation, and whilst you know not what day or hour that end may come, I can tell you to the minute.” The finger of his gloved hand pointed the threat.

===Chinese===

曼弗雷德点了点头。

「比您自己知道得还多。因为，当您正与时间赛跑，想逃过一条性命的完结——那条命瞧上去并不特别值得保全——当您还不知道那完结会在哪一天、哪个时辰降临，我却能对您报得分秒不差。」他戴着手套的手伸出一根手指，把这威胁点了出来。

===Original===

All trace of a smile had vanished from Monty Newton’s face. His eyes did not leave the caller’s.

“Perhaps you shall tell me.” Oberzohn found a difficulty in speaking. Rage possessed him, and only his iron will choked down the flames from view.

===Chinese===

蒙蒂·牛顿脸上笑意的痕迹已荡然无存。他的眼睛一瞬不瞬地盯着来客。

「也许您肯把它告诉我。」欧伯佐恩觉得开口都艰难。狂怒攫住了他，全凭他那铁一般的意志，才把满腔火焰生生扼住，未让它现于形色。

===Original===

“The day that injury comes to Mirabelle Leicester, that day you go out﻿—you and those who are with you!”

===Chinese===

「米拉贝尔·莱斯特受到伤害的那一天，就是你完蛋的那一天——你，还有同你在一起的那些人！」

===Original===

“Look here, Manfred, there’s a law in this country﻿—” began Monty Newton hotly.

“I am the law.” The words rang like a knell of fate. “In this matter I am judge, jury, hangman. Old or young, I will not spare,” he said evenly.

===Chinese===

「我说，曼弗雷德，这个国家是有法律的——」蒙蒂·牛顿怒气冲冲地开口。

「我就是法律。」这句话响如命运的丧钟。「在这件事上，我是法官，是陪审团，也是行刑人。老的少的，我一概不留情。」他语气平缓地说。

===Original===

“Are you immortal too?” sneered Monty.

Only for a second did Manfred’s eyes leave the old man’s face.

===Chinese===

「难道你也是不死之身？」蒙蒂讥诮道。

曼弗雷德的目光只离开老头儿的脸一秒钟。

===Original===

“The law is immortal,” he said. “If you dream that, by some cleverly concerted coup, you can sweep me from your path before I grow dangerous, be sure that your sweep is clean.”

===Chinese===

「法律是不死的。」他说。「倘若你梦想凭一场精心串通的突袭，趁我尚未变得危险之前把我从你路上扫除，那就务必扫个干净彻底。」

===Original===

“You haven’t asked me to come here to listen to this stuff, have you?” asked Newton, and though his words were bold, his manner aggressive, there were shadows on his face which were not there when Manfred had come into the room﻿—shadows under his eyes and in his cheeks where plumpness had been.

===Chinese===

「你们请我到这儿来，总不会就是为了听这套话吧？」牛顿问道。他的话虽说得硬，态度也咄咄逼人，脸上却已现出阴影——曼弗雷德进屋时还不曾有的阴影：眼下的阴影，还有两颊上的阴影，那两颊原先是有过丰腴的。

===Original===

“I’ve come here to tell you to let up on Miss Leicester. You’re after something that you cannot get, and nobody is in a position to give you. I don’t know what it is﻿—I will make you a present of that piece of information. But it’s big﻿—bigger than any prize you’ve ever gone after in your wicked lives. And to get that, you’re prepared to sacrifice innocent lives with the recklessness of spendthrifts who think there is no bottom to their purse. The end is near!”

He rose slowly and stood by the table, towering over the stiff-backed doctor.

===Chinese===

「我到这里来，是要告诉你别再纠缠莱斯特小姐。你们追逐的东西，你们弄不到手，也没有任何人拿得出来交给你们。那东西究竟是什么，我不知道——这条情报，我白送给你。可它非同小可——比你们罪恶一生里追逐过的哪一件猎物都大。而为了得到它，你们不惜搭上无辜的性命，挥霍起来全无顾忌，活像那些自以为钱袋没有底的败家子。末日近了！」

他缓缓起身，在桌旁站定，在那腰板僵直的博士面前巍然高耸。

===Original===

“I cannot say what action the police will take over this providential snakebite, Oberzohn, but I’ll make you this offer: I and my friends will stand out of the game and leave Meadows to get you in his own way. You think that means you’ll go scot-free? But it doesn’t. These police are like bulldogs: once they’ve got a grip of you, they’ll never let go.”

===Chinese===

「这场天意使然的蛇咬案，警方会有什么举动，我说不上来，欧伯佐恩；不过我愿向你开个价：我同我的朋友们退出这局棋，让米多斯按他自己的路数来拿你。你以为那就意味着你可以逍遥法外？并不。这些警察像斗牛犬：一旦咬住你，就死也不撒口。」

===Original===

“What is the price you ask for this interesting service?” Newton was puffing steadily at his cigar, his hands clasped behind him, his feet apart, a picture of comfort and well-being.

===Chinese===

「这么有趣的一项效劳，你要讨什么价？」牛顿稳稳地一口口喷着雪茄，双手扣在背后，两脚分立，好一幅富态安逸的图画。

===Original===

“Leave Miss Leicester alone. Find a new way of getting the money you need so badly.”

Newton laughed.

===Chinese===

「别再碰莱斯特小姐。另想一条路子，去弄你们急缺的那笔钱。」

牛顿笑了。

===Original===

“My dear fellow, that’s a stupid thing to say. Neither Oberzohn nor I are exactly poor.”

===Chinese===

「我亲爱的伙计，这话可说得太蠢了。欧伯佐恩也好，我也好，哪一个都算不得穷。」

===Original===

“You’re bankrupt, both of you,” said Manfred quietly. “You are in the position of gamblers when the cards have run against you for a long time. You have no reserve, and your expenses are enormous. Find another way, Newton﻿—and tell your sister”﻿—he paused by the door, looking down into the white lining of his silk hat﻿—“I’d like to see her at Curzon Street tomorrow morning at ten o’clock.”

===Chinese===

「你们两个全都破产了。」曼弗雷德不动声色地说。「你们的处境，正像手气一连背了很久的赌徒：没有一点后备，开销却又大得骇人。另谋出路吧，牛顿——还有，告诉令妹」——他在门边停了停，低头望着丝质礼帽的白色衬里——「明天上午十点，我想在柯曾街见她。」

===Original===

“Is that an order?” asked Newton sarcastically.

Manfred nodded.

===Chinese===

「这是命令么？」牛顿揶揄地问。

曼弗雷德点了点头。

===Original===

“Then let me tell you,” roared the man, white with passion, “that I take no orders for her or for me. Got swollen heads since you’ve had your pardon, haven’t you? You look out for me, Manfred. I’m not exactly harmless.”

He felt the pressure of the doctor’s foot upon his and curbed his temper.

===Chinese===

「那你听好了，」这人咆哮起来，脸都气白了，「不论为她还是为我自己，我谁的命令也不受。得了特赦，脑袋就发起胀来了，是不是？你给我小心着点，曼弗雷德。我可绝非善类。」

他觉出博士的脚在桌下抵住自己的脚，便按捺住了火气。

===Original===

“All right,” he growled, “but don’t expect to see Joan.”

He added a coarse jest, and Manfred raised his eyes slowly and met his.

“You will be hanged by the State or murdered by Oberzohn﻿—I am not sure which,” he said simply, and he spoke with such perfect confidence that the heart of Monty Newton turned to water.

===Chinese===

「行啊。」他咕哝道，「不过别指望见着琼。」

他又添了一句粗鄙的浑话，曼弗雷德缓缓抬起眼来，迎住了他的目光。

「你迟早不是被国家绞死，就是被欧伯佐恩谋杀——哪一样在先，我说不准。」他语气平淡地说；那话说得笃定无比，蒙蒂·牛顿的心霎时化作了一汪凉水。

===Original===

Manfred stood in the sidewalk and signalled, and the little car came swiftly and noiselessly across. Leon’s eyes were on the entrance. A tall man standing in the shadow of the hall was watching. He was leaning against the wall in a negligent attitude, and for a second Leon was startled.

===Chinese===

曼弗雷德站到人行道上打了个信号，小车迅捷无声地驶了过来。莱昂的眼睛盯住大门：一个高个子男人正立在门厅的暗影里守望。他倚着墙，姿态闲散，莱昂一时间竟吃了一惊。

===Original===

“Get in quickly!”

Leon almost shouted the words back, and Manfred jumped into the machine, as the chauffeur sent the car forward, with a jerk that strained every gear.

===Chinese===

「快上车！」

莱昂这话几乎是回头吼出来的；司机猛地把车朝前一送，曼弗雷德跃进车厢，车身一挫，每个齿轮都吃紧了。

===Original===

“What on﻿—?” began Manfred, but the rest of his words were lost in the terrific crash which followed.

The leather hood of the machine was ripped down at the back, a splinter of glass struck Leon’s cap and sliced a half-moon neatly. He jammed on the brakes, threw open the door of the saloon and leaped out. Behind the car was a mass of wreckage; a great iron casting lay split into three pieces amidst a tangle of broken packing-case. Leon looked up; immediately above the entrance to Oberzohn & Smitts’ was a crane, which had swung out with a heavy load just before Manfred came out. The steel wire hung loosely from the derrick. He heard excited voices speaking from the open doorway three floors above, and two men in large glasses were looking down and gabbling in a language he did not understand.

===Chinese===

「这是搞什——？」曼弗雷德刚一开口，后半句便淹没在紧接着那声惊天动地的巨响里了。

车子的皮顶篷后部被砸得绽裂下来，一片玻璃碴打在莱昂的帽子上，齐齐削去一块月牙。他一脚踩死刹车，推开厢门，纵身跳了出去。车后是一大片残骸；一大件铸铁裂成三块，横在一堆散了架的破板条箱中间。莱昂抬头望去：欧伯佐恩-斯米茨商行大门的正上方装着一台吊车，就在曼弗雷德出来之前，它吊着重物荡了出去。钢索从吊架上松松垂下。他听见三层楼上一个敞开的门口里传出激动的人声，两个戴大眼镜的男人正往下张望，叽里咕噜说着一种他听不懂的语言。

===Original===

“A very pretty accident. We might have filled half a column in the evening newspapers if we had not moved.”

“And the gentleman in the hall﻿—what was he doing?”

===Chinese===

「好一场漂亮的『意外』。方才我们若不是挪动得快，晚上的报纸怕要登掉半栏了。」

「那门厅里的那位先生——他在做什么？」

===Original===

Leon walked back through the entrance: the man had disappeared, but near where he had been standing was a small bell-push which, it was obvious, had recently been fixed, for the wires ran loosely on the surface of the wall and were new.

===Chinese===

莱昂从大门折了回去：那人已经不见，但在他方才立身之处的近旁有一只小小的电铃按钮，一望便知是新近装上的——电线松松地布在墙面上，还是崭新的。

===Original===

He came back in time to see a policeman crossing the road.

“I wish to find out how this accident occurred, constable,” he said. “My master was nearly killed.”

===Chinese===

他走回来，正赶上一名警察穿过马路。

「我想弄清楚这场意外是怎么发生的，警官。」他说。「我的主人差点儿丢了性命。」

===Original===

The policeman looked at the ton of debris lying half on the sidewalk, half on the road, then up at the slackened hawser.

“The cable has run off the drum, I should think.”

===Chinese===

警察看了看那足足一吨重的残骸——半边塌在人行道上，半边压在马路上——又抬头望了望松垂下来的钢缆。

「依我看，是钢缆从卷筒上滑脱了。」

===Original===

“I should think so,” said Leon gravely.

He did not wait for the policeman to finish his investigations, but went home at a steady pace, and made no reference to the “accident” until he had put away his car and had returned to Curzon Street.

===Chinese===

「我也这么想。」莱昂一本正经地说。

他没有等警察查完，便稳稳当当地驱车回家，直到把车收好、人回到柯曾街，对那场「意外」只字未提。

===Original===

“The man in the hall was put there to signal when you were under the load﻿—certain things must not happen,” he said. “I am going out to make a few inquiries.”

===Chinese===

「门厅里那个人，是安插在那里发信号的，就在你走到重物底下的一刻——有些事，是万万不容发生的。」他说。「我这就出去打探几件事。」

===Original===

Gonsalez knew one of Oberzohn’s staff: a clean young Swede, with that knowledge of English which is normal in Scandinavian countries; and at nine o’clock that night he drifted into a Swedish restaurant in Dean Street and found the young man at the end of his meal. It was an acquaintance﻿—one of many﻿—that Leon had assiduously cultivated. The young man, who knew him as Mr. Heinz﻿—Leon spoke German remarkably well﻿—was glad to have a companion with whom he could discuss the inexplicable accident of the afternoon.

===Chinese===

冈萨雷斯认得欧伯佐恩商行里的一名职员：一个整洁清爽的瑞典青年，英语说得不坏，正是斯堪的纳维亚诸国寻常可见的那种程度。当晚九点，他信步踱进迪安街（Dean Street）一家瑞典餐馆，正赶上那青年用完晚餐。这段相识是莱昂刻意经营的许多相识中的一段；那青年只知道他叫海因茨先生（Heinz）——莱昂的德语说得极好——正巴不得有个伴儿，好把下午那场莫名其妙的事故议论一番。

===Original===

“The cable was not fixed to the drum,” he said. “It might have been terrible: there was a gentleman in a motorcar outside, and he had only moved away a few inches when the case fell. There is bad luck in that house. I am glad that I am leaving at the end of the week.”

===Chinese===

「那根钢缆压根儿没有固定在卷筒上。」他说。「差点儿就要出大祸了：外头有位先生坐在汽车里，货箱砸下来的时候，他刚挪开不过几英寸。那座楼里有股邪气。我在这个周末就离职了，真是走得好。」

===Original===

Leon had some important questions to put, but he did not hurry, having the gift of patience to a marked degree. It was nearly ten when they parted, and Gonsalez went back to his garage, where he spent a quarter of an hour.

===Chinese===

莱昂有几桩要紧的问题要问，但他不慌不忙——论耐心，他天赋异禀。两人分手时已近十点，冈萨雷斯回到自己的车库，在里面待了一刻钟。

===Original===

At midnight, Manfred had just finished a long conversation with the Scotland Yard man who was still at Brightlingsea, when Leon came in, looking very pleased with himself. Poiccart had gone to bed, and Manfred had switched out one circuit of lights when his friend arrived.

===Chinese===

午夜时分，曼弗雷德刚同还留在布莱特灵西的那位苏格兰场的人通完一席长谈，莱昂进来了，一脸的自得之色。波伊卡特已经上床睡了；朋友进来时，曼弗雷德刚熄掉一路电灯。

===Original===

“Thank you, my dear George,” said Gonsalez briskly. “It was very good of you, and I did not like troubling you, but﻿—”

“It was a small thing,” said Manfred with a smile, “and involved merely the changing of my shoes. But why? I am not curious, but why did you wish me to telephone the night watchman at Oberzohn’s to be waiting at the door at eleven o’clock for a message from the doctor?”

===Chinese===

「多谢了，我亲爱的乔治。」冈萨雷斯干脆利落地说。「难为你了，我本不愿劳动你，可是——」

「小事一桩，」曼弗雷德含笑说，「不过费了换双鞋的工夫。可是为什么呢？我不好奇，可你为什么要我打电话给欧伯佐恩那边的守夜人，叫他十一点钟在门口等候博士的口信？」

===Original===

“Because,” said Leon cheerfully, rubbing his hands, “the night watchman is an honest man; he has a wife and six children, and I was particularly wishful not to hurt anybody. The building doesn’t matter: it stands, or stood, isolated from all others. The only worry in my mind was the night watchman. He was at the door﻿—I saw him.”

===Chinese===

「因为呀，」莱昂喜滋滋地搓着手说，「那守夜人是个老实人；他有妻子，还有六个孩子，而我特别不愿意伤到任何人。那座楼无所谓：它孤零零一座，同旁的楼不相挨——如今该说，从前不相挨。我心里唯一的挂虑就是那守夜人。他当时就在门口——我亲眼看见的。」

===Original===

Manfred asked no further questions. Early the next morning he took up the paper and turned to the middle page, read the account of the “Big Fire in City Road” which had completely gutted the premises of Messrs. Oberzohn & Smitts; and, what is more, he expected to read it before he had seen the paper.

“Accidents are accidents,” said Leon the philosopher that morning at breakfast. “And that talk I had with the clerk last night told me a lot: Oberzohn has allowed his fire insurance to lapse!”

===Chinese===

曼弗雷德没有再问。第二天一早，他拿起报纸翻到中页，读到「城市路大火」的报道——大火把欧伯佐恩-斯米茨商行的整座行址烧了个精光；而且，不瞒你说，还没见着报纸之前，他就已经料定会读到这条新闻了。

「意外终归是意外。」那天早晨吃早饭时，哲学家莱昂说道。「昨晚同那职员的一番谈话告诉了我不少东西：欧伯佐恩听凭他的火险失效了！」

## XVI Rath Hall / 十六 拉思府
===Original===
In one of the forbidden rooms that was filled with the apparatus which Dr. Oberzohn had accumulated for his pleasure and benefit, was a small electrical furnace which was the centre of many of his most interesting experiments. There were, in certain known drugs, constituents which it was his desire to eliminate. Dr. Oberzohn believed absolutely in many things that the modern chemist would dismiss as fantastical.

===Chinese===
在一间禁室里——屋里堆满了欧伯佐恩博士为自家乐趣与利益攒聚起来的各色器械——立着一只小小的电炉，他许多最有趣的实验，都以它为中心。在某些已知的药物之中，存在着一些他一心要清除的成分。现代化学家斥为荒诞无稽的许多东西，欧伯佐恩博士却深信不疑。

===Original===
He believed in the philosopher’s stone, in the transmutation of base metals to rare; he had made diamonds, of no great commercial value, it is true; but his supreme faith was that somewhere in the materia medica was an infallible elixir which would prolong life far beyond the normal span. It was to all other known properties as radium is to pitchblende. It was something that only the metaphysician could discover, only the patient chemist could materialize. Every hour he could spare he devoted himself to his obsession; and he was in the midst of one of his experiments when the telephone bell called him back to his study. He listened, every muscle of his face moving, to the tale of disaster that Monty Newton wailed. “It is burning still? Have you no fire-extinguishing machinery in London?”

===Chinese===
他信点金石，信贱金属能炼成贵金属；他造出过钻石——不假，没有多大的商业价值；可他至高的信仰是：药物学（materia medica）的某一处，藏着一服万无一失的仙丹，能把寿命延展到远逾常限。它之于一切其他已知的药性，恰如镭之于沥青铀矿。这样的东西，唯有玄学家能够发现，唯有那有耐心的化学家能将它化成实物。凡是挤得出的时辰，他尽数奉献给这份痴迷；他正埋头做着一场实验，电话铃把他唤回了书房。他听蒙蒂·牛顿哭腔哭调地数说那场灾祸，脸上的肌肉一块块都在抽动。「还在烧？你们伦敦就没有灭火的机器吗？」

===Original===
“Is the place insured or is it not?” asked Monty for the second time.

===Chinese===
「那地方到底保了险没有？」蒙蒂第二遍问道。

===Original===
Dr. Oberzohn considered. “It is not,” he said. “But this matter is of such small importance compared with the great thing which is coming, that I shall not give it a thought.”

===Chinese===
欧伯佐恩博士沉吟了一下。「没有。」他说。「不过比起即将临头的那桩大事，此事区区不足道，我连想也不去想它。」

===Original===
“It was incendiary,” said Newton angrily. “The fire brigade people are certain of it. That cursed crowd are getting back on us for what happened this afternoon.”

===Chinese===
「那是纵火。」牛顿怒冲冲地说。「消防队的人拿得准。是那帮该死的家伙为今天下午的事对咱们还以颜色。」

===Original===
“I know of nothing that happened this afternoon,” said Dr. Oberzohn coldly. “You know of nothing either. It was an accident which we all deplored. As to this man… we shall see.”

===Chinese===
「今天下午出过什么事，我一无所知。」欧伯佐恩博士冷冷地说。「你也一无所知。那是一场事故，我们人人都痛惜。至于这个人……咱们走着瞧。」

===Original===
He hung up the telephone receiver very carefully, went along the passage, down a steep flight of dark stairs, and into a basement kitchen. Before he opened the door he heard the sound of furious voices, and he stood for a moment surveying the scene with every feeling of satisfaction. Except for two men, the room was empty. The servants used the actual kitchen at the front of the house, and this place was little better than a scullery. On one side of the deal table stood Gurther, white as death, his round eyes red with rage. On the other, the short, stout Russian Pole, with his heavy pasty face and baggy eyes; his little moustache and beard bristling with anger. The cards scattered on the table and the floor told the Herr Doktor that this was a repetition of the quarrel which was so frequent between them.

===Chinese===
他小心翼翼地挂上听筒，沿过道走去，下了一段陡峭的黑楼梯，进了一间地下厨房。还没推门，他就听见了狂怒的吵嚷声；他站着打量了片刻，心头怀着十二分的满意。屋里除了两个男人，空无一人。仆人们用的是宅子前部那间正厨房，这地方比洗碗间强不了多少。松木餐桌的一边站着格瑟，面白如死，一双圆眼睛气得通红。另一边是那个矮胖的俄籍波兰人，肥厚的白脸，浮肿的眼泡，小胡子和颏须根根倒竖。桌上地上散落的纸牌告诉这位博士先生：两人之间那场屡见不鲜的口角，又重演了一回。

===Original===
“*Schweinhund!*” hissed Gurther. “I saw you palm the King as you dealt. Thief and robber of the blind—”

===Chinese===
「*Schweinhund*（猪猡）！」格瑟嘶声骂道。「我看见你发牌时把老K扣在掌心里了。贼，连瞎子都要抢的贼——」

===Original===
“You German dog! You—”

===Chinese===
「你这德国狗！你这——」

===Original===
They were both speaking in German. Then the doctor saw the hand of Gurther steal down and back.

===Chinese===
两人都用德语对骂着。这时，博士看见格瑟那只手悄悄往下、往后摸去。

===Original===
“Gurther!” he called, and the man spun round. “To my parlour—march!”

===Chinese===
「格瑟！」他喝了一声，那人霍地转过身来。「到我的客厅来——开步走！」

===Original===
Without a word, the man strode past him, and the doctor was left with the panting Russian.

===Chinese===
那人一言不发，大步从他身边走了过去；博士同那个直喘粗气的俄国人留在原地。

===Original===
“Herr Doktor, this Gurther is beyond endurance!” His voice trembled with rage. “I would sooner live with a pig than this man, who is never normal unless he is drugged.”

===Chinese===
「博士先生，这个格瑟叫人忍无可忍！」他的嗓音气得直颤。「我宁可同一头猪住在一处，也不同这个人住——他除非吃了药，就没有一刻是正常的。」

===Original===
“Silence!” shouted Oberzohn, and pointed to the chair. “You shall wait till I come,” he said.

===Chinese===
「住口！」欧伯佐恩厉声喝道，抬手指着那把椅子。「你在这儿等着，等我回来。」他说。

===Original===
When he came back to his room, he found Gurther standing stiffly to attention.

===Chinese===
他回到自己房里，只见格瑟直挺挺地立正候着。

===Original===
“Now, Gurther,” he said—he was almost benevolent as he patted the man on the shoulder—“this matter of Gonsalez must end. Can I have my Gurther hiding like a worm in the ground? No, that cannot be. Tonight I will send you to this man, and you are so clever that you cannot fail. He whipped you, Gurther—tied you up and cruelly beat you. Always remember that, my brave fellow—he beat you till you bled. Now you shall see the man again. You will go in a dress for-every-occasion,” he said. “The city-clerk manner. You will watch him in your so clever way, and you shall strike—it is permitted.”

===Chinese===
「听好，格瑟。」他说着拍了拍那人的肩膀，几乎算得上和颜悦色。「冈萨雷斯这桩事必须了结。我的格瑟，岂能像条虫子似的藏在地底下？不，断乎不能。今夜我就派你去会这个人；你如此机灵，断无失手之理。他用鞭子抽过你，格瑟——把你捆起来，狠狠地打。永远记住这个，我的勇士——他打得你鲜血淋漓。如今你就要再见这个人了。你要穿一身场场合宜的行头去，」他说。「城里小职员的做派。你要用你那顶顶聪明的法子盯住他，然后你就出击——这是准许的。」

===Original===
“*Ja*, Herr Doktor.”

===Chinese===
「*Ja*, Herr Doktor.」

===Original===
He turned on his heels and disappeared through the door. The doctor waited till he heard him going up the stairs, and then he rang for Pfeiffer. The man came in sullenly. He lacked all the precision of the military Gurther; yet, as Oberzohn knew, of the two he was the more alert, the more cunning.

===Chinese===
他脚跟一转，出门去了。博士等到听见他上楼的动静，这才按铃叫法伊弗。那人绷着脸进来了。他全无军人格瑟那股一丝不苟的劲头；然而欧伯佐恩知道，两人之中，倒是这一个更警觉、更狡黠。

===Original===
“Pfeiffer, it has come to me that you are in some danger. The police wish to take you back to Warsaw, where certain unpleasant things happened, as you well know. And I am told”—he lowered his voice—“that a friend of ours would be glad to see you go, *hein*?”

===Chinese===
「法伊弗，我风闻你正身处几分危险。警察想把你押回华沙——那里出过几桩不愉快的事，你清楚得很。我还听说，」——他压低了嗓音——「咱们的一位朋友，巴不得见你走，*hein*？」

===Original===
The man did not raise his sulky eyes from the floor, did not answer, or by any gesture or movement of body suggest that he had heard what the older man had said.

===Chinese===
那人阴沉的眼睛没有从地板上抬起来，没有作声，也没有用任何手势或身体的动静，表示他听见了年长者说的这番话。

===Original===
“Gurther goes tomorrow, perhaps on our good work, perhaps to speak secretly to his friends in the police—who knows? He has work to do: let him do it, Pfeiffer. All my men will be there—at a place called Brightlingsea. You also shall go. Gurther would rob a blind man? Good! You shall rob one also. As for Gurther, I do not wish him back. I am tired of him: he is a madman. All men are mad who sniff that white snuff up their foolish noses—eh, Pfeiffer?”

===Chinese===
「格瑟明天动身，或许是去办咱们的好事，或许是去同他在警界的朋友们密谈——谁知道呢？他有活儿要干：就让他干去，法伊弗。我的人马都会到场——在一个叫布莱特灵西的地方。你也去。格瑟要抢一个瞎子？好！你也去抢一个。至于格瑟，我不愿他回来了。我腻烦他了：他是个疯子。凡是把那种白鼻粉往愚蠢的鼻孔里嗅的人，个个是疯子——呃，法伊弗？」

===Original===
Still the awkward-looking man made no reply.

===Chinese===
那个长相笨拙的人仍旧一声不吭。

===Original===
“Let him do his work: you shall not interfere, until—it is done.”

===Chinese===
「让他干他的活儿：你不得干预，直到——活儿干完。」

===Original===
Pfeiffer was looking at him now, a cold sneer on his face.

===Chinese===
法伊弗此刻正眼望着他，脸上挂着一丝冷笑。

===Original===
“If he comes back, I do not,” he said. “This man is frightening me. Twice the police have been here—three times… you remember the woman. The man is a danger, Herr Doktor. I told you he was the day you brought him here.”

===Chinese===
「他要是回来了，我就不去。」他说。「这个人叫我害怕。警察来过这儿两回——三回……你还记得那个女人的事。这个人是个祸胎，博士先生。你把他带到这儿来的那天，我就这么告诉过你。」

===Original===
“He can dress in the gentleman-club manner,” said the doctor gently.

===Chinese===
「他倒穿得出一身绅士俱乐部的做派。」博士温声道。

===Original===
“Pshaw!” said the other scornfully. “Is he not an actor who has postured and painted his face and thrown about his legs for so many marks a week?”

===Chinese===
「呸！」对方满脸不屑。「他不就是个戏子么——每礼拜挣那么几个马克，描着脸摆姿势、抡腿子的货色？」

===Original===
“If he does not come back I shall be relieved,” murmured the doctor. “Though it would be a mistake to leave him so that these cunning men could pry into our affairs.”

===Chinese===
「他若回不来，我倒如释重负。」博士低声道。「只是留着他性命、任那帮精明人从他嘴里掏出咱们的勾当，那就失着了。」

===Original===
Pfeiffer said nothing: he understood his instructions; there was nothing to be said. “When does he go?”

===Chinese===
法伊弗没说什么：吩咐他已了然于心；没有什么可说的。「他几时动身？」

===Original===
“Early tomorrow, before daylight. You will see him, of course.”

===Chinese===
「明日一早，天亮之前。你自然会见到他。」

===Original===
He said something in a low tone, that only Pfeiffer heard. The shadow who stood in stockinged feet listening at the door only heard two words. Gurther grinned in the darkness; his bright eyes grew luminous. He heard his companion move towards the door and sped up the stairs without a sound.

===Chinese===
他又低声说了几句，只有法伊弗听得见。那个只穿着袜子、贴在门外偷听的影子，只听清了两个词。格瑟在黑暗里咧嘴一笑；一双亮眼睛灼灼放光。他听见他那位同伴朝门口走来，便悄没声儿地一溜烟上了楼。

---
===Original===
Rath Hall was a rambling white building of two stories, set in the midst of a little park, so thickly wooded that the house was invisible from the road; and since the main entrance to the estate was a very commonplace gate, without lodge or visible drive beyond, Gonsalez would have missed the place had he not recognized the man who was sitting on the moss-grown and broken wall who jumped down as Leon stopped his car.

===Chinese===
拉思府是一座格局铺展的两层白色建筑，坐落在一片小庄园正中，林木密得使宅子从大路上全然望不见；而庄园的正门又只是一扇再寻常不过的大门，既没有门房，门内也望不见车道——若不是认出了坐在那道青苔斑驳的破墙头上的人（莱昂一刹车，那人便跳了下来），冈萨雷斯险些要与这地方错身而过。

===Original===
“Mr. Meadows is at the house, sir. He said he expected you.”

“And where on earth is the house?” asked Leon Gonsalez, as he went into reverse.

===Chinese===
「米多斯先生在宅子里，先生。他说过等您来。」

「可这宅子究竟在哪儿？」莱昂·冈萨雷斯问道，一面挂上倒挡。

===Original===
For answer the detective opened the gate wide and Leon sent his car winding between the trees, for close at hand he recognized where a gravel drive had once been, and, moreover, saw the tracks of cars in the soft earth. He arrived just as Mr. Johnson Lee was taking his two guests in to dinner, and Meadows was obviously glad to see him. He excused himself and took Leon aside into the hall, where they could not be overheard.

===Chinese===
那侦探算是作答，把大门推得敞开；莱昂驱车在树木之间蜿蜒而行——近在眼前，他已认出砾石车道旧日所在的走势，况且松软的泥地上还印着一道道车辙。他抵达时，约翰逊·李先生正引着两位客人入席用晚餐；米多斯见了他，分明是高兴的。米多斯向主人告了便，把莱昂拉到一边进了门厅——在那里说话，不怕叫人听了去。

===Original===
“I have had your message,” he said. “The only thing that has happened out of the ordinary is that the servants have an invitation to a big concert at Brightlingsea. You expected that?”

===Chinese===
「你的口信我收到了。」他说。「唯一出格的事，是仆人们收到了请柬，去布莱特灵西听一场大型音乐会。这个你料到了？」

===Original===
Leon nodded.

“Yes: I hope Lee will let them go. I prefer that they should be out of the way. A crude scheme—but Oberzohn does these things. Has anything else happened?”

===Chinese===
莱昂点了点头。

「料到了：但愿李放他们去。我宁可他们离得远远的。法子是糙了些——可欧伯佐恩专干这种事。还有别的动静吗？」

===Original===
“Nothing. There have been one or two queer people around.”

===Chinese===
「没有。左近转悠过一两个形迹古怪的人。」

===Original===
“Has he showed you the letters he had from Barberton?”

To his surprise the inspector answered in the affirmative.

===Chinese===
「他给你看过巴伯顿写给他的那些信了吗？」

出乎他意料，探长竟给了肯定的回答。

===Original===
“Yes, but they are worse than Greek to me. A series of tiny protuberances on thick brown paper. He keeps them in his safe. He read some of the letters to me: they were not very illuminating.”

===Chinese===
「看过了，可它们于我比希腊文还难懂。厚厚的棕色纸上，一串小小的凸点。他把它们全收在保险柜里。他念了几封给我听：听不出多少名堂。」

===Original===
“But the letter of letters?” asked Leon anxiously. “That which Lee answered—by the way, you know that Mr. Lee wrote all his letters between perforated lines?”

“I’ve seen the paper,” nodded the detective. “No, I asked him about that, but apparently he is not anxious to talk until he has seen his lawyer, who is coming down tonight. He should have been here, in fact, in time for dinner.”

===Chinese===
「可那封信中之信呢？」莱昂急切地问。「李作复的那封——对了，你可知道，李先生所有的信都是循着针孔线写的？」

「那种纸我见过。」探长点头道。「没有，这件事我问过他，可他分明不愿多谈，非要先见他的律师不可——律师今夜就下乡来。说起来，他本该赶得上门来吃晚饭的。」

===Original===
They passed into the dining-room together. The blind man was waiting patiently at the head of the table, and with an apology Leon took the place that had been reserved for him. He sat with his back to the wall, facing one of the three long windows that looked out upon the park. It was a warm night and the blinds were up, as also was the middle window that faced him. He made a motion to Mr. Washington, who sat opposite him, to draw a little aside, and the American realized that he wished an uninterrupted view of the park.

===Chinese===
两人一道进了餐室。盲人已耐心候在桌首；莱昂道了声歉，在给他留出的座上落了座。他背墙而坐，正对着朝园子开的三扇长窗中的一扇。夜气温暖，窗帘都卷了上去，正对着他的那扇中窗也敞开着。他朝对座的华盛顿先生做了个手势，示意他稍稍挪开些；那美国人随即明白：他要的是一望无碍地看住园子。

===Original===
“Would you like the window closed?” asked Mr. Lee, leaning forward and addressing the table in general. “I know it is open,” he said with a little laugh, “because I opened it! I am a lover of fresh air.”

===Chinese===
「这窗要不要关上？」李先生俯身向前，问满桌的人。「我知道它开着，」他含着一点笑意说，「是我开的！我爱新鲜空气。」

===Original===
They murmured their agreement and the meal went on without any extraordinary incident. Mr. Washington was one of those adaptable people who dovetail into any environment in which they find themselves. He was as much at home at Rath Hall as though he had been born and bred in the neighbourhood. Moreover, he had a special reason for jubilation: he had found a rare adder when walking in the woods that morning, and spent ten minutes explaining in what respect it differed from every other English adder.

===Chinese===
众人低声应和，这一餐便照常吃了下去，并无别样的变故。华盛顿先生是那种随遇而安的人，落到什么环境里都能严丝合缝地嵌进去。他在拉思府自在得很，仿佛就在这左近生养长大。何况他还有一桩特别的喜事：那天早晨在林间散步，他寻着了一条稀罕的蝰蛇，足足花了十分钟讲解，它同英国一切别的蝰蛇如何不同。

===Original===
“Is it dead?” asked Meadows nervously.

===Chinese===
「它死了没有？」米多斯提心吊胆地问。

===Original===
“Kill it?” said the indignant Mr. Washington. “Why should I kill it? I saw a whole lot of doves out on the lawn this morning—should I kill ’em? No, sir! I’ve got none of those mean feelings towards snakes. I guess the Lord sent snakes into this world for some other purpose than to be chased and killed every time they’re seen. I sent him up to London today by train to a friend of mine at the Zoological Gardens. He’ll keep him until I’m ready to take him back home.”

===Chinese===
「弄死它？」华盛顿先生愤愤然道。「我干吗弄死它？今天早晨我还瞧见草坪上落了满满一群鸽子——难道我该把它们都打死？不，先生！我对蛇可没有那种小家子气的恶感。我琢磨着，上帝把蛇放进这个世界，本另有别的用场，不是叫人一瞧见就追着打的。我今天已经把它抬上火车送到伦敦，交给动物园里我的一位朋友。他会替我养着，等我预备带它回家。」

===Original===
Meadows drew a long sigh.

“As long as he’s not in your pocket,” he said.

===Chinese===
米多斯长长吁了一口气。

「只要它不在你口袋里就好。」他说。

===Original===
“Do you mind?”

Leon’s voice was urgent as he signalled Washington to move yet farther to the left, and when the big man moved his chair, Leon nodded his thanks. His eyes were on the window and the darkening lawn. Not once did he remove his gaze.

===Chinese===
「劳驾，再挪一挪？」

莱昂的口气急切起来，一面示意华盛顿再往左边挪；等大个子挪开了椅子，莱昂点头致谢。他两眼盯着那扇窗和渐渐沉入暮色的草坪，目光一刻也不曾挪开。

===Original===
“It’s an extraordinary thing about Poole, my lawyer,” Mr. Lee was saying. “He promised faithfully he’d be at Rath by seven o’clock. What is the time?”

Meadows looked at his watch.

“Half-past eight,” he said. He saw the cloud that came over the face of the blind owner of Rath Hall.

===Chinese===
「说到我的律师普尔（Poole），事情有些蹊跷。」李先生正说着。「他信誓旦旦，答应七点钟准到拉思府。现在几点了？」

米多斯看了看表。

「八点半。」他说。他看见一片阴云掠过拉思府盲主人的脸。

===Original===
“It is extraordinary! I wonder if you would mind—”

His foot touched a bell beneath the table and his butler came in.

“Will you telephone to Mr. Poole’s house and ask if he has left?”

The butler returned in a short time.

“Yes, sir, Mr. Poole left the house by car at half-past six.”

===Chinese===
「真是蹊跷！不知可否有劳——」

他的脚尖碰了碰桌下的一枚唤人铃，管家走了进来。

「请打个电话到普尔先生府上，问他动身了没有？」

管家不多时便回来了。

「是，先生，普尔先生六点半坐汽车离开了家。」

===Original===
Johnson Lee sat back in his chair.

“Half-past six? He should have been here by now.”

“How far away does he live?”

“About fifteen miles. I thought he might have come down from London rather late. That is extraordinary.”

===Chinese===
约翰逊·李靠回椅背里。

「六点半？这会儿早该到了。」

「他住得多远？」

「十五英里上下。我原想着，他也许从伦敦动身得晚些。这事真是蹊跷。」

===Original===
“He may have had tyre trouble,” said Leon, not shifting his fixed stare.

“He could have telephoned.”

===Chinese===
「也许车胎出了毛病。」莱昂说，目不转睛地盯着。

「可他满可以打个电话来。」

===Original===
“Did anyone know he was coming—anybody outside your own household?” asked Gonsalez.

The blind man hesitated.

“Yes, I mentioned the fact to the post office this morning. I went in to get my letters, and found that one I had written to Mr. Poole had been returned through a stupid mistake on my part. I told the postmaster that he was coming this evening and that there was no need to forward it.”

===Chinese===
「可有人知道他要来——府上以外，可有人知道？」冈萨雷斯问。

盲人迟疑了一下。

「有。今天早晨我在邮局提过这事。我进去取信，发现我写给普尔先生的一封，因我一时糊涂出的错被退了回来。我告诉邮政局长，说他今晚要来，那封信不必再转寄了。」

===Original===
“You were in the public part of the post office?”

“I believe I was.”

===Chinese===
「你当时在邮局对外营业的那半边？」

「我想是在。」

===Original===
“You said nothing else, Mr. Lee—nothing that would give any idea of the object of this visit?”

Again his host hesitated. “I don’t know. I’m almost afraid that I did,” he confessed. “I remember telling the postmaster that I was going to talk to Mr. Poole about poor Barberton—Mr. Barberton was very well known in this neighbourhood.”

===Chinese===
「你没说别的吧，李先生——没说什么、足以叫人猜到此行来意的话吧？」

主人又迟疑了。「说不准。我怕多半是说了。」他招认道。「我记得同邮政局长说过，我要同普尔先生谈谈可怜的巴伯顿——巴伯顿先生在这一带是鼎鼎有名的。」

===Original===
“That is extremely unfortunate,” said Leon.

===Chinese===
「那就大大不妙了。」莱昂说。

===Original===
He was thinking of two things at the same time: the whereabouts of the missing lawyer, and the wonderful cover that the wall between the window and the floor gave to any man who might creep along out of sight until he got back suddenly to send the snake on its errand of death.

===Chinese===
他心里同时想着两件事：失踪律师的下落；还有窗子与地面之间那道墙所能提供的绝好遮蔽——任什么人都能贴着它潜行，遁出视线，再猝然杀回，放蛇去干那夺命的差事。

===Original===
“How many men have you got in the grounds, by the way, Meadows?”

===Chinese===
「对了，园子里你布置了几个人，米多斯？」

===Original===
“One, and he’s not in the grounds but outside on the road. I pull him in at night, or rather in the evening, to patrol the grounds, and he is armed.” He said this with a certain importance. An armed English policeman is a tremendous phenomenon, that few have seen.

===Chinese===
「一个，还不在园子里，在外头大路上。到夜里——不如说到傍晚——我把他收进园子巡逻，他还带着枪。」他说这话时，颇有点郑重其事。带枪的英国警察是桩了不得的奇观，见过的没有几人。

===Original===
“Which means that he has a revolver that he hasn’t fired except at target practice,” said Leon. “Excuse me—I thought I heard a car.”

===Chinese===
「也就是说，他有支左轮枪，除了打靶就没放过一响。」莱昂说。「失陪——我好像听见汽车声了。」

===Original===
He got up noiselessly from the table, went round the back of Mr. Lee, and, darting to the window, looked out. A flowerbed ran close to the wall, and beyond that was a broad gravel drive. Between gravel and flowers was a wide strip of turf. The drive continued some fifty feet to the right before it turned under an arch of rambler roses. To the left it extended for less than a dozen feet, and from this point a path parallel to the side of the house ran into the drive.

===Chinese===
他悄没声儿地从桌边起身，绕到李先生背后，一个箭步抢到窗前，朝外望去。一溜花床贴着墙根，花床外是一道宽阔的砾石车道；砾石与花床之间，隔着一条宽宽的草皮。车道向右再伸展五十来英尺，才在一架攀缘蔷薇的拱门下拐了弯；向左延出去不满十二英尺，从那里起，一条与宅子侧面平行的小径汇入车道。

===Original===
“Do you hear it?” asked Lee.

“No, sir, I was mistaken.”

===Chinese===
「你听见了吗？」李问。

「没有，先生，是我听岔了。」

===Original===
Leon dipped his hand into his side pocket, took out a handful of something that looked like tiny candies wrapped in coloured paper. Only Meadows saw him scatter them left and right, and he was too discreet to ask why. Leon saw the inquiring lift of his eyebrows as he came back to his seat, but was wilfully dense. Thereafter, he ate his dinner with only an occasional glance towards the window.

===Chinese===
莱昂把手伸进侧袋，掏出一把什么东西，看去像一粒粒彩纸裹着的小糖果。只有米多斯看见他左一把右一把撒了出去；他为人知趣，没有问缘故。莱昂回到座上，瞧见他眉毛探询地一挑，却只装糊涂。此后他吃他的饭，只偶尔朝窗子瞥上一眼。

===Original===
“I’m not relying entirely upon my own lawyer’s advice,” said Mr. Lee. “I have telegraphed to Lisbon to ask Dr. Pinto Caillao to come to England, and he may be of greater service even than Poole, though where—” The butler came in at this moment.

===Chinese===
「我不单指望自己律师的主意。」李先生说。「我已致电里斯本，请平托·卡亚奥博士（Pinto Caillao）来英国，他说不定比普尔更有用处，只是他眼下在哪里——」管家恰在此时走了进来。

===Original===
“Mrs. Poole has just telephoned, sir. Her husband has had a bad accident: his car ran into a tree trunk which was lying across the road near Lawley. It was on the other side of the bend, and he did not see it until too late.”

===Chinese===
「普尔太太刚来了电话，先生。她丈夫出了事故：他的车撞上一根横在大路上的树干，就在劳利（Lawley）附近。树干在弯道那一头，他瞧见时已经来不及了。」

===Original===
“Is he very badly hurt?”

“No, sir, but he is in the Cottage Hospital. Mrs. Poole says he is fit to travel home.”

===Chinese===
「伤得重吗？」

「不重，先生，只是他眼下住在乡村医院（Cottage Hospital）里。普尔太太说，他经得起路，可以回家。」

===Original===
The blind man sat open-mouthed.

“What a terrible thing to have happened!” he began.

===Chinese===
盲人张着嘴坐在那里。

「怎么竟出了这样的事！」他开口道。

===Original===
“A very lucky thing for Mr. Poole,” said Leon cheerfully. “I feared worse than that—”

===Chinese===
「对普尔先生来说，这可是天大的运气。」莱昂轻快地说。「我还怕比这更糟——」

===Original===
From somewhere outside the window came a “snap!”—the sound that a Christmas cracker makes when it is exploded. Leon got up from the table, walked swiftly to the side of the window and jumped out. As he struck the earth, he trod on one of the little bonbons he had scattered and it cracked viciously under his foot.

===Chinese===
窗外不知哪里传来「啪」的一声脆响——正是圣诞拉炮炸开时的那种声音。莱昂从桌边起身，疾步抢到窗侧，纵身跳了出去。落地时一脚踩在他撒下的那些小拉炮糖中的一粒上，那东西在他脚底下恶狠狠地炸了一响。

===Original===
There was nobody in sight. He ran swiftly along the grass-plot, slowing his pace as he came to the end of the wall, and then jerked round, gun extended stiffly. Still nobody. Before him was a close-growing box hedge, in which had been cut an opening. He heard the crack of a signal behind him, guessed that it was Meadows, and presently the detective joined him. Leon put his fingers to his lips, leapt the path to the grass on the other side, and dodged behind a tree until he could see straight through the opening in the box hedge. Beyond was a rose-garden, a mass of pink and red and golden blooms.

===Chinese===
四下里不见人影。他沿着草地疾奔，将到墙的尽头时放缓脚步，随即霍地转身，手臂僵直地平端着枪。还是没有人。面前是一排密植的黄杨树篱，篱上开着一道缺口。他听见身后一声信号似的脆响，料到是米多斯，不多时探长便赶了上来。莱昂把手指抵在唇上，跃过小径落到那边的草地上，闪身避到一棵树后，直到能从树篱的缺口正中望穿过去。缺口那边是一座玫瑰园，粉的、红的、金黄的花朵开成烂漫一片。

===Original===
Leon put his hand in his pocket and took out a black cylinder, fitting it, without taking his eyes from the hedge opening, to the muzzle of his pistol. Meadows heard the dull thud of the explosion before he saw the pistol go up. There was a scatter of leaves and twigs and the sound of hurrying feet. Leon dashed through the opening in time to see a man plunge into a plantation.

===Chinese===
莱昂把手伸进口袋，掏出一个黑色的圆筒，眼睛不离树篱缺口，把它装上了手枪的枪口。米多斯还没看见手枪抬起，先听见了那一声闷响。枝叶碎屑簌簌四散，跟着是仓皇奔逃的脚步声。莱昂冲过缺口，恰见一条人影扎进了树丛。

===Original===
“*Plop!*”

The bullet struck a tree not a foot from the fugitive.

===Chinese===
*噗！*

子弹打在离那逃亡者不到一英尺的一棵树上。

===Original===
“That’s that!” said Leon, and took off his silencer. “I hope none of the servants heard it, and most of all that Lee, whose hearing is unfortunately most acute, mistook the shot for something else.”

===Chinese===
「这就了账了！」莱昂说着，卸下消音器。「但愿仆人们谁也没听见；最要紧是但愿李——他的耳朵不幸尖得出奇——把这声枪响错当成了别的动静。」

===Original===
He went back to the window, stopping to pick up such of his crackers as had not exploded.

===Chinese===
他走回窗边，一路俯身拾起那些没有炸响的拉炮。

===Original===
“They are useful things to put on the floor of your room when you’re expecting to have your throat cut in the middle of the night,” he said pleasantly. “They cost exactly two dollars a hundred, and they’ve saved my life more often than I can count. Have you ever waited in the dark to have your throat cut?” he asked. “It happened to me three times, and I will admit that it is not an experience that I am anxious to repeat. Once in Bohemia, in the city of Prague; once in New Orleans, and once in Ortona.”

===Chinese===
「料到半夜里要被人割喉咙的时候，把它们撒在自己房间的地板上，是顶管用的东西。」他怡然说道。「两块钱整整一百个，救过我性命的回数，我已数不过来。你可曾在黑暗里干等着人来割喉咙？」他问。「这样的遭遇我碰上过三回，我承认，那滋味我不急于重温。一回在波希米亚，在布拉格城里；一回在新奥尔良；还有一回在奥托纳。」

===Original===
“What happened to the assassins?” asked Meadows with a shiver.

“That is a question for the theologian, if you will forgive the well-worn jest,” said Leon. “I think they are in hell, but then I’m prejudiced.”

===Chinese===
「那几个刺客落了个什么下场？」米多斯打了个寒噤，问。

「这个问题该去请教神学家——恕我借用这句用滥了的玩笑。」莱昂说。「依我想，他们都在地狱里，不过我有偏见。」

===Original===
Mr. Lee had left the dining-table and was standing at the front door, leaning on his stick; and with him an interested Mr. Washington.

“What was the trouble?” asked the old man in a worried voice. “It is a great handicap not being able to see things. But I thought I heard a shot fired.”

===Chinese===
李先生已离了餐桌，拄着手杖立在前门口；陪着他的，是满脸关切的华盛顿先生。

「出了什么事？」老人忧心忡忡地问。「眼前看不见东西，真是天大的不便。不过我好像听见放枪了。」

===Original===
“Two,” said Leon promptly. “I hoped you hadn’t heard them. I don’t know who the man was, Mr. Lee, but he certainly had no right in the grounds, and I scared him off.”

===Chinese===
「两声。」莱昂应声道。「我还指望您没听见呢。那人来历我不知道，李先生，不过他确实没有权利待在园子里，我把他吓跑了。」

===Original===
“You must have used a silencer: I did not hear the shots fully. Did you catch a view of the man’s face?”

“No, I saw his back,” he said. Leon thought it was unnecessary to add that a man’s back was as familiar to him as his face. For when he studied his enemies, his study was a very thorough and complete one. Moreover, Gurther ran with a peculiar swing of his shoulder.

===Chinese===
「你准是装了消音器：那两枪我没听真切。那人的脸你可瞧见了？」

「没有，我瞧见的是他的背。」他说。莱昂觉得没有必要补充一句：一个人的背，在他眼里同那张脸一样熟悉。他研究起仇敌来，研究得透彻完备、不留一手。何况，格瑟奔跑时，肩膀的摆动自有一种独一份的姿势。

===Original===
He turned suddenly to the master of Rath Hall. “May I speak with you privately for a few minutes, Mr. Lee?” he asked. He had taken a sudden resolution.

===Chinese===
他突然转向拉思府的主人。「我能同您单独谈几分钟吗，李先生？」他问。他已陡然拿定了一个主意。

===Original===
“Certainly,” said the other courteously, and tapped his way into the hall and into his private study.

===Chinese===
「当然可以。」对方谦和有礼地说，用杖点着路走进门厅，进了他的私人书房。

===Original===
For ten minutes Leon was closeted with him. When he came out, Meadows had gone down to his man at the gate, and Washington was standing disconsolately alone. Leon took him by the arm and led him on to the lawn.

“There’s going to be real trouble here tonight,” he said, and told him the arrangement he had made with Mr. Johnson Lee. “I’ve tried to persuade him to let me see the letter which is in his safe, but he is like rock on that matter, and I’d hate to burgle the safe of a friend. Listen.”

===Chinese===
莱昂同他密谈了十分钟。出来时，米多斯已下大门去看他自己那个人了，华盛顿正怏怏地独自站着。莱昂挽起他的手臂，引他上了草坪。

「今夜这里要出真正的乱子了。」他说，并把同约翰逊·李先生定下的安排告诉了他。「我劝过他，求他让我看看那封收在保险柜里的信，可他在这件事上硬得像块岩石，而撬一位朋友的保险柜，我于心不忍。你听。」

===Original===
Elijah Washington listened and whistled.

“They stopped the lawyer coming,” Gonsalez went on, “and now they’re mortally scared if, in his absence, the old man tells us what he intended keeping for his lawyer.”

===Chinese===
伊莱贾·华盛顿侧耳听着，吹了一声呼哨。

「他们把律师半路拦下了，不叫他来。」冈萨雷斯接着说，「如今他们怕得要死，生怕老头儿趁律师不在，把原本留着只讲给律师听的话，讲给了我们。」

===Original===
“Meadows is going to London, isn’t he?”

Leon nodded slowly.

“Yes, he is going to London—by car. Did you know all the servants were going out tonight?”

===Chinese===
「米多斯要上伦敦去，是吧？」

莱昂缓缓点了点头。

「是的，他要上伦敦去——坐汽车。你可知道，今晚全体仆人都要出门？」

===Original===
Mr. Washington stared at him.

“The women, you mean?”

“The women and the men,” said Leon calmly. “There is an excellent concert at Brightlingsea tonight, and though they will be late for the first half of the performance, they will thoroughly enjoy the latter portion of the programme. The invitation is not mine, but it is one I thoroughly approve.”

===Chinese===
华盛顿先生瞪着他瞧。

「你是说，那些女仆？」

「女仆男仆，一概在内。」莱昂平静地说。「今晚布莱特灵西有一场上好的音乐会；他们虽要误了上半场，后半场的节目尽可痛痛快快听到底。请柬不是我发的，我却打心眼里赞成。」

===Original===
“But does Meadows want to go away when the fun is starting?”

Apparently Inspector Meadows was not averse from leaving at this critical moment. He was, in fact, quite happy to go.

Mr. Washington’s views on police intelligence underwent a change for the worse.

===Chinese===
「可热闹刚要开场，米多斯倒愿意走？」

看样子，米多斯探长并不反感在这紧要关头离场。事实上，他走得相当乐意。

华盛顿先生对警察智力的高见，就此又下调了一层。

===Original===
“But surely he had better stay?” said the American. “If you’re expecting an attack… they are certain to marshal the whole of their forces?”

“Absolutely certain,” said the calm Gonsalez. “Here is the car.”

The Rolls came out from the back of the house at that moment and drew up before the door.

===Chinese===
「可他留下来岂不更好？」美国人说。「你们既料到要有一场进攻……对方必定会把人马倾巢调齐了吧？」

「必定无疑。」冈萨雷斯不动声色。「车来了。」

恰在此时，那辆劳斯莱斯从宅子后面绕了出来，停在门前。

===Original===
“I don’t like leaving you,” said Meadows, as he swung himself up by the driver’s side and put his bag on the seat.

“Tell the driver to avoid Lawley like the plague,” said Leon. “There’s a tree down, unless the local authorities have removed it—which is very unlikely.”

He waited until the tail lights of the machine had disappeared into the gloom, then he went back to the hall.

===Chinese===
「把你们留在这儿，我可不大放心。」米多斯说着，从司机座那一侧跨上车，把提包搁上了座位。

「叫司机躲着劳利走，当瘟疫一样躲。」莱昂说。「那儿倒着一棵树，除非本地官府已经抬走——那可是万万靠不住的。」

他等到那辆车的尾灯没入昏暗，才回进门厅。

===Original===
“Excuse me, sir,” said the butler, struggling into his greatcoat as he spoke. “Will you be all right—there is nobody left in the house to look after Mr. Lee. I could stay—”

“It was Mr. Lee’s suggestion you should all go,” said Gonsalez briefly. “Just go outside and tell me when the lights of the charabanc come into view. I want to speak to Mr. Lee before you go.”

===Chinese===
「对不起，先生。」管家一边往身上套大衣一边说。「您不会有事吧——宅子里没有人留下来照应李先生了。我可以留下——」

「让你们全去，本是李先生的意思。」冈萨雷斯简短地说。「你只管出去，等大游览车的灯火一露头就知会我。你们动身之前，我要同李先生说句话。」

===Original===
He went into the library and shut the door behind him. The waiting butler heard the murmur of his voice and had some qualms of conscience. The tickets had come from a local agency; he had never dreamt that, with guests in the house, his employer would allow the staff to go in its entirety.

===Chinese===
他走进图书室，随手带上了门。候着的管家听见他低低的语声，良心上起了几分不安。票是一家本地代理处送来的；他做梦也不曾想到，府上既有客人在，东家竟会放全体仆役一齐出门。

===Original===
