It was not a charabanc but a big closed bus that came lumbering up the apology for a drive, and swept round to the back of the house, to the annoyance of the servants, who were gathered in the hall.

===Chinese===
隆隆驶上那条勉强算作车道的破路、又一路绕到宅子后头的，不是大游览车，而是一辆封闭式大客车，惹得聚在门厅里的仆人们好生不痛快。

===Original===
“Don’t bother, I will tell him,” said Leon. He seemed to have taken full charge of the house, an unpardonable offence in the eyes of well-regulated servants.

He disappeared through a long passage leading into the mysterious domestic regions, and returned to announce that the driver had rectified his error and was coming to the front entrance: an unnecessary explanation, since the big vehicle drew up as he was telling the company.

===Chinese===
「不劳费心，我去告诉他。」莱昂说。他俨然把整座宅子接管了过去——在规矩本分的仆人眼里，这是不可饶恕的冒犯。

他穿进一条通往神秘家务区域的长过道，不见了人影；回来时宣称，司机已纠正了差错，正把车绕到正门来——这解释纯属多余，因为他话音未落，那辆大车已经停到了门前。

===Original===
“There goes the most uneasy bunch of festive souls it has ever been my misfortune to see,” he said, as the bus, its brakes squeaking, went down the declivity towards the unimposing gate. “And yet they’ll have the time of their lives. I’ve arranged supper for them at the Beech Hotel, and although they are not aware of it, I am removing them to a place where they’d give a lot of money to be—if they hadn’t gone!”

===Chinese===
「瞧这一车愁眉苦脸的过节人——我有生以来最不幸，竟见识了这么一帮子。」他说；大客车刹车吱呀作响，顺坡而下，朝那扇不起眼的大门驶去。「可他们自会玩个痛快的。我在山毛榉旅馆（Beech Hotel）给他们备下了宵夜；而且他们自己虽不知道，我这是把他们挪往一处地方——倘若不是已经上了路，那可是他们肯掏大把的价钱、也求着要去待的地方！」

===Original===
“That leaves you and me alone,” said Mr. Washington glumly, but brightened up almost at once. “I can’t say that I mind a rough house, with or without gunplay,” he said. He looked round the dark hall a little apprehensively. “What about fastening the doors behind?” he asked.

“They’re all right,” said Leon. “It isn’t from the back that danger will come. Come out and enjoy the night air… it is a little too soon for the real trouble.”

===Chinese===
「这一走，就剩你我两个了。」华盛顿先生怏怏地说，可几乎立刻又打起了精神。「闹它一场也好，动枪也罢，我都不在乎。」他说着，略带不安地朝昏暗的门厅四下张了张。「要不要把门都闩上？」他问。

「门不碍事。」莱昂说。「危险不会打后面来。出来享受享受夜气吧……离真正的乱子，还稍稍早了点。」

===Original===
But here, for once, he was mistaken.

Elijah Washington followed him into the park, took two paces, and suddenly Leon saw him stagger. In a second he was by the man’s side, bent and peering, his glasses discarded on the grass.

===Chinese===
可在这上头，他破天荒失算了一回。

伊莱贾·华盛顿跟着他走进园子，才迈出两步，莱昂猛然看见他一个踉跄。转瞬之间，他已抢到那人身边，俯身察看——那人的眼镜，已经甩落在草地上。

===Original===
“Get me inside,” said Washington’s voice. He was leaning heavily upon his companion.

With his arm round his waist, taking half his weight, Leon pushed the man into the hall but did not close the door. Instead, as the American sat down with a thud upon a hall seat, Leon fell to the ground, and peered along the artificial skyline he had created. There was no movement, no sign of any attacker. Then and only then did he shut the door and drop the bar, and pushing the study door wide, carried the man into the room and switched on the lights.

===Chinese===
「扶我进去。」是华盛顿的声音。他整个儿的重量，沉沉压在同伴身上。

莱昂一只胳膊揽住他的腰，分了他一半的体重，把人搀进门厅，却没有关门。待美国人一屁股跌坐在门厅长椅上，莱昂便伏倒在地，顺着他自己造出的那道人工天际线望去。没有动静，不见来袭者的一丝踪影。直到那时、也只有在那时，他才关上门、落下门闩，推开书房的门，把人抱进屋里，开了灯。

===Original===
“I guess something got me then,” muttered Washington.

His right cheek was red and swollen, and Leon saw the telltale bite; saw something else. He put his hand to the cheek and examined his fingertips.

===Chinese===
「我琢磨着，方才有东西咬着了我。」华盛顿咕哝道。

他的右颊红肿起来，莱昂看见了那处泄底的咬痕；还看见了别的什么。他把手贴上那面颊，又细细检视自己的指尖。

===Original===
“Get me some whisky, will you?—about a gallon of it.”

He was obviously in great pain and sat rocking himself to and fro.

===Chinese===
「给我弄点威士忌来，行吗？——来它一加仑。」

他显然痛得厉害，坐在那儿前后摇晃着身子。

===Original===
“Gosh! This is awful!” he groaned. “Never had any snake that bit like this!”

“You’re alive, my friend, and I didn’t believe you when you said you were snake-proof.”

===Chinese===
「天哪！这可真要命！」他哼哼道。「从来没养过哪条蛇是这么个咬法的！」

「你还活着呢，我的朋友——当初你说自己蛇毒不侵，我可是不信。」

===Original===
Leon poured out a tumbler of neat whisky and held it to the American’s lips.

“Down with Prohibition!” murmured Washington, and did not take the glass from his lips until it was empty. “You can give me another dose of that—I shan’t get pickled,” he said.

===Chinese===
莱昂斟满一大杯不掺水的威士忌，送到美国人唇边。

「打倒禁酒令！」华盛顿喃喃道；杯子不离嘴唇，一直喝到见了底。「这一剂你尽管再给我来一份——我不会醉成一摊泥的。」他说。

===Original===
He put his hand up to his face and touched the tiny wound gingerly—“It is wet,” he said in surprise.

“What did it feel like?”

“Like nothing so much as a snakebite,” confessed the expert.

===Chinese===
他抬手摸向自己的脸，轻轻碰了碰那处小小的伤口——「是湿的。」他讶异地说。

「当时是什么感觉？」

「再没有比蛇咬更像的了。」这位行家招认道。

===Original===
Already his face was puffed beneath the eyes, and the skin was discoloured black and blue.

Leon crossed to the fireplace and pushed the bell, and Washington watched him in amazement.

“Say, what’s the good of ringing? The servants have gone.”

===Chinese===
他眼眶底下已经肿了起来，皮肉泛出青一块紫一块的颜色。

莱昂走到壁炉边按铃，华盛顿愕然望着他。

「喂，按铃顶什么用？仆人都走光啦。」

===Original===
There was a patter of feet in the hall, the door was flung open and George Manfred came in, and behind him the startled visitor saw Meadows and a dozen men.

“For the Lord’s sake!” he said sleepily.

===Chinese===
门厅里响起一片急促的脚步，房门被推开，乔治·曼弗雷德走了进来；他身后，这位受惊的来客又看见了米多斯和十来条汉子。

「我的老天爷！」他迷迷糊糊地说。

===Original===
“They came in the charabanc, lying on the floor,” explained Leon, “and the only excuse for bringing a charabanc here was to send the servants to that concert.”

“You got Lee away?” asked Manfred.

Leon nodded.

“He was in the car that took friend Meadows, who transferred to the charabanc somewhere out of sight of the house.”

===Chinese===
「他们是躺在大游览车的地板上来的。」莱昂解释道，「而把一辆大游览车开到这儿来的唯一由头，就是送仆人们去听那场音乐会。」

「李你打发走了？」曼弗雷德问。

莱昂点了点头。

「送米多斯朋友走的那辆车里坐着他；车到了宅子望不见的地方，米多斯便换乘了大游览车。」

===Original===
Washington had taken a small cardboard box from his pocket and was rubbing a red powder gingerly upon the two white-edged marks, groaning the while.

“This is certainly a snake that’s got the cobra skinned to death and a rattlesnake’s bite ain’t worse than a dog nip,” he said. “Mamba nothing! I know the mamba; he is pretty fatal, but not so bad as this.”

===Chinese===
华盛顿已从口袋里摸出一只小纸盒，正把一种红色药末小心翼翼地揉在两道镶着白边的牙痕上，一边揉一边哼哼。

「这条蛇，真是把眼镜蛇都盖得没影儿了；响尾蛇咬一口，也不过像叫狗轻啃了一口。」他说。「曼巴蛇算得了什么！曼巴蛇我识得；它够毒的了，可比不上这个。」

===Original===
Manfred looked across to Leon.

“Gurther?” he asked simply, and Gonsalez nodded.

“It was intended for me obviously, but, as I’ve said before, Gurther is nervous. And it didn’t help him any to be shot up.”

===Chinese===
曼弗雷德望向莱昂。

「格瑟？」他只简单地问了一声，冈萨雷斯点了点头。

「那一击明摆着是冲我来的；不过我说过，格瑟神经质。再说他挨过一枪，手上就更没准头了。」

===Original===
“Do you fellows mind not talking so loud?” He glanced at the heavy curtains that covered the windows. Behind these the shutters had been fastened, and Dr. Oberzohn was an ingenious man.

Leon took a swift survey of the visitor’s feet; they wore felt slippers.

“I don’t think I can improve upon the tactics of the admirable Miss Leicester,” he said, and went up to Mr. Lee’s bedroom, which was in the centre of the house and had a small balcony, the floor of which was formed by the top of the porch.

===Chinese===
「二位说话，能不能劳驾小点儿声？」他朝遮着窗子的厚窗帘瞥了一眼。窗帘后面，窗板都闩得严严实实——欧伯佐恩博士是个足智多谋的人。

莱昂飞快地扫了一眼来客的脚：那脚上穿着毡拖鞋。

「那位令人叹服的莱斯特小姐的战法，我想不出还有什么可改进的。」他说着，上楼进了李先生的卧室。卧室在宅子正中，带一座小阳台，阳台的底板，正是门廊的顶。

===Original===
The long French windows were open and Leon crawled out into the darkness and took observation through the pillars of the balustrade. They were in the open now, making no attempt to conceal their presence. He counted seven, until he saw the cigarette of another near the end of the drive. What were they waiting for? he wondered. None of them moved; they were not even closing on the house. And this inactivity puzzled him. They were awaiting a signal. What was it to be? Whence would it come?

===Chinese===
长长的落地窗敞着，莱昂爬出去没入黑暗，从栏杆的柱子中间观察。那伙人此刻全在明处，全不掩饰自己的行藏。他数出了七个，接着又望见车道尽头附近另一个人嘴上的烟头。他们在等什么？他纳闷。谁也不动；甚至没有向宅子围拢过来。这番按兵不动叫他摸不着头脑。他们在等一个信号。信号会是什么？又会从哪里来？

===Original===
He saw a man come stealthily across the lawn… one or two? His eyes were playing tricks. If there were two, one was Gurther. There was no mistaking him. For a second he passed out of view behind a pillar of the balcony. Leon moved his head… Gurther had fallen! He saw him stumble to his knees and tumble flat upon the ground. What did that mean?

===Chinese===
他看见一个人影蹑手蹑脚地穿过草坪……是一个，还是两个？他的眼睛在捉弄他。若是两个，其中一个必是格瑟，错不了的。有一瞬，那人影隐到阳台的一根柱子背后，不见了。莱昂侧了侧头……格瑟栽倒了！他看见他踉跄跪倒，直挺挺扑倒在地。这是什么意思？

===Original===
He was still wondering when he heard a soft scraping, and a deep-drawn breath, and tried to locate the noise. Suddenly, within a few inches of his face, a hand came up out of the darkness and gripped the lower edge of the balcony.

===Chinese===
他正纳闷着，忽然听见一阵轻轻的刮擦声，和一声深深的吸气，便循声去定位。猛然间，离他的脸不过几英寸，一只手从黑暗里探上来，扣住了阳台的下沿。

===Original===
Swiftly, noiselessly, Gonsalez wriggled back to the room, drew erect in the cover of the curtains and waited. His hand touched something; it was a long silken cord by which the curtains were drawn. Leon grinned in the darkness and made a scientific loop.

===Chinese===
冈萨雷斯迅捷无声地蹭回房里，借着窗帘的遮蔽直起身子，静候。他的手碰着一样东西：是拉窗帘用的一根长长的丝绳。莱昂在黑暗里咧嘴一笑，打了个极讲究的活圈。

===Original===
The intruder drew himself up on to the parapet, stepped quietly across, then tiptoed to the open window. He was not even suspicious, for the French windows had been open all the evening. Without a sound, he stepped into the room and was momentarily silhouetted against the starlight reflected in the window.

===Chinese===
那不速之客撑着栏墙翻身上来，悄然跨过去，踮着脚尖走向敞开的窗子。他竟连一点疑心也没有，因为这扇落地窗整晚都敞着。他悄没声儿地跨进房里，一时之间，身影正衬在窗玻璃映着的星光上。

===Original===
“Hatless,” thought Leon. That made things easier. As the man took another stealthy step, the noose dropped over his neck, jerked tight and strangled the cry in his throat. In an instant he was lying flat on the ground with a knee in his back. He struggled to rise, but Leon’s fist came down with the precision of a piston-rod, and he went suddenly quiet.

===Chinese===
「没戴帽子。」莱昂心想。这就省事了。趁那人又蹑手蹑脚挪了一步，绳圈落上了他的脖颈，倏地收紧，把他喉咙里的喊声勒了回去。转瞬之间，他已面朝下平平贴在地上，背上顶着一只膝盖。他挣扎着要起来，莱昂的拳头却带着活塞杆一般的精准砸落，他登时没了声息。

===Original===
Gonsalez loosened the slipknot, and, flinging the man over his shoulder, carried him out of the room and down the stairs. He could only guess that this would be the only intruder, but left nothing to chance, and after he had handed his prisoner to the men who were waiting in the hall, he ran back to the room, to find, as he had expected, that no other adventurer had followed the lead. They were still standing at irregular intervals where he had seen them last. The signal was to come from the house. What was it to be? he wondered.

===Chinese===
冈萨雷斯解开活结，把那人往肩头一扛，扛出房门，下了楼。他只能猜想这会是唯一闯进来的一个，却不肯有半分侥幸；把俘虏交给候在门厅里的人之后，他又跑回房中，果然不出所料，再没有第二个亡命徒跟着上来。他们仍旧零零落落立在原处，他末次见到的地方。信号要从宅子里发出。会是个什么信号呢？他纳闷着。

===Original===
He left one of his men on guard in the room and went back to the study, to find that the startled burglar was an old friend. Lew Cuccini was looking from one of his captors to the other, a picture of dumbfounded chagrin. But the most extraordinary discovery that Leon made on his return to the study was that the American snake-charmer was his old cheerful self, and, except for his unsightly appearance, seemed to be none the worse for an ordeal which would have promptly ended the lives of ninety-nine men out of a hundred. “Snake-proof—that’s me. Is this the guy that did it?” He pointed to Cuccini.

===Chinese===
他留下一个人在房里看守，自己回到书房，发现那个惊惶的夜贼竟是位老相识。卢·库奇尼正从抓住他的这人脸看到那人脸，一副目瞪口呆又懊恼万状的尊容。而莱昂回到书房最奇的发现是：那位美国弄蛇人已恢复了往日的欢快，除了那副有碍观瞻的模样，经了这场百人中要当场收走九十九条性命的磨难，竟似毫发无损。「蛇毒不侵——说的就是我。干这事的是这家伙？」他指着库奇尼。

===Original===
“Where is Gurther?” asked Manfred.

Cuccini grinned up into his face.

“You’d better find out, boss,” he said. “He’ll fix you. As soon as I shout—”

===Chinese===
「格瑟在哪儿？」曼弗雷德问。

库奇尼仰起脸，冲他龇牙一乐。

「你顶好去打听打听，头儿。」他说。「他会来收拾你们的。等我这一嗓子——」

===Original===
“Cuccini—” Leon’s voice was gentle. The point of the long-bladed knife that he held to the man’s neck was indubitably sharp. Cuccini shrank back. “You will not shout. If you do, I shall cut your throat and spoil all these beautiful carpets—that is a genuine silken Bokhara, George. I haven’t seen one in ten years.” He nodded to the soft-hued rug on which George Manfred was standing. “What is the signal, Cuccini?” turning his attention again to the prisoner. “And what happens when you give the signal?”

===Chinese===
「库奇尼——」莱昂的声音很温和。他抵在那人脖颈上的长刃刀，刀尖之锋利，毋庸置疑。库奇尼缩了回去。「你是不会喊的。你若喊，我就割了你的喉咙，糟蹋了这一屋子漂亮地毯——乔治，那条可是正宗的布哈拉（Bokhara）真丝货，我有十年没见过一条了。」他朝乔治·曼弗雷德脚下那条色泽柔润的地毯点了点头。「信号是什么，库奇尼？」他把注意力重新转回俘虏身上。「你一发信号，又要出什么事？」

===Original===
“Listen,” said Cuccini, “that throat-cutting stuff don’t mean anything to me. There’s no third degree in this country, and don’t forget it.”

===Chinese===
「听着，」库奇尼说，「割喉咙那一套，吓不着我。这个国家不兴三级审讯，你可别忘了。」

===Original===
“You have never seen my ninety-ninth degree.” Leon smiled like a delighted boy. “Put something in his mouth, will you?”

One of the men tied a woollen scarf round Cuccini’s head.

“Lay him on the sofa.”

He was already bound hand and foot and helpless.

===Chinese===
「你可没见识过我的九十九级审讯。」莱昂笑得像个欢天喜地的孩子。「谁往他嘴里塞点东西？」

一个汉子把一条羊毛围巾缠上了库奇尼的脑袋。

「把他放倒在沙发上。」

他早已被捆住手脚，动弹不得了。

===Original===
“Have you any wax matches? Yes, here are some.” Leon emptied a cut-glass container into the palm of his hand and looked blandly round at the curious company. “Now, gentlemen, if you will leave me alone for exactly five minutes, I will give Mr. Cuccini an excellent imitation of the persuasive methods of Gian Visconti, an excellent countryman of his, and the inventor of the system I am about to apply.”

===Chinese===
「你们有蜡梗火柴吗？有了，这儿就有几根。」莱昂把一只雕花玻璃缸倒进手心，和颜悦色地环视一圈那群面露好奇的同伴。「好了，诸位，请让我单独待上整五分钟，我将为库奇尼先生献上一段精彩的模仿——模仿吉安·维斯孔蒂（Gian Visconti）那套劝供的手段。那是他的一位顶出色的同乡，也是我即将施行的这套法门的发明人。」

===Original===
Cuccini was shaking his head furiously. A mumble of unintelligible sounds came from behind the scarf.

“Our friend is not unintelligent. Any of you who say that Signor Cuccini is unintelligent will incur my severest displeasure,” said Leon.

===Chinese===
库奇尼拼命摇头。围巾后头发出一串含混不清的呜呜声。

「咱们这位朋友并非没有头脑。你们当中谁要说库奇尼先生没有头脑，必招致我最严厉的不快。」莱昂说。

===Original===
They sat the man up and he talked brokenly, hesitatingly.

“Splendid,” said Leon, when he had finished. “Take him into the kitchen and give him a drink—you’ll find a tap above the kitchen sink.”

===Chinese===
众人把那人扶坐起来，他断断续续、吞吞吐吐地说了。

「好极了。」等他说完，莱昂道。「带他到厨房去，给他口水喝——厨房水槽上方有个水龙头。」

===Original===
“I’ve often wondered, Leon,” said George, when they were alone together, “whether you would ever carry out these horrific threats of yours of torture and malignant savagery?”

===Chinese===
「莱昂，我常纳闷，」等到只剩他们两个，乔治说，「你这些酷刑加恶毒兽行的吓人威胁，究竟会不会当真兑现？」

===Original===
“Half the torture of torture is anticipation,” said Leon easily, lighting a cigarette with one of the matches he had taken from the table, and carefully guiding the rest back into the glass bowl. “Any man versed in the art of suggestive description can dispense with thumbscrews and branding irons, little maidens and all the ghastly apparatus of criminal justice ever employed by our ancestors. I, too, wonder,” he mused, blowing a ring of smoke to the ceiling, “whether I could carry my threats into execution—I must try one day.” He nodded pleasantly, as though he were promising himself a great treat.

===Chinese===
「酷刑之酷，一半在预期。」莱昂从容说道。他用从桌上取的一根火柴点着香烟，又把其余的几根小心翼翼拨回玻璃缸里。「凡是精通暗示性描述这门艺术的，尽可省下拶指、烙铁、铁处女，连同我们祖先用过的刑事司法的全套狰狞刑具。我也纳闷，」他若有所思，朝天花板吐出一个烟圈，「我的威胁究竟兑现不兑现——来日务必一试。」他愉快地点头，仿佛许了自己一场大大的享受。

===Original===
Manfred looked at his watch.

“What do you intend doing—giving the signal?”

Gonsalez nodded.

“And then?”

“Letting them come in. We may take refuge in the kitchen. I think it would be wiser.”

===Chinese===
曼弗雷德看了看表。

「你打算怎么着——去发那个信号？」

冈萨雷斯点了点头。

「然后呢？」

「放他们进来。我们可以退进厨房避一避。我想这样更稳妥。」

===Original===
George Manfred nodded. “You’re going to allow them to open the safe?”

“Exactly,” said Leon. “I particularly wish that safe to be opened, and since Mr. Lee demurs, I think this is the best method. I had that in my mind all the time. Have you seen the safe, George? I have. Nobody but an expert could smash it. I have no tools. I did not provide against such a contingency, and I have scruples. Our friends have the tools—and no scruples!”

===Chinese===
乔治·曼弗雷德点了点头。「你要任他们去开那口保险柜？」

「正是。」莱昂说。「我巴不得那口柜子被打开；李先生既然不肯，我想这便是最好的法子。我自始至终打的都是这个主意。那口柜子你见过吗，乔治？我见过。除了行家，谁也砸不开它。我没有工具。这一层变故我未曾预备应对，何况我还有顾忌。咱们的朋友们有工具——却没有顾忌！」

===Original===
“And the snake—is there any danger?”

Leon snapped his fingers.

“The snake has struck for the night, and will strike no more! As for Gurther—”

“He owes you something.”

===Chinese===
「那蛇呢——可有什么危险？」

莱昂打了个响指。

「蛇今夜已出过一次击，再不会出第二次！至于格瑟——」

「他还欠着你一笔账呢。」

===Original===
Leon sent another ring up and did not speak until it broke on the ceiling.

“Gurther is dead,” he said simply. “He has been lying on the lawn in front of the house for the past ten minutes.”

===Chinese===
莱昂又送上去一个烟圈，直到它在天花板上散开，才开口。

「格瑟死了。」他简单地说。「过去这十分钟里，他一直躺在宅子前的草坪上。」

## XVII Written in Braille / 十七 盲文写就
===Original===
Leon briefly related the scene he had witnessed from the balcony.

“It was undoubtedly Gurther,” he said. “I could not mistake him. He passed out of view for a second behind one of the pillars, and when I looked round he was lying flat on the ground.”

He threw his cigarette into the fireplace.
===Chinese===
莱昂把他从阳台上亲眼目睹的那一幕简略讲了一遍。

「那毫无疑问是格瑟。」他说。「我不会认错人。他在一根柱子后头隐没了一秒钟，等我回过头再看时，他已经直挺挺地躺在地上了。」

他把香烟抛进了壁炉。

===Original===
“I think it is nearly time,” he said. He waited until Manfred had gone, and, going to the door, moved the bar and pulled it open wide.

Stooping down, he saw that the opening of the door had been observed, for one of the men was moving across the lawn in the direction of the house. From his pocket he took a small electric lamp and sent three flickering beams into the darkness. To his surprise, only two men walked forward to the house. Evidently Cuccini was expected to deal with any resistance before the raid occurred.
===Chinese===
「我看时候差不多了。」他说。他等到曼弗雷德走开，才走到门边，挪开门闩，把门整个拉开。

他俯下身去，看出开门的动静已被对方察觉——果然有一条人影正穿过草坪，朝宅子这边移动。他从口袋里掏出一只小电筒，朝黑暗里打了三下闪烁的灯光。叫他意外的是，朝宅子走过来的只有两个人。显然，这伙人指望库奇尼在动手之前，先把一切抗拒料理妥当。

===Original===
The house had been built in the fifteenth century, and the entrance hall was a broad, high barn of a place. Some Georgian architect, in the peculiar manner of his kind, had built a small minstrel gallery over the dining-room entrance and immediately facing the study. Leon had already explored the house and had found the tiny staircase that led to this architectural monstrosity. He had no sooner given the signal than he dived into the dining-room, through the tall door, and was behind the thick curtains at the back of the narrow gallery when the first two men came in. He saw them go straight into the study and push open the door. At the same time a third man appeared under the porch, though he made no attempt to enter the hall.
===Chinese===
这宅子建于十五世纪，进门的大厅是一间又宽又高、谷仓似的大屋子。某位乔治王朝的建筑师，秉着他那一行特有的做派，在饭厅入口的上方、正对着书房的位置，造了一座小小的乐师楼座。莱昂早把宅子里外探看了一遍，找到了通往这处建筑怪物的窄楼梯。他一发完信号，便闪进饭厅，穿过那扇高大的房门，藏进窄楼座后头的厚帷幕里——头两个人进来时，他已就位。他看着他们径直走进书房，推开了房门。与此同时，第三个人出现在门廊底下，却没有要进大厅的意思。

===Original===
Presently one of those who had gone into the study came out and called Cuccini by name. When no answer came, he went grumbling back to his task. What that task was, Leon could guess, before the peculiarly acrid smell of hot steel was wafted to his sensitive nostrils.
===Chinese===
不多时，进书房去的两个人里有一个出来，喊库奇尼的名字。没人应声，他嘟嘟囔囔回去干他的活计了。那是桩什么活计，不等热钢那股特有的辛辣气味飘送到他灵敏的鼻端，莱昂就已猜着了。

===Original===
By crouching down he could see the legs of the men who were working at the safe. They had turned on all the lights, and apparently expected no interruption. The man at the door was joined by another man.
===Chinese===
蹲下身子，他望得见正在保险柜上干活的那几个人的腿。他们把灯全打开了，显然料不到会有人来搅扰。守在门口的那人身边，又来了一个。

===Original===
“Where is Lew?”

In the stillness of the house the words, though spoken in a low tone, were audible.

“I don’t know﻿—inside somewhere. He had to fix that dago.”

Leon grinned. This description of himself never failed to tickle him.
===Chinese===
「卢在哪儿？」

宅子里静极了，这话虽说得很低，也听得真切。

「不知道——多半在里头什么地方。他还得去收拾那个拉丁佬呢。」

莱昂咧嘴一乐。人家用这称呼说他，回回都叫他觉得好笑。

===Original===
One of the workers in the library came out at this point.

“Have you seen Cuccini?”

“No,” said the man at the door.

“Go in and find him. He ought to be here.”

Cuccini’s absence evidently made him uneasy, for though he returned to the room he was out again in a minute, asking if the messenger had come back. Then, from the back of the passage, came the searcher’s voice: “The kitchen’s locked.”
===Chinese===
这时，在书房里干活的人里有一个走了出来。

「见着库奇尼了吗？」

「没有。」守门的说。

「进去找找他。他这会儿本该在这儿。」

库奇尼的缺席显然叫他心神不定：他虽回了房里，不到一分钟又出来了，问派出去送信的回来没有。随后，过道深处传来那找人的声音：「厨房上锁了。」

===Original===
The safe-cutter uttered an expression of amazement.

“Locked? What’s the idea?”

He came to the foot of the stairs and bellowed up: “Cuccini!”

Only the echo answered him.
===Chinese===
割保险柜的那个人发出一声惊咦。

「锁了？这是耍的什么把戏？」

他走到楼梯脚下，朝上吼：「库奇尼！」

回答他的只有回声。

===Original===
“That’s queer.” He poked his head in the door of the study. “Rush that job, Mike. There’s some funny business here.” And over his shoulder, “Tell the boys to get ready to jump.”

The man went out into the night and was absent some minutes, to return with an alarming piece of news.

“They’ve gone, boss. I can’t see one of them.”
===Chinese===
「邪门。」他把脑袋探进书房门里。「加紧赶工，迈克。这儿有点蹊跷。」又扭头丢下一句：「叫弟兄们准备随时开溜。」

那人走到外头的夜色里去了，一去好几分钟，回来时带回一条叫人心惊的消息。

「都跑了，头儿。连个人影也看不见。」

===Original===
The “boss” cursed him, and himself went into the grounds on a visit of inspection. He came back in a hurry, ran into the study, and Leon heard his voice: “Stand ready to clear.”

“What about Cuccini?”

“Cuccini will have to look after himself﻿ ﻿… got it, Mike?”
===Chinese===
「头儿」骂了他一句，亲自到园子里巡视了一遭。他急匆匆赶回来，奔进书房，莱昂听见他的声音：「准备随时撤。」

「库奇尼怎么办？」

「库奇尼只好自己顾自己了……明白吗，迈克？」

===Original===
The deep voice said something. There followed the sound of a crack, as though something of iron had broken. It was the psychological moment. Leon parted the curtains and dropped lightly to the floor.
===Chinese===
那副低沉的嗓门应了一句。接着传来咔的一声脆响，仿佛什么铁器断了。正是千钧一发的关头。莱昂拨开帷幕，轻巧地落到地板上。

===Original===
The man at the door turned in a flash at the sound.

“Put ’em up!” he said sharply.
===Chinese===
门口那人闻声霍地转过身来。

「举起手来！」他厉声喝道。

===Original===
“Don’t shoot.” Leon’s voice was almost conversational in its calmness. “The house is surrounded by police.”

With an oath the man darted out of the door, and at that instant came the sound of the first shot, followed by desultory firing from the direction of the road. The second guard had been the first to go. Leon ran to the door, slammed it tight and switched on the lights as the two men came from the study. Under the arm of one was a thick pad of square brown sheets. He dropped his load and put up his hands at the sight of the gun; but his companion was made of harder material, and, with a yell, he leapt at the man who stood between him and freedom. Leon twisted aside, advanced his shoulder to meet the furious drive of the man’s fist; then, dropping his pistol, he stooped swiftly and tackled him below the knees. The man swayed, sought to recover his balance and fell with a crash on the stone floor. All the time his companion stood dazed and staring, his hands waving in the air.
===Chinese===
「别开枪。」莱昂的嗓音平静得近乎闲谈。「这宅子已被警察团团围住了。」

那人咒骂一声，夺门而出；就在这一瞬，响起了第一声枪响，跟着是大路方向稀稀落落的枪声。那第二名看守，倒是头一个开溜的。莱昂奔到门边，砰地把门关严，又拧亮了灯——书房里的两个人正冲出来。其中一个腋下夹着厚厚一沓方形的棕色纸页。一见枪口，他撂下那抱东西，举起了双手；可他的同伙却是块更硬的料，大吼一声，朝那个挡在他与自由之间的男人扑来。莱昂侧身一拧，送肩迎上那人怒拳的猛冲；随即撂下手枪，疾速俯身，朝膝下抱腿一揽。那人身子一晃，极力想稳住重心，轰隆一声摔在石板地上。这工夫，他那同伙始终呆站着直瞪眼，两只手在半空里乱摆。

===Original===
There was a knock at the outer door. Without turning his back upon his prisoners, Leon reached for the bar and pulled it up. Manfred came in.

“The gentleman who shouted ‘Cuccini’ scared them. I think they’ve got away. There were two cars parked on the road.”

His eyes fell upon the brown sheets scattered on the floor and he nodded.

“I think you have all you want, Leon,” he said.
===Chinese===
外门上响起叩门声。莱昂不把后背朝着俘虏，探身够到门闩，拉了起来。曼弗雷德走了进来。

「就是那位高叫『库奇尼』的先生把他们吓着了。我看他们已经跑掉了。路上原先停着两辆车。」

他的目光落在散了一地的棕色纸页上，点了点头。

「我想，你要的东西全都到手了，莱昂。」他说。

===Original===
The detectives came crowding in at that moment and secured their prisoners whilst Leon Gonsalez and his friend went out on to the lawn to search for Gurther.
===Chinese===
侦探们这时涌进门来，把俘虏们上了绑收押；莱昂·冈萨雷斯则同他的朋友走到草坪上，去寻格瑟。

===Original===
The man lay as he had fallen, on his face, and as Leon flashed his lamp upon the figure, he saw that the snake had struck behind the ear.

“Gurther?” frowned Leon.

He turned the figure on its back and gave a little gasp of surprise, for there looked up to the starry skies the heavy face of Pfeiffer.
===Chinese===
那人保持着倒地时的姿势，伏面躺着；莱昂把手电照上那身影，看出那条蛇已在耳后出了击。

「格瑟？」莱昂皱起了眉头。

他把那身影翻过来仰面朝天，不由得轻轻惊呼一声——只见朝着满天星斗仰望上去的那张沉甸甸的脸，竟是法伊弗。

===Original===
“Pfeiffer! I could have sworn it was the other! There has been some double-crossing here. Let me think.” He stood for fully a minute, his chin on his hand. “I could have understood Gurther; he was becoming a nuisance and a danger to the old man. Pfeiffer, the more reliable of the two, hated him. My first theory was that Gurther had been put out by order of Oberzohn.”
===Chinese===
「法伊弗！我本来可以赌咒发誓，说准是那一个！这里头有一场黑吃黑。容我想想。」他手托着下巴，足足站了一分钟。「死的是格瑟，这我还想得通：他渐渐成了老头儿的累赘和祸害。法伊弗——两人里更靠得住的那个——恨他。我最初的推测是：格瑟是奉欧伯佐恩之命被做掉的。」

===Original===
“Suppose Gurther heard that order, or came to know of it?” asked Manfred quietly.

Leon snapped his fingers.

“That is it! We had a similar case a few years ago, you will remember, George? The old man gave the ‘out’ order to Pfeiffer﻿—and Gurther got his blow in first. Shrewd fellow!”
===Chinese===
「假如格瑟听见了那道命令，或是打探到了呢？」曼弗雷德不动声色地问。

莱昂打了个响指。

「正是如此！几年前咱们经手过一桩相仿的案子，你总记得吧，乔治？老头儿把『做掉』的命令下给了法伊弗——结果格瑟抢先一步下了手。精明的家伙！」

===Original===
When they returned to the house, the three were seated in a row in Johnson Lee’s library. Cuccini, of course, was an old acquaintance. Of the other two men, Leon recognized one, a notorious gunman whose photograph had embellished the pages of *Hue and Cry* for months.

The third, and evidently the skilled workman of the party, for he it was whom they had addressed as “Mike” and who had burnt out the lock of Lee’s safe, was identified by Meadows as Mike Selwyn, a skilful burglar and bank-smasher, who had, according to his statement, only arrived from the Continent that afternoon in answer to a flattering invitation which promised considerable profit to himself.
===Chinese===
他们回到宅子里时，那三个人已一溜排开，坐在约翰逊·李的书房里。库奇尼自不必说，是个旧相识。另外两人里，莱昂认得其中一个——那是个声名狼藉的枪手，他的照片接连数月装点着《缉凶公报》（*Hue and Cry*）的版面。

第三个，显然是这伙人里的行家好手——他们方才唤作「迈克」的便是他，烧掉李家保险柜门锁的也是他——被米多斯认出是迈克·塞尔温（Mike Selwyn），一个手段娴熟的窃贼兼爆银行的老手；据他自陈，他当天下午才应一封许以厚利的捧场邀约，从欧洲大陆赶到此地。

===Original===
“And why I left Milan,” he said bitterly, “where the graft is easy and the money’s good, I’d like you to tell me!”
===Chinese===
「我到底为什么要离开米兰，」他愤愤地说，「那地方营生容易，来钱又多——你们倒给我说说看！」

===Original===
The prisoners were removed to the nearest secure lockup, and by the time Lee’s servants returned from their dance, all evidence of an exciting hour had disappeared, except that the blackened and twisted door of the safe testified to the sinister character of the visitation.
===Chinese===
俘虏们被押往就近一处稳当的拘留所；等李家的仆人们跳舞归来，那惊心动魄的一个钟头留下的一切痕迹都已收拾干净，唯独保险柜那扇烧得焦黑、扭作一团的柜门，还见证着这番造访的凶险。

===Original===
Meadows returned as they were gathering together the scattered sheets. There were hundreds of them, all written in Braille characters, and Manfred’s sensitive fingers were skimming their surface.
===Chinese===
他们正把散落的纸页归拢到一处，米多斯回来了。纸页数以百计，全以盲文写就，曼弗雷德那双敏锐的手指正贴着纸面飞快掠过。

===Original===
“Oh, yes,” he said, in answer to a question that was put to him, “I knew Lee was blind, the day we searched Barberton’s effects. That was my mystery.” He laughed. “Barberton expected a call from his old friend and had left a message for him on the mantelpiece. Do you remember that strip of paper? It ran: ‘Dear Johnny, I will be back in an hour.’ These are letters,”﻿—he indicated the papers.
===Chinese===
「哦，是的，」他在回答别人的一句问话时说，「我们搜检巴伯顿遗物的那一天，我就知道李是个盲人。我当时那桩谜，谜底就在这里。」他笑了。「巴伯顿料定他的老朋友会来造访，在壁炉台上给他留了话。你总记得那一小条纸吧？上面写着：『亲爱的约翰尼，我一小时后就回来。』这些就是信件，」——他指了指那堆纸。

===Original===
“The folds tell me that,” said Meadows. “You may not get a conviction against Cuccini; the two burglars will come up before a judge, but to charge Cuccini means the whole story of the snake coming out, and that means a bigger kick than I’m prepared to laugh away﻿—I am inclined to let Cuccini go for the moment.”
===Chinese===
「折痕告诉了我这一点。」米多斯说。「你们未必能坐实库奇尼的罪名；那两个窃贼是要提到法官面前受审的，可一旦指控库奇尼，就得把『毒蛇』的全案始末抖搂出来，那掀起的动静就大得不是我准备一笑置之的了——我倾向于眼下先放库奇尼一马。」

===Original===
Manfred nodded. He sat with the embossed sheets on his knee.

“Written from various places,” he went on.

It was curious to see him, his fingers running swiftly along the embossed lines, his eyes fixed on vacancy.
===Chinese===
曼弗雷德点了点头。他把那沓凸点纸页搁在膝上坐着。

「是在各个不同的地方写下的。」他接着说。

看他念书的模样很叫人称奇：手指沿着一行行凸起的点字疾速游走，双眼却凝望着虚空。

===Original===
“So far I’ve learnt nothing, except that in his spare time Barberton amused himself by translating native fairy stories into English and putting them into Braille for use in the blind school. I knew, of course, that he did that, because I’d already interviewed his sister, who is the mistress of the girls’ section.”
===Chinese===
「到目前为止我一无所获，只知道巴伯顿闲暇时以此自娱：把土著的神话故事译成英文，再转成盲文，供盲人学校使用。这个我自然早知道，因为我已经访问过他的妹妹——她就是那学校女生部的学监。」

===Original===
He had gone through half a dozen letters when he rose from the table and walked across to the safe.

“I have a notion that the thing we’re seeking is not here,” he said. “It is hardly likely that he would allow a communication of that character to be jumbled up with the rest of the correspondence.”
===Chinese===
他看完了六封信，便从桌边站起身，走到保险柜跟前。

「我有个预感，咱们要找的东西不在这儿。」他说。「他多半不会容许一封这等性质的函件，同其余的信函混作一堆。」

===Original===
The safe door was open and the steel drawer at the back had been pulled out. Evidently it was from this receptacle that the letters had been taken. Now the drawer was empty. Manfred took it out and measured the depth of it with his finger.

“Let me see,” said Gonsalez suddenly.

He groped along the floor of the safe, and presently he began to feel carefully along the sides.
===Chinese===
保险柜门敞开着，后壁的钢抽屉已被拉了出来。那些信显然就是从这个容器里取走的。此刻抽屉空空如也。曼弗雷德把它整个抽出，用手指量了量它的深度。

「我瞧瞧。」冈萨雷斯忽然说。

他在柜底四下摸索，不多时，又开始沿着两壁细细探摸。

===Original===
“Nothing here,” he said. He drew out half a dozen account books and a bundle of documents which at first glance Manfred had put aside as being personal to the owner of Rath Hall. These were lying on the floor amidst the mass of molten metal that had burnt deep holes in the carpet. Leon examined the books one by one, opening them and running his nail along the edge of the pages. The fourth, a weighty ledger, did not open so easily﻿—did not, indeed, open at all. He carried it to the table and tried to pull back the cover. “Now, how does this open?”
===Chinese===
「这里头没有。」他说。他又抽出半打账本和一捆文件——那捆文件，曼弗雷德起初扫了一眼，便当作拉思府主人的私物搁在了一边。它们摊在地上，混在一摊熔化的金属里——那摊熔铁在地毯上烧出一个个深深的窟窿。莱昂把账本一本本查看过去，翻开，用指甲顺着页边刮。第四本是个沉甸甸的大账本，翻起来不甚爽利——实际上压根儿翻不开。他把它端到桌上，试着把封面扳开。「咦，这本子是怎么个开法？」

===Original===
The ledger covers were of leather; to all appearance a very ordinary book, and Leon was anxious not to disturb so artistic a camouflage. Examining the edge carefully, he saw a place where the edges had been forced apart. Taking out a knife, he slipped the thin blade into the aperture. There was a click and the cover sprang up like the lid of a box.
===Chinese===
账本的封面是皮面的；任谁看去都是一本再平常不过的书，莱昂一心不想惊动如此精妙的一件伪装。他细细察看切口，发现有一处边口曾被撬开过。他掏出刀来，把薄薄的刀刃送进那道缝隙。咔哒一声，封面像盒盖似的弹了起来。

===Original===
“And this, I think, is what we are looking for,” said Gonsalez.

The interior of the book had been hollowed out, the edges being left were gummed tight, and the receptacle thus formed was packed close with brown papers; brown, except for one, which was written on a large sheet of foolscap, headed:

> “Bureau of the Ministry of Colonies, Lisbon.”
===Chinese===
「我想，咱们要找的正是这个。」冈萨雷斯说。

书的内里被掏空了，留下的边缘用胶粘得严严实实，这样形成的暗匣里，密密实实塞满了棕色纸页；都是棕色，唯独一份例外——那一份写在一大张公文纸上，抬头是：

> 「里斯本殖民地部公署」

===Original===
Barberton had superimposed upon this long document his Braille writing, and now one of the mysteries was cleared up.

“Lee said he had never received any important documents,” said Manfred, “and, of course, he hadn’t, so far as he knew. To him this was merely a sheet of paper on which Braille characters were inscribed. Read this, Leon.”
===Chinese===
巴伯顿把自己的盲文覆写在这份长长的公文之上，于是一桩疑团就此解开了。

「李说过，他从未收到过什么重要文件，」曼弗雷德说，「当然，就他所知，他也确实没收过。在他眼里，这不过是一张写着盲文字符的纸罢了。念念这个，莱昂。」

===Original===
Leon scanned the letter. It was dated “July 21st, 1912,” and bore, in the lower left-hand corner, the seal of the Portuguese Colonial Office. He read it through rapidly and at the end looked up with a sigh of satisfaction.

“And this settles Oberzohn and Co., and robs them of a fortune, the extent of which I think we shall discover when we read Barberton’s letter.”
===Chinese===
莱昂把信浏览了一遍。落款日期是「1912 年 7 月 21 日」，左下角钤着葡萄牙殖民地部的官印。他飞快地通读了一遍，末了满意地轻叹一声，抬起头来。

「这一下就把欧伯佐恩一伙定了局，还夺走他们一笔巨财——财有多大，我想等咱们读了巴伯顿的信，自会分晓。」

===Original===
He lit a cigarette and scanned the writing again, whilst Meadows, who did not understand Leon’s passion for drama, waited with growing impatience.

> “**Illustrious Senhor**,” began Leon, reading. “I have this day had the honour of placing before His Excellency the President, and the Ministers of the Cabinet, your letter dated May 15th, 1912. By a letter dated January 8th, 1911, the lands marked Ex. 275 on the Survey Map of the Biskara district, were conceded to you, Illustrious Senhor, in order to further the cause of science﻿—a cause which is very dear to the heart of His Excellency the President. Your further letter, in which you complain, Illustrious Senhor, that the incursion of prospectors upon your land is hampering your scientific work, and your request that an end may be put to these annoyances by the granting to you of an extension of the concession, so as to give you title to all minerals found in the aforesaid area, Ex. 275 on the Survey Map of Biskara, and thus making the intrusion of prospectors illegal, has been considered by the Council, and the extending concession is hereby granted, on the following conditions: The term of the concession shall be for twelve years, as from the 14th day of June, 1912, and shall be renewable by you, your heirs or nominees, every twelfth year, on payment of a nominal sum of 1,000 milreis. In the event of the concessionnaire, his heirs or nominees, failing to apply for a renewal on the 14th day of June, 1924, the mineral rights of the said area, Ex. 275 on the Survey Map of Biskara, shall be open to claim in accordance with the laws of Angola﻿—”
===Chinese===
他点上一支香烟，又把那纸文字审读起来；米多斯不懂得莱昂这份做戏的癖好，等得越来越不耐烦。

> 「**显赫的先生**（Illustrious Senhor），」莱昂念道。「本日，业已荣幸地将阁下 1912 年 5 月 15 日的来函，呈置于总统大人阁下暨内阁诸位部长之前。查据 1911 年 1 月 8 日公函，比斯卡拉区测绘图上标为 Ex. 275 的土地，业已让与显赫的先生，以襄科学之业——此业乃总统大人阁下心中所深系。显赫的先生复函陈称，探矿人等侵入贵地，有碍阁下科学之务，并恳请将租契延展授予阁下，俾阁下取得前述区域（比斯卡拉区测绘图 Ex. 275）内所出一切矿产之产权，如此则探矿人等的侵越即属违法，以杜绝此类烦扰——该函业经部务会议审议，兹特批准延展租契，其条件如下：本租契期限为十二年，自 1912 年 6 月 14 日起算；每满十二年，得由阁下、阁下之继承人或受让人缴付名义款额 1,000 米尔雷斯（milreis）续展一次。倘承租人、其继承人或受让人未于 1924 年 6 月 14 日申请续展，前述区域（比斯卡拉区测绘图 Ex. 275）之矿权，即依照安哥拉法律开放于他人声请——」

===Original===
Leon sat back.

“Fourteenth of June?” he said, and looked up. “Why, that is next week﻿—five days! We’ve cut it rather fine, George.”
===Chinese===
莱昂向后一靠。

「六月十四？」他说着抬起眼来。「哎呀，那就是下礼拜——只剩五天了！咱们这日子掐得也够悬的，乔治。」

===Original===
“Barberton said there were six weeks,” said Manfred. “Obviously he made the mistake of timing the concession from July 21st﻿—the date of the letter. He must have been the most honest man in the world; there was no other reason why he should have communicated with Miss Leicester. He could have kept quiet and claimed the rights for himself. Go on, Leon.”
===Chinese===
「巴伯顿说的是还有六个礼拜。」曼弗雷德说。「他显然算错了时限——把租契从 7 月 21 日、也就是那封信的日期起算了。他准是天下头一号的诚实人；要不然，他断没有理由去联络莱斯特小姐。他满可以一声不吭，把矿权自己占了。往下念，莱昂。」

===Original===
“That is about all,” said Leon, glancing at the tail of the letter. “The rest is more or less flowery and complimentary and has reference to the scientific work in which Professor Leicester was engaged. Five days﻿—phew!” he whistled.
===Chinese===
「差不多就这些了。」莱昂扫了一眼信的末尾说。「余下的无非是些花团锦簇的客套，说的都是莱斯特教授所致力的那门科学工作。五天——嚯！」他打了个呼哨。

===Original===
“We may now find something in Barberton’s long narrative to give us an idea of the value of this property.” Manfred turned the numerous pages. “Do any of you gentlemen write shorthand?”

Meadows went out into the hall and brought back an officer. Waiting until he had found pencil and paper, Leon began the extraordinary story of William Barberton﻿—most extraordinary because every word had been patiently and industriously punched in the Braille characters.
===Chinese===
「如今咱们兴许能在巴伯顿那篇长叙述里寻出点什么，好掂一掂这份产业的价值。」曼弗雷德翻动那厚厚一沓纸页。「诸位先生里，有会速记的吗？」

米多斯出去到门厅里，带回一名警员。等那人找齐了铅笔和纸，莱昂便开始讲威廉·巴伯顿那篇异乎寻常的故事——其所以格外异乎寻常，是因为每一个字，都是他耐着性子、勤勤恳恳地一个点一个点戳成盲文的。

## XVIII The Story of Mont d’Or / 十八 蒙多尔山的故事
===Original===
> “Dear Friend Johnny—
> “I have such a lot to tell you that I hardly know where to begin. I’ve struck rich at last, and the dream I’ve often talked over with you has come true. First of all, let me tell you that I have come upon nearly £50,000 worth of wrought gold. We’ve been troubled round here with lions, one of which took away a carrier of mine, and at last I decided to go out and settle accounts with this fellow. I found him six miles from the camp and planted a couple of bullets into him without killing him, and decided to follow up his spoor. It was a mad thing to do, trailing a wounded lion in the jungle, and I didn’t realize how mad until we got out of the bush into the hills and I found Mrs. Lion waiting for me. She nearly got me too. More by accident than anything else, I managed to shoot her dead at the first shot, and got another pot at her husband as he was slinking into a cave which was near our tent.

===Chinese===
> 「亲爱的朋友约翰尼：
> 「我要对你说的话实在太多，竟不知从何说起。我终于挖着富矿了，常同你念叨的那个梦，这回成了真。头一件事先告诉你：我撞见了一批精工金器，价值将近 £50,000。这一带狮子搅得我们不得安生，其中一头叼走了我的一名挑夫，我索性决定出去同这家伙清账。在离营地六英里的地方我寻着了它，给它身上种进两颗子弹，却没能要它的命，便决意循着兽迹追下去。在丛林里缀着一头受伤的狮子走，本就是桩疯子干的事；直到我们钻出灌木丛进了山，我发现狮子太太正候着我，这才明白这事儿疯到了什么地步。她也差一点要了我的命。与其说凭本事，不如说凭撞上的大运，我头一枪就把它撂倒了；它丈夫正溜进我们帐篷近旁的一个山洞，我又朝它顺势补了一枪。

===Original===
> “As I had gone so far, I thought I might as well go the whole hog, especially as I’d seen two lion cubs playing round the mouth of the cave, and bringing up my boys, who were scared to death, I crawled in, to find, as I expected, that the old lion was nearly gone, and a shot finished him. I had to kill the cubs; they were too young to be left alone, and too much of a nuisance to bring back to camp. This cave had been used as a lair for years; it was full of bones, human amongst them.

===Chinese===
> 「事已至此，我想索性一不做二不休，何况我早瞧见两只幼狮在洞口玩耍。我把吓得魂不附体的那班小子吆喝上来，自己爬进洞去，果然不出所料，那头老狮子已近断气，一枪送了终。两只幼狮也只好杀掉：撇下不管吧，它们太小；带回营去，又实在太累赘。这洞多少年来一直被当作兽窟，里头堆满了骨头，其中还夹着人骨。

===Original===
> “But what struck me was the appearance of the roof, which, I was almost certain, had been cut out by hand. It was like a house, and there was a cut door in the rock at the back. I made a torch and went through on a tour of inspection, and you can imagine my surprise when I found myself in a little room with a line of stone niches or shelves. There were three lines of them on each side. Standing on these at intervals there were little statuettes. They were so covered with dust that I thought they were stone, until I tried to take one down to examine it; then I knew by its weight that it was gold, as they all were.

===Chinese===
> 「真正叫我吃惊的是洞顶的模样——我几乎可以断定，那是人工开凿出来的。洞里像一间屋宇，后头的岩壁上还凿着一道门。我扎了个火把，穿进去巡视了一遭；你可以想见我那时的惊异：我发现自己站在一间小屋里，屋里砌着一列列石龛，或者叫石搁架，每边三列，架上相间立着小小的塑像。它们蒙尘太厚，我起初只当是石头的；及至取下一尊细看，那分量立刻告诉我：这是金的——而且尊尊都是。

===Original===
> “I didn’t want my boys to know about my find, because they are a treacherous lot, so I took the lightest, after weighing them all with a spring balance, and made a note where I’d taken it from. You might think that was enough of a find for one man in a lifetime, but my luck had set in. I sent the boys back and ordered them to break camp and join me on top of the Thaba. I called it the Thaba, because it is rather like a hill I know in Basutoland, and is one of two.

===Chinese===
> 「我可不想叫我那班小子知道这桩发现——那是一帮靠不住的家伙。于是我用弹簧秤把金像一一过了秤，只拣最轻的一尊带走，还记下了取它出来的位置。你或许以为，一个人一辈子撞上这一注已尽够了；可我的好运这才刚开头。我打发小子们回营，传令拔营，上山到塔巴（Thaba）顶上同我会合。我管它叫塔巴，因为它颇像我熟识的巴苏陀兰（Basutoland）的一座山，而且是双峰之一。

===Original===
> “The camp was moved up that night; it was a better pitch than any we had had. There was water, plenty of small game, and no mosquitoes. The worst part of it was the terrific thunderstorms which come up from nowhere, and until you’ve seen one in this ironstone country you don’t know what a thunderstorm is like! The hill opposite was slightly smaller than the one I had taken as a camp, and between was a shallow valley, through which ran a small shallow river—rapids would be a better word.

===Chinese===
> 「当夜营地便搬了上去；那营址比我们一路所扎的哪一处都强。有水，小野味多的是，又没有蚊子。最糟的是那凭空里就压过来的可怕雷暴——不经见一场这铁矿石山地的雷暴，你就不算知道雷暴是什么模样！对面那座山比我扎营的这座略小些，两山之间是一道浅谷，谷里淌着一条浅浅的小河——说它是一片激流浅滩反倒更贴切。

===Original===
> “Early the next morning I was looking round through my glasses, and saw what I thought was a house on the opposite hill. I asked my headman who lived there, and he told me that it was once the house of the Star Chief; and I remembered that somebody told me, down in Mossamedes, that an astronomer had settled in this neighbourhood and had been murdered by the natives. I thought I would go over and have a look at the place. The day being cloudy and not too hot, I took my gun and a couple of boys and we crossed the river and began climbing the hill. The house was, of course, in ruins; it had only been a wattle hut at the best of times. Part of it was covered with vegetation, but out of curiosity I searched round, hoping to pick up a few things that might be useful to me, more particularly kettles, for my boys had burnt holes in every one I had. I found a kettle, and then, turning over a heap of rubbish which I think must have been his bed, I found a little rusty tin box and broke it open with my stick. There were a few letters which were so faded that I could only read a word here and there, and in a green oilskin, a long letter from the Portuguese Government.”

===Chinese===
> 「第二天一早，我举着望远镜四下瞭望，瞧见对面山上像是有座房子。我问工头那里住的是谁，他说那从前是「星酋」（Star Chief）的宅子；我记起在莫萨马德斯时听人说过，有位天文学家在这一带落了脚，后来叫土人害了。我想过去瞧瞧那地方。那天多云，不算太热，我带上枪，领了两个小子，过了河，开始爬山。那房子自然早已坍圮；便在它顶好的年月，也不过是一座枝条糊泥的棚屋。半截埋在荒草藤蔓里，我却出于好奇四下翻寻，指望捡几样于我有用的物件，尤其是水壶——我带的壶，叫我那班小子一只只都烧出了窟窿。我寻着了一只壶，随后又翻开一堆破烂——我想那必是他的床铺——翻出一只小小的锈铁皮盒，用手杖把它撬开。里面有寥寥几封信，字迹褪得只能东一个西一个认出个把字；还有一层绿油布，包着一封葡萄牙政府来的长信。」

===Original===
> (It was at this point, either by coincidence or design, that the narrative continued on the actual paper to which he referred.)

===Chinese===
> （正是写到这里——不知是出于巧合还是有意安排——他的叙述便接续在了他所提到的那份文书原件之上。）

===Original===
> “I speak Portuguese and can read it as easily as English, and the only thing that worried me about it was that the concession gave Professor Leicester all rights to my cave. My first idea was to burn it, but then I began to realize what a scoundrelly business that would be, and I took the letters out into the sun and tried to find if he had any relations, hoping that I’d be able to fix it up with them to take at any rate 50 percent of my find. There was only one letter that helped me. It was written in a child’s hand and was evidently from his daughter. It had no address, but there was the name—‘Mirabelle Leicester.’

===Chinese===
> 「我懂葡萄牙文，读起来同英文一样轻松。叫我犯嘀咕的只有一桩：那纸租契把我这山洞的一切权利，尽数给了莱斯特教授。我的头一个念头是把它烧了；可转念一想，又明白那是何等下作的勾当。我把信件拿到太阳底下，想查查他有无亲眷，指望能同他们私下讲妥，叫他们至少占我这注发现的 50%。于我有用的只有一封信。那信出自孩童笔迹，显然是他女儿写的。信上没有地址，只落着一个名字——『米拉贝尔·莱斯特』。

===Original===
> “I put it in my pocket with the concession and went on searching, but found nothing more. I was going down the hill towards the valley when it struck me that perhaps this man had found gold, and the excuse for getting the concession was a bit of artfulness. I sent a boy back to the camp for a pick, a hammer and a spade, and when he returned I began to make a cutting in the side of the hill. There was nothing to guide me—no outcrop, such as you usually find near a true reef—but I hadn’t been digging for an hour before I struck the richest bed of conglomerate I’ve ever seen. I was either dreaming, or my good angel had at last led me to the one place in the hill where gold could be found. I had previously sent the boys back to the camp and told them to wait for me, because, if I did strike metal, I did not want the fact advertised all over Angola, where they’ve been looking for gold for years.

===Chinese===
> 「我把信连同租契揣进衣袋，接着翻寻，却再无所获。我正顺山坡往谷底走，忽然起了一个念头：兴许这人早就找着了金子，讨这份租契的由头，不过是耍的一点滑头。我打发一个小子回营取鹤嘴锄、铁锤和铁锹；等他回来，我便在山腰上开起一道探槽。没有任何可资指引的东西——没有露头，那种你在真正矿脉近旁常见的露头——可我挖了不到一个钟头，就凿上了我此生所见最富的一片砾岩层。若非在做梦，便是我的守护天使终于把我领到了这山里唯一藏金的地方。此前我早把小子们打发回营，叫他们等我——因为一旦真挖着了矿，我可不愿这消息扬得整个安哥拉都知道，那里的人找金子已经找了多少年。

===Original===
> “Understand, it was not a reef in the ordinary sense of the word, it was all conglomerate, and the wider I made my cutting, the wider the bed appeared. I took the pick to another part of the hill and dug again, with the same result—conglomerate. It was as though nature had thrown up a huge golden hump on the earth. I covered both cuttings late that night and went back to camp. (I was stalked by a leopard in the low bush, but managed to get him.)

===Chinese===
> 「你要明白，这不是寻常字面意义上的矿脉，全是砾岩；我的探槽开得越宽，那岩层显得越宽。我又扛起鹤嘴锄到山的另一处再挖，结果照样——还是砾岩。就像大自然在大地上堆起了一座硕大无朋的金色驼峰。当夜深更，我把两处探槽都掩埋妥当，回了营。（穿过矮树丛时，一头豹子悄悄缀上了我，好在叫我打发掉了。）

===Original===
> “Early next morning, I started off and tried another spot, and with the same result; first three feet of earth, then about six inches of shale, and then conglomerate. I tried to work through the bed, thinking that it might be just a skin, but I was saved much exertion by coming upon a deep rift in the hill about twenty feet wide at the top and tapering down to about fifty feet below the ground level. This gave me a section to work on, and as near as I can judge, the conglomerate bed is something over fifty feet thick and I’m not so sure that it doesn’t occur again after an interval of twenty feet or more, for I dug more shale and had a showing of conglomerate at the very bottom of the ravine.

===Chinese===
> 「第二天一早我又出动，换了个地点试挖，结果依旧：先见三英尺浮土，再是约六英寸页岩，接着便是砾岩。我只当这岩层兴许是一层薄皮，便想凿穿它看看；幸而山体上有一道深深的裂谷，省了我许多力气——谷口宽约二十英尺，往下渐次收窄，直收到地面以下约五十英尺处。这等于替我现成剖开了一段剖面。据我估量，这砾岩层厚达五十多英尺；而且隔上二十英尺乃至更多之后，底下会不会再来一层，我也说不准——因为我在裂谷的最底下又掘出页岩，见到了砾岩的露苗。

===Original===
> “What does this mean, Johnny? It means that we have found a hill of gold; not solid gold, as in the storybooks, but gold that pays ounces and probably pounds to the ton. How the prospectors have missed it all these years I can’t understand, unless it is that they’ve made their cuttings on the north side of the hill, where they have found nothing but slate and sandstone. The little river in the valley must be feet deep in alluvial, for I panned the bed and got eight ounces of pure gold in an hour—and that was by rough and ready methods. I had to be careful not to make the boys too curious, and I am breaking camp tomorrow, and I want you to cable or send me £500 to Mossamedes. The statuette I’m bringing home is worth all that. I would bring more, only I can’t trust these Angola boys; a lot of them are mission boys and can read Portuguese, and they’re too friendly with a half-breed called Villa, who is an agent of Oberzohn & Smitts; the traders and I know these people to be the most unscrupulous scoundrels on the coast.

===Chinese===
> 「这意味着什么，约翰尼？意味着我们发现了一座金山——不是故事书里那种实心的金子，而是每吨出金以盎司计、弄好了以磅计的金子。这些年勘探的人怎么会把它漏掉，我真想不通；除非他们的探槽都开在了山北坡，那里除了板岩和砂岩，什么也没有。谷里那条小河，冲积砂金少说也有几英尺厚：我用淘盘淘洗河床，一个钟头得了八盎司纯金——用的还是因陋就简的土法子。我不得不留神，别引得小子们起了疑心。我明天拔营，你要即刻电汇、或径直寄 £500 到莫萨马德斯给我。我带回家的这一尊金像，就值回这全部数目。本想多带几尊，只是这些安哥拉小子信不过：他们好些是教会学堂出来的，识得葡萄牙文，又同一个名叫维拉的混血儿走得太近——那人是欧伯佐恩-斯米茨商行的代理；做买卖的和我都知道，这一带海岸上再没有比这伙人更无法无天的恶棍了。

===Original===
> “I shall be at Mossamedes about three weeks after you get this letter, but I don’t want to get back to the coast in a hurry, otherwise people are going to suspect I have made a strike.”

===Chinese===
> 「你接到这封信约莫三个礼拜后，我就该到莫萨马德斯了；但我不急于赶回海岸——不然人家就要疑心我挖着矿了。」

===Original===
Leon put the letter down.

“There is the story in a nutshell, gentlemen,” he said. “I don’t, for one moment, believe that Mr. Barberton showed Villa the letter. It is more likely that one of the educated natives he speaks about saw it and reported it to Oberzohn’s agent. Portuguese is the lingua franca of that part of the coast. Barberton was killed to prevent his meeting the girl and telling her of his find—incidentally, of warning her to apply for a renewal of the concession. It wasn’t even necessary that they should search his belongings to recover the letter, because once they knew of its existence and the date which Barberton had apparently confounded with the date the letter was written, their work was simply to present an application to the Colonial Office at Lisbon. It was quite different after Barberton was killed, when they learnt or guessed that the letter was in Mr. Lee’s possession.”

===Chinese===
莱昂把信放下。

「整个故事，就这么一目了然了，诸位。」他说。「巴伯顿先生把这封信给维拉看过——这一点我片刻也不信。更可能的是，他信里提到的那班识字的土人中，有一个撞见了此信，报与了欧伯佐恩的代理。葡萄牙语是那一带海岸的通用语。杀掉巴伯顿，为的就是不叫他见着姑娘、不叫他把自己的发现讲给她听——顺带也就不叫她警觉，去申请续展那纸租契。他们甚至犯不着去搜他的行李、把信起回来；因为一旦知道了这封信的存在、又知道了那个日期——巴伯顿显然把它同写信的日期弄混了——余下的活计，便只是向里斯本的殖民部递上一纸申请。待到巴伯顿一死，他们得知、或猜到了信在李先生手里，情形便全然两样了。」

===Original===
Meadows agreed.

“That was the idea behind Oberzohn’s engagement of Mirabelle Leicester?”

===Chinese===
米多斯表示赞同。

「欧伯佐恩雇用米拉贝尔·莱斯特，根子就在这上头？」

===Original===
“Exactly, and it was also behind the attack upon Heavytree Farm. To secure this property they must get her away and keep her hidden either until it is too late for her to apply for a renewal, or until she has been bullied or forced into appointing a nominee.”

===Chinese===
「正是；攻打赫维特里农场，根子也在这上头。要谋得这份产业，他们必须把她掳走匿起，或则拖到她想申请续展也为时已晚，或则逼她屈从、胁迫她指定一个挂名的代理人。」

===Original===
“Or married,” said Leon briskly. “Did that idea occur to you? Our tailor-made friend, Monty Newton, may have had matrimonial intentions. It would have been quite a good stroke of business to secure a wife and a large and auriferous hill at the same time. This, I think, puts a period to the ambitions of Herr Doktor Oberzohn.”

===Chinese===
「再不然，便是成亲。」莱昂爽利地说。「这一层你可曾想到？咱们那位一身定制的时髦朋友蒙蒂·牛顿，保不齐打的正是娶亲的主意。一笔买卖同时到手一位太太和一座广产金砂的大山，实在算是桩好生意。我想，这可给欧伯佐恩博士先生的野心画上句点了。」

===Original===
He got up from the table and handed the papers to the custody of the detective, and turned with a quizzical smile to his friend.

“George, do you look forward with any pleasure to a two hundred and fifty miles’ drive?”

“Are you the chauffeur?” asked George.

“I am the chauffeur,” said Leon cheerfully. “I have driven a car for many years and I have not been killed yet. It is unlikely that I shall risk my precious life and yours tonight. Come with me and I promise never to hit her up above sixty except on the real speedways.”

===Chinese===
他从桌边起身，把那沓文件交由探长收执，随即含着半分调侃的笑意，转向他的朋友。

「乔治，一程两百五十英里的车程，你可是带着几分欣然期待？」

「司机是你？」乔治问。

「司机正是敝人。」莱昂欣然道。「汽车我开了许多年，至今尚未把命送掉。今夜我还不至于拿我这条金贵的性命、连同你的性命去涉险。跟我来吧，我担保——除非在真正的高速车道上，绝不把她踩过时速六十英里。」

===Original===
Manfred nodded.

“We will stop at Oxley and try to get a phone call through to Gloucester,” said Leon. “This line is, of course, out of order. They would do nothing so stupid as to neglect the elementary precaution of disconnecting Rath Hall.”

===Chinese===
曼弗雷德点了点头。

「我们在奥克斯利（Oxley）停一停，想法子把电话接到格洛斯特。」莱昂说。「拉思府这条线，自然是断了的。他们才不会蠢到连切断拉思府电话线这份起码的防备都疏忽掉。」

===Original===
At Oxley the big Spanz pulled up before the dark and silent exterior of an inn, and Leon, getting down, brought the half-clad landlord to the door and explained his mission, and also learned that two big cars had passed through half an hour before, going in the direction of London.

“That was the gang. I wonder how they’ll explain to their paymaster their second failure?”

===Chinese===
到了奥克斯利，那辆大「斯潘兹」在一座漆黑沉寂的旅店门前停住。莱昂下了车，把衣衫不整的店主唤到门口，说明来意，又打听到半个钟头之前，有两辆大车打此经过，往伦敦方向去了。

「那就是那伙人。这第二回失手，他们回去怎么向发饷的主子交代，我倒想知道。」

===Original===
His first call was to the house in Curzon Street, but there was no reply. “Ring them again,” said Leon. “You left Poiccart there?”

Manfred nodded.

They waited for five minutes; still there was no reply.

“How queer!” said Manfred. “It isn’t like Poiccart to leave the house. Get Gloucester.”

===Chinese===
他头一个电话打往柯曾街的宅子，却无人应答。「再打一遍。」莱昂说。「波伊卡特是你留在那儿的？」

曼弗雷德点了点头。

他们等了五分钟；仍旧无人应答。

「怪事！」曼弗雷德说。「波伊卡特可不是会离宅的人。接格洛斯特。」

===Original===
At this hour of the night the lines are comparatively clear, and in a very short time he heard the Gloucester operator’s voice, and in a few seconds later the click that told them they were connected with Heavytree Farm. Here there was some delay before the call was answered.

It was not Mirabelle Leicester nor her aunt who spoke. Nor did he recognize the voice of Digby, who had recovered sufficiently to return to duty.

===Chinese===
夜里这个时辰，线路相对清净，不多时他便听到了格洛斯特接线生的声音，又过几秒，咔哒一响——告知他们已接通赫维特里农场。接电话之前，那头耽搁了片刻。

开口的既不是米拉贝尔·莱斯特，也不是她姨妈。他也听不出那是迪格比的声音——迪格比的伤已将养得差不多，回岗当差来了。

===Original===
“Who is that?” asked the voice sharply. “Is that you, sergeant?”

“No, it is Mr. Meadows,” said Leon mendaciously.

“The Scotland Yard gentleman?” It was an eager inquiry.

“I’m Constable Kirk, of the Gloucester Police. My sergeant’s been trying to get in touch with you, sir.”

“What is the matter?” asked Leon, a cold feeling at his heart.

===Chinese===
「是哪一位？」那声音厉声问。「是巡佐您吗？」

「不是，我是米多斯先生。」莱昂扯了个谎。

「是苏格兰场那位先生？」问得急切。

「我是格洛斯特警局的巡警柯克（Kirk），先生。我们巡佐一直在设法同您联系。」

「出了什么事？」莱昂问，心头掠过一阵寒意。

===Original===
“I don’t know, sir. About half an hour ago, I was riding past here—I’m one of the mounted men—and I saw the door wide open and all the lights on, and when I came in there was nobody up. I woke Miss Goddard and Mr. Digby, but the young lady was not in the house.”

“Lights everywhere?” asked Leon quickly.

“Yes, sir—in the parlour at any rate.”

“No sign of a struggle?”

“No, sir, but a car passed me three miles from the house and it was going at a tremendous rate. I think she may have been in that. Mr. Digby and Miss Goddard have just gone into Gloucester.”

“All right, officer. I am sending Mr. Gonsalez down to see you,” said Leon, and hung up the receiver.

===Chinese===
「说不清，先生。约莫半个钟头前，我骑马路过这里——我是骑警队的一员——瞧见宅门四敞大开，灯火通明；进得屋来，却无一人起身。我唤醒了戈达德小姐和迪格比先生，可那位年轻小姐不在屋里。」

「处处都亮着灯？」莱昂急问。

「是的，先生——至少客厅如此。」

「没有搏斗的痕迹？」

「没有，先生。只是在离宅子三英里处，一辆汽车从我身旁掠过，速度快得吓人。我想她多半就在那辆车里。迪格比先生和戈达德小姐刚刚进了格洛斯特城。」

「好，警官。我这就派冈萨雷斯先生下去见你。」莱昂说着，挂上了听筒。

===Original===
“What is it?” asked George Manfred, who knew that something was wrong by his friend’s face.

“They’ve got Mirabelle Leicester after all,” said Leon. “I’m afraid I shall have to break my promise to you, George. That machine of mine is going to travel before daybreak!”

===Chinese===
「怎么回事？」乔治·曼弗雷德问——看朋友的脸色，他已明白出了岔子。

「他们到底还是把米拉贝尔·莱斯特弄到手了。」莱昂说。「只怕我得对你食言了，乔治。我那台机器，天亮之前可要跑一趟了！」

## XIX At Heavytree Farm / 十九 赫维特里农场
===Original===
It had been agreed that, having failed in their attack, and their energies for the moment being directed to Rath Hall, an immediate return of the Old Guard to Heavytree Farm was unlikely. This had been Meadows' view, and Leon and his friend were of the same mind. Only Poiccart, that master strategist, working surely with a queer knowledge of his enemies' psychology, had demurred from this reasoning; but as he had not insisted upon his point of view, Heavytree Farm and its occupants had been left to the care of the local police and the shaken Digby.

===Chinese===
众人本已议定：「老近卫军」进袭失利，眼下的气力又都使向拉思府那边，一时半刻不至于再折回赫维特里农场来。这本是米多斯的见解，莱昂与他的朋友所见亦同。唯有波伊卡特——那位大师级的战略家，凭着对仇敌心理的某种古怪了解，落子笃定——对这套推断不以为然；只是他既没有坚执己见，赫维特里农场和它的主人们，便交给了当地警察和惊魂未定的迪格比照看。

===Original===
Aunt Alma offered to give up her room to the wounded man, but he would not hear of this, and took the spare bedroom; an excellent position for a defender, since it separated Mirabelle's apartment from the pretty little room which Aunt Alma used as a study and sleeping-place.

===Chinese===
阿尔玛姨妈提出把自己的房间让给伤者，他却说什么也不肯，住进了那间备用卧室；于守宅之人，这是个极好的位置，因为它一头隔开米拉贝尔的套间，一头隔开阿尔玛姨妈兼作书房与卧房的那间漂亮小屋。

===Original===
The staff of Heavytree Farm consisted of an ancient cowman, a cook and a maid, the latter of whom had already given notice and left on the afternoon of the attack. She had, as she told Mirabelle in all seriousness, a weak heart.

===Chinese===
赫维特里农场的仆役，是一名上了年纪的牛倌、一个厨娘外加一个女仆；女仆早已递了辞呈，袭击那天下午便走了。她曾一本正经地告诉米拉贝尔，自己心脏不好。

===Original===
"And a weak head too!" snapped Alma. "I should not worry about your heart, my girl, if I were you."

"I was top of my class at school," bridled the maid, touched to the raw by this reflection upon her intelligence.

"It must have been a pretty small class," retorted Alma.

===Chinese===
「心脏不好，脑子也不怎么样！」阿尔玛厉声道。「我要是你，我的姑娘，才不为自己的心脏操心呢。」

「我在学校可是全班头一名。」女仆昂着头抢白，智力遭此非议，正戳在痛处。

「那班上的人数必定少得可怜。」阿尔玛反唇相讥。

===Original===
A new maid had been found, a girl who had been thrilled by the likelihood that the humdrum of daily labour would be relieved by exciting events out of the ordinary, and before evening the household had settled down to normality. Mirabelle was feeling the reaction and went to bed early that night, waking as the first slant of sunlight poured through her window. She got up, feeling, she told herself, as well as she had felt in her life. Pulling back the chintz curtains, she looked out upon a still world with a sense of happiness and relief beyond measure. There was nobody in sight. Pools of mist lay in the hollows, and from one white farmstead, far away on the slope of the hill, she saw the blue smoke was rising. It was a morning to remember, and, to catch its spirit the better, she dressed hastily and went down into the garden. As she walked along the path she heard a window pulled open and the bandaged head of Mr. Digby appeared.

===Chinese===
新女仆找着了，是个姑娘，一想到日复一日的乏味活计有望让出格的刺激事件来调剂，便兴奋得不行；不到傍晚，这一家已然各归常态。米拉贝尔只觉一阵后怕的倦意袭来，那晚早早上了床，一觉醒来，第一缕斜照的阳光正涌进她的窗子。她起了身，自觉平生从没有这样康健过。她拉开印花布窗帘，望向外头静悄悄的世界，心头涌起莫可名状的欢喜与宽慰。四下不见人影。洼地里积着一汪汪的雾，远远的山坡上，一座白色农舍正升起袅袅青烟。这是一个值得记住的早晨；为更好地领受它的神韵，她匆匆穿好衣裳，下楼进了园子。她沿小径走着，忽听得一扇窗被推开，迪格比先生缠着绷带的脑袋探了出来。

===Original===
"Oh, it's you, is it, miss?" he said with relief, and she laughed.

"There is nothing more terrible in sight than a big spider," she said, and pointed to a big flat fellow, who was already spinning his web between the tall hollyhocks. And the first of the bees was abroad.

===Chinese===
「哦，是您哪，小姐？」他松了口气说；她笑了。

「视野里再没有比一只大蜘蛛更可怕的了。」她说着，指了指一只又大又扁的家伙，它已经在高高的蜀葵之间织起了网。第一拨蜜蜂也出巢了。

===Original===
"If anybody had come last night I shouldn't have heard them," he confessed. "I slept like a dead man." He touched his head gingerly. "It smarts, but the ache is gone," he said, not loth to discuss his infirmities. "The doctor said I had a narrow escape; he thought there was a fracture. Would you like me to make you some tea, miss, or shall I call the servant?"

===Chinese===
「昨夜真要有人来，我也听不见。」他招认道。「我睡得跟死人似的。」他小心翼翼地碰了碰脑袋。「还火辣辣地有点痛，不过疼劲已经过去了。」他说着，倒不忌讳谈论自己的伤病。「医生说我死里逃生；他还当是骨折了呢。要不要我给您沏杯茶，小姐，还是去叫女仆？」

===Original===
She shook her head, but he had already disappeared, and came seeking her in the garden ten minutes later, with a cup of tea in his hand. He told her for the second time that he was a police pensioner and had been in the employ of Gonsalez for three years. The Three paid well, and had, she learned to her surprise, considerable private resources.

===Chinese===
她摇了摇头，可他早已没了人影，十分钟后端着一杯茶到园子里来寻她。他又说了一遍自己是个领警队退休金的人，受冈萨雷斯雇用已经三年。三义士出手阔绰，而且——她诧异地得知——还有相当可观的私财。

===Original===
"Does it pay them—this private detective business?"

"Lord bless your heart, no, miss!" He scoffed at the idea. "They are very rich men. I thought everybody knew that. They say Mr. Gonsalez was worth a million even before the war."

===Chinese===
「这私家侦探的营生——于他们有利可图么？」

「老天保佑，不赚的，小姐！」他对这念头嗤之以鼻。「他们都是富得很的人。我还当人人都知道呢。听说冈萨雷斯先生早在战前就有百万身家。」

===Original===
This was astonishing news.

"But why do they do this"—she hesitated—"this sort of thing?"

"It is a hobby, miss," said the man vaguely. "Some people run racehorses, some own yachts—these gentlemen get a lot of pleasure out of their work and they pay well," he added.

===Chinese===
这可是惊人的消息。

「可他们为什么要干这个」——她迟疑了一下——「干这种事呢？」

「那是桩癖好，小姐。」那人含含糊糊地说。「有人养赛马，有人置游艇——这几位先生从营生里得着许多乐趣，出手又大方。」他补了一句。

===Original===
Men in the regular employ of the Three Just Men not only received a good wage, but frequently a bonus which could only be described as colossal. Once, after they had rounded up and destroyed a gang of Spanish bank robbers, they had distributed £1,000 to every man who was actively employed. He hinted rather than stated that this money had formed part of the loot which the Three had recovered, and did not seem to think that there was anything improper in this distribution of illicit gains.

===Chinese===
长期受雇于三义士的人，不但薪水优厚，还常常领到一笔只能用「巨大」来形容的赏金。有一回，他们围剿歼灭了一伙西班牙银行劫匪，给每一个实际出力的人发了 £1,000。他倒没有明说，只暗示这笔钱原是三义士追回赃款的一部分；而对这笔不义之财的如此分派，他似乎并不觉得有什么不妥。

===Original===
"After all, miss," he said philosophically, "when you collect money like that, it's impossible to give it back to the people it came from. This Diego had been holding up banks for years, and banks are not like people—they don't feel the loss of money."

===Chinese===
「说到底，小姐，」他一副达观的口气，「那样的钱收缴到手，是没法归还原主的。那个迪亚戈抢银行抢了好些年，银行可不比活人——丢了钱也不觉得疼。」

===Original===
"That's a thoroughly immoral view," said Mirabelle, intent upon her flower-picking.

"It may be, miss," agreed Digby, who had evidently been one of the recipients of bounty, and took a complacent and a tolerant view. "But a thousand pounds is a lot of money."

===Chinese===
「这观点彻头彻尾不讲道德。」米拉贝尔说，只顾低头采她的花。

「兴许是吧，小姐。」迪格比应道——他分明领过赏，看法既自得又宽容。「可一千镑是很大一笔钱哪。」

===Original===
The day passed without event. From the early evening papers that came from Gloucester she learned of the fire at Oberzohn's, and did not connect the disaster with anything but an accident. She was not sorry. The fire had licked out one ugly chapter from the past. Incidentally it had destroyed a crude painting which was, to Dr. Oberzohn, more precious than any that Leonardo had painted or Raphael conceived, but this she did not know.

===Chinese===
这一天平安无事。她从格洛斯特送来的晚报早版上得知欧伯佐恩家失火，只当那场灾祸是桩意外，没往别处想。她并不难过。那场火舔去了过去丑恶的一章。顺带地，它还烧掉了一幅拙劣的画像——在欧伯佐恩博士眼里，它比达·芬奇画过的、拉斐尔构思过的一切都更珍贵——只是这一点她无从知晓。

===Original===
It was just before the dinner hour that there came the first unusual incident of the day. Mirabelle was standing by the garden gate, intent upon the glories of the evening sky, which was piled high with red and slate-coloured cumuli. The glass was falling and a wet night was promised. But the loveliness of that lavish colouring held her. And then she became dimly aware that a man was coming towards the house from the direction of Gloucester. He walked in the middle of the road slowly, as though he, too, were admiring the view and there was no need to hurry. His hands were behind him, his soft felt hat at the back of his head. A stocky-looking man, but his face was curiously familiar. He turned his unsmiling eyes in her direction, and, looking again at his strong features, at the tiny grey-black moustache under his aquiline nose, she was certain she had seen him before. Perhaps she had passed him in the street, and had retained a subconscious mental picture of him.

===Chinese===
恰在晚饭前，这一天头一桩不寻常的事来了。米拉贝尔立在院门边，正看晚空的壮丽出神：天上高高堆着红色与石板灰的积云。晴雨表的度数在落，预告着一个雨夜。可那泼洒开去的浓艳色彩之美把她攫住了。恰在此时，她隐约觉出有个男人从格洛斯特方向朝宅子走来。他在路正中慢慢踱着，仿佛也在赏景，用不着赶路。双手背在身后，软毡帽推在后脑勺上。是个敦实身量的汉子，那张脸却出奇地眼熟。他把不带笑意的眼睛转向她这边；她再度端详那刚毅的面容、鹰钩鼻下那一小撮灰黑胡子，确信自己从前见过他。也许她曾在街上与他擦肩而过，心里存下了一幅潜意识的画像。

===Original===
He slowed his step until, when he came abreast of her, he stopped.

"This is Heavytree Lane?" he asked, in a deep, musical voice.

"No—the lane is the first break in the hedge," she smiled. "I'm afraid it isn't much of a road—generally it is ankle-deep in mud."

===Chinese===
他放慢脚步，到与她并肩时，停了下来。

「这儿是赫维特里巷？」他问，嗓音低沉悦耳。

「不是——巷子是树篱上的头一个豁口。」她微笑道。「只怕那条道不成样子——平常泥浆没到脚踝深。」

===Original===
He looked past her to the house; his eyes ranged the windows, dropped for a moment upon a climbing clematis, and came back to her.

===Chinese===
他的目光越过她投向宅子；扫过一扇扇窗子，在一架攀缘的铁线莲上略略一停，又回到她身上。

===Original===
"I don't know Gloucestershire very well," he said, and added: "You have a very nice house."

"Yes," she said in surprise.

"And a garden." And then, innocently: "Do you grow onions?"

===Chinese===
「格洛斯特郡这一带我不大熟。」他说，又添一句：「您这宅子真好。」

「是啊。」她讶异地说。

「还有这园子。」接着，他一脸无辜地问：「你们种洋葱吗？」

===Original===
She stared at him and laughed.

"I think we do—I am not sure. My aunt looks after the kitchen garden."

===Chinese===
她瞪住他，随即笑了。

「我想是种的——说不准。菜园是我姨妈照管。」

===Original===
His sad eyes wandered over the house again.

"It is a very nice place," he said, and, lifting his hat, went on.

===Chinese===
他那双忧郁的眼睛又把宅子巡了一遍。

「这地方真好。」他说着，举了举帽子，自去了。

===Original===
Digby was out: he had gone for a gentle walk, and, looking up the road after the stranger, she saw the guard appear round a bend in the road, saw him stop and speak to the stranger. Apparently they knew one another, for they shook hands at meeting, and after a while Digby pointed down the road to where she was standing, and she saw the man nod. Soon after the stranger went on out of view. Who could he be? Was it an additional guard that the three men had put to protect her? When Digby came up to her, she asked him. "That gentleman, miss? He is Mr. Poiccart."

===Chinese===
迪格比不在家：他缓步散步去了。她顺着大路望那生人的背影，看见看守从路的拐弯处出现，看见他停下同生人说话。两人分明相识，见面还握了手；过了一会儿，迪格比朝她所站的那头大路一指，她看见那人点了点头。不多时生人便走出了视野。他能是谁呢？莫不是那三位先生为保护她加派的又一名看守？迪格比走到她跟前时，她便问他。「那位先生么，小姐？他是波伊卡特先生。」

===Original===
"Poiccart?" she said, delighted. "Oh, I wish I had known!"

"I was surprised to see him," said the guard. "As a matter of fact, he's the one of the three gentlemen I've met the most. He's generally in Curzon Street, even when the others are away."

===Chinese===
「波伊卡特？」她欢声道。「唉，早知道就好了！」

「见着他我也吃了一惊。」看守说。「说实在的，三位先生里头，就数我同他见面最多。哪怕另外两位都出门在外，他多半也守在柯曾街。」

===Original===
Digby had nothing to say about Poiccart except that he was a very quiet gentleman and took no active part in the operations of the Just Men.

"I wonder why he wanted to know about onions?" asked the girl thoughtfully. "That sounded awfully mysterious."

It would not have been so mysterious to Leon.

===Chinese===
关于波伊卡特，迪格比说不上别的，只说他是个极安静的绅士，从不出头参与义士们的行动。

「不知他打听洋葱做什么？」姑娘若有所思地问。「听着神秘得很。」

这话若让莱昂听见，就不至于这么神秘了。

===Original===
The house retired to bed soon after ten, Alma going the rounds, and examining the new bolts and locks which had been attached that morning to every door which gave ingress to the house.

Mirabelle was unaccountably tired, and was asleep almost as soon as her head touched the pillow.

===Chinese===
十点刚过，全宅便安歇了；阿尔玛挨屋巡查，逐一检视当天早晨给每扇进宅的门新装的插销与门锁。

米拉贝尔没来由地困乏，头一挨枕头便睡着了。

===Original===
She heard in her dreams the swish of the rain beating against her window, lay for a long time trying to energize herself to rise and shut the one open window where the curtains were blowing in. Then came a heavier patter against a closed pane, and something rattled on the floor of her room. She sat up. It could not be hail, although there was a rumble of thunder in the distance.

===Chinese===
她在睡梦里听见雨扫窗子的沙沙声，躺了好一阵，想攒起精神起身去关那扇敞着的、窗帘正往里灌风的窗。接着，一记更重的敲击落在关着的窗玻璃上，有什么东西弹落到她房里的地板上。她坐起身。那不可能是雹子，虽说远处正滚着闷雷。

===Original===
She got out of bed, pulled on her dressing-gown, went to the window, and had all her work to stifle a scream. Somebody was standing on the path below… a woman! She leaned out.

===Chinese===
她下了床，披上晨袍，走到窗前，费了好大的劲才把尖叫压了下去。下面的小径上立着一个人影……是个女人！她探出身去。

===Original===
"Who is it?" she asked.

"It is me—I—Joan!" There was a sob in the voice of the girl. Even in that light Mirabelle could see that the girl was drenched. "Don't wake anybody. Come down—I want you."

"What is wrong?" asked Mirabelle in a low voice.

"Everything… everything!"

===Chinese===
「是谁？」她问。

「是我——我——琼！」姑娘的嗓音里带着哭腔。即便借着那样的光，米拉贝尔也看得出她浑身湿透了。「别吵醒任何人。下来——我要你下来。」

「出了什么事？」米拉贝尔低声问。

「什么都完了……什么都完了！」

===Original===
She was on the verge of hysteria. Mirabelle lit a candle and crossed the room, went downstairs softly, so that Alma should not be disturbed. Putting the candle on the table, she unbarred and unbolted the door, opened it, and, as she did so, a man slipped through the half-opened door, his big hands smothering the scream that rose to her lips.

===Chinese===
她眼看就要发作歇斯底里。米拉贝尔点起一支蜡烛，穿过房间，放轻脚步下了楼，免得惊动阿尔玛。她把蜡烛搁在桌上，拉开门闩、退了插销，把门打开——就在这一开之间，一条汉子从半开的门缝里闪身挤入，两只大手死死捂住了她涌到嘴边的尖叫。

===Original===
Another man followed and, lifting the struggling girl, carried her into the drawing-room. One of the men took a small iron bottle from his pocket, to which ran a flexible rubber tube ending in a large red cap. Her captor removed his hands just as long as it took to fix the cap over her face. A tiny faucet was turned. Mirabelle felt a puff on her face, a strangely sweet taste, and then her heart began to beat thunderously. She thought she was dying, and writhed desperately to free herself.

===Chinese===
又一条汉子跟着进来，抱起拼命挣扎的姑娘，把她抬进了客厅。两人中有一个从口袋里掏出一只小铁瓶，瓶上接出一根软橡皮管，管子头上是一顶红色的大罩。擒她的人把手挪开，时间刚够把那罩子扣上她的脸。一个小小的旋塞被拧开了。米拉贝尔觉着脸上一股气扑来，一股奇异的甜味，跟着她的心便擂鼓般狂跳起来。她只道自己要死了，拼命扭动，想挣脱出去。

---
===Original===
"She's all right," said Monty Newton, lifting an eyelid for a second. "Get a blanket." He turned fiercely to the whimpering girl behind him. "Shut up, you!" he said savagely. "Do you want to rouse the whole house?"

A woebegone Joan was whimpering softly, tears running down her face, her hands clasping and unclasping in the agony of her mind.

===Chinese===
「她没事。」蒙蒂·牛顿说着，掀起她一边眼皮看了一瞬。「拿条毯子来。」他猛地转身，冲身后呜咽的姑娘发狠。「闭嘴，你！」他凶神恶煞地说。「你想把全宅的人都吵醒么？」

琼一脸愁苦，低低地呜咽着，泪水淌了满脸，一双手在揪心的痛楚里攥紧又松开。

===Original===
"You told me you weren't going to hurt her!" she sobbed.

"Get out," he hissed, and pointed to the door. She went meekly.

===Chinese===
「你说过不会伤害她的！」她抽泣道。

「滚出去。」他嘶声说，指着门。她乖乖地走了。

===Original===
A heavy blanket was wrapped round the unconscious girl, and, lifting her between them, the two men went out into the rain, where the old trolley was waiting, and slid her along the straw-covered floor. In another second the trolley moved off, gathering speed.

===Chinese===
一条厚毯裹住不省人事的姑娘，两个汉子把她一抬，冒雨走了出去——那辆旧篷车正候在外头——顺着铺了稻草的车厢底板把她塞了进去。转瞬间篷车开动，越走越快。

===Original===
By this time the effect of the gas had worn off and Mirabelle had regained consciousness. She put out a hand and touched a woman's knee. "Who is that—Alma?"

"No," said a miserable voice, "it's Joan."

===Chinese===
这时药气的效力已经散去，米拉贝尔恢复了知觉。她伸出一只手，碰到一个女人的膝盖。「是谁——阿尔玛？」

「不是，」一个凄惶的声音说，「是琼。」

===Original===
"Joan? Oh, yes, of course… why did you do it?—how wicked!"

"Shut up!" Monty snarled. "Wait until you get to—where you're going, before you start these 'whys' and 'wherefores.'"

===Chinese===
「琼？哦，是啊，当然了……你为什么这么干？——多歹毒啊！」

「闭嘴！」蒙蒂咆哮道。「等到了——你要去的地方，再来发这些『为什么』『凭什么』。」

===Original===
Mirabelle was deathly sick and bemused, and for the next hour she was too ill to feel even alarmed. Her head was going round and round, and ached terribly, and the jolting of the truck did not improve matters in this respect.

===Chinese===
米拉贝尔难受得要死，脑子昏昏沉沉；此后一个钟头，她病恹恹的，连害怕都顾不上。她的头天旋地转，疼得厉害，篷车一路的颠簸在这上头更是雪上加霜。

===Original===
Monty, who was sitting with his back to the truck's side, was smoking. He cursed now and then, as some unusually heavy jolt flung him forward. They passed through the heart of the storm: the flicker of lightning was almost incessant and the thunder was deafening. Rain was streaming down the hood of the trolley, rendering it like a drum.

===Chinese===
蒙蒂背靠车厢板坐着，抽着烟。偶尔一下格外重的颠簸把他掀得朝前一扑，他便骂上一句。他们从风暴中心穿行而过：闪电几乎接连不断，雷声震耳欲聋。雨水顺着篷车的车篷往下直淌，把它敲打得像一面鼓。

===Original===
Mirabelle fell into a little sleep and woke feeling better. It was still dark, and she would not have known the direction they were taking, only the driver took the wrong turning coming through a country town, and by the help of the lightning she saw what was indubitably the stand of a racetrack, and a little later saw the word "Newbury." They were going towards London, she realized.

===Chinese===
米拉贝尔小小睡了一觉，醒来觉得好些了。天还黑着，她本无从辨认他们走的方向，只因司机在一座乡间市镇里拐错了弯，借着闪电，她看清了那分明是一座赛马场的看台，过了一会儿又望见「纽伯里」的字样。他们这是往伦敦去，她明白了。

===Original===
At this hour of the morning there was little or no traffic, and when they turned on to the new Great West Road a big car went whizzing past at seventy miles an hour and the roar of it woke the girl. Now she could feel the trolley wheels skidding on tramlines. Lights appeared with greater frequency. She saw a store window brilliantly illuminated, the night watchman having evidently forgotten to turn off the lights at the appointed hour.

===Chinese===
清早这个钟点，路上几乎没有来往车辆。他们拐上新修的大西路（Great West Road）时，一辆大轿车以时速七十英里呼啸而过，那轰鸣把姑娘惊醒了。此刻她能觉出篷车轮子在电车轨道上打滑。灯火出现得越来越密。她看见一家店铺的橱窗灯火通明——守夜人显然忘了在钟点上熄灯。

===Original===
Soon they were crossing the Thames. She saw the red and green lights of a tug, and black upon near black a string of barges in midstream. She dozed again and was jerked wide awake when the trolley swayed and skidded over a surface more uneven than any. Once its wheels went into a pothole and she was flung violently against the side. Another time it skidded and was brought up with a crash against some obstacle. The bumping grew more gentle, and then the machine stopped, and Monty jumped down and called to her sharply.

===Chinese===
不久他们便驶过泰晤士河。她看见一艘拖船的红灯绿灯，看见河心一长串驳船，黑影叠着近乎黑的夜色。她又打起盹来，篷车猛地一晃、在一截比哪段都坎坷的路面上打了滑，把她惊得完全醒了。有一回车轮陷进坑洼，她被狠狠抛向车帮。又有一回车打滑，砰地撞上什么障碍才停住。颠簸渐渐轻了，随后车停了下来，蒙蒂跳下车，尖声唤她。

===Original===
Her head was clear now, despite its throbbing. She saw a queer-shaped house, all gables and turrets, extraordinarily narrow for its height. It seemed to stand in the middle of a field. And yet it was in London: she could see the glow of furnace fires and hear the deep boom of a ship's siren as it made its way down the river on the tide.

===Chinese===
她的脑袋此刻是清楚的，尽管还突突地跳疼。她看见一座形状古怪的房子，山墙尖塔一应俱全，以它的高度论却窄得出奇。它仿佛立在一片田野正中。然而这却是在伦敦：她望得见熔炉火光的红晕，听得见一艘海轮趁着潮水顺河而下时低沉悠长的汽笛。

===Original===
She had not time to take observations, for Monty fastened to her arm and she squelched through the mud up a flight of stone steps into a dimly lit hall. She had a confused idea that she had seen little dogs standing on the side of the steps, and a big bird with a long bill, but these probably belonged to the smoke of dreams which the gas had left.

===Chinese===
她来不及细看，蒙蒂已一把攥住她的胳膊，她噗唧噗唧踩着烂泥，登上一段石阶，进了灯光昏暗的门厅。她恍恍惚惚觉得看见台阶两旁立着一只只小狗，还有一只长喙的大鸟；不过这些多半是那药气留下的梦魅余烟。

===Original===
Monty opened a door and pushed her in before him, and she stared into the face of Dr. Oberzohn.

===Chinese===
蒙蒂推开门，把她搡在自己前头进了屋，她一抬眼，正对着欧伯佐恩博士的脸。

===Original===
He wore a black velvet dressing-gown that had once been a regal garment but was now greasy and stained. On his egg-shaped head he had an embroidered smoking-cap. His feet were encased in warm velvet slippers. He put down the book he had been reading, rubbed his glasses on one velvet sleeve, and then: "So!" he said.

===Chinese===
他身上一件黑丝绒晨袍，本曾是王者气象的衣裳，如今已油腻污渍斑斑。蛋形的头上扣一顶绣花吸烟帽。脚上裹着保暖的天鹅绒拖鞋。他放下正读着的书，在一只丝绒袖子上擦了擦眼镜，然后——「来了！」他说。

===Original===
He pointed to the remains of a fire.

"Sit down, Mirabelle Leicester, and warm yourself. You have come quickly, my friend,"—he addressed Monty.

===Chinese===
他指了指炉膛里的余烬。

「坐下，米拉贝尔·莱斯特，暖一暖身子。你来得很快，我的朋友。」——他这话是对蒙蒂说的。

===Original===
"I'm black and blue all over," growled Newton. "Why couldn't we have a car?"

"Because the cars were engaged, as I told you."

===Chinese===
「我浑身都摔得青一块紫一块。」牛顿咕哝道。「为什么就不能给我们一辆汽车？」

「因为汽车都有差事在身，这是我告诉过你的。」

===Original===
"Did you—" began Newton quickly, but the old man glanced significantly at the girl, shivering before the fire and warming her hands mechanically.

"I will answer, but you need not ask, in good time. This is not of all moments the most propitious. Where is your woman?"

===Chinese===
「那你可——」牛顿急急开口，老人却意味深长地朝姑娘瞥了一眼——她正抖着立在炉前，机械地搓着两只手取暖。

「我会答复的，但你不必问——时候到了自会明白。此刻不是一切时机之中最相宜的一个。你的女人在哪里？」

===Original===
He had forgotten Joan, and went out to find her shivering in the passage.

"Do you want her?" he asked, poking his head in the door.

"She shall go with this girl. You will explain."

===Chinese===
他倒把琼忘了，出去找她，只见她在过道里瑟瑟发抖。

「你要她吗？」他把脑袋探进门来问。

「她要同这位姑娘住在一处。你来对她说明。」

===Original===
"Where are you going to put her?"

Oberzohn pointed to the floor.

"Here? But—"

===Chinese===
「你打算把她安置在哪儿？」

欧伯佐恩朝地板指了指。

「就在这儿？可是——」

===Original===
"No, no. My friend, you are too quick to see what is not meant. The gracious lady shall live in a palace—I have a certain friend who will no longer need it."

===Chinese===
「不，不。我的朋友，你太快了，看见了并非此意的东西。这位雍容的小姐要住进一座宫殿——我有那么一位朋友，往后用不着它了。」

===Original===
His face twitched in the nearest he ever approached to a smile. Groping under the table, he produced a pair of muddy Wellingtons, kicked off his slippers and pulled on the boots with many gasps and jerks.

"All that they need is there: I have seen to it. March!"

===Chinese===
他的脸抽搐了一下，那已是他最接近微笑的样子。他在桌下摸索一气，掏出一双沾满泥的威灵顿长靴，蹬掉拖鞋，喘着粗气、一顿一顿地把靴子拽上。

「她们所需的一切都在那下面：我已亲自安排妥当。开步走！」

===Original===
He led the way out of the room, pulling the girl to her feet, and Newton followed, Joan bringing up the rear. Inside the factory, Oberzohn produced a small hand torch from his pocket and guided them through the debris till he came to that part of the floor where the trap was. With his foot he moved the covering of rubbish, pulled up the trap and went down.

===Chinese===
他在前头引路出屋，把姑娘拽了起来，牛顿跟在后面，琼殿后。进了厂房，欧伯佐恩从口袋里摸出一支小手电，领他们穿过满地残砾，走到地板上安着活板门的那一块。他用脚拨开盖着的垃圾，掀起活板门，下去了。

===Original===
"I can't go down there, Monty, I can't!" said Joan's agitated voice. "What are you going to do with us? My God! if I'd known—"

"Don't be a fool," said Newton roughly. "What have you got to be afraid of? There's nothing here. We want you to look after her for a day or two. You don't want her to go down by herself: she'd be frightened to death."

===Chinese===
「我不能下那儿去，蒙蒂，我不能！」琼的嗓音慌乱已极。「你们要拿我们怎么样？我的天哪！我要是早知道——」

「别犯傻。」牛顿粗声粗气地说。「你有什么可怕的？这儿什么也没有。我们要你照看她一两天。你总不想叫她独自下去吧：她会吓死的。」

===Original===
Her teeth chattering, Joan stumbled down the steps behind him. Certainly the first view of her new quarters was reassuring. Two little trestle beds had been made; the underground room had been swept clean, and a new carpet laid on the floor. Moreover, the apartment was brilliantly lit, and a furnace gave almost an uncomfortable warmth which was nevertheless very welcome, for the temperature had dropped 20° since noon.

===Chinese===
琼牙齿打着战，磕磕绊绊跟在他身后下了台阶。头一眼望见新居，确实叫人安心。两张小小的支架床已经支好；地下这间屋子扫得干干净净，地板上铺了新地毯。再说，屋里灯火通明，一只炉子烘出几乎有些过分的暖意——却也正是求之不得，因为气温自正午以来已跌了 20°。

===Original===
"In this box there are clothes of all varieties, and expensive to purchase," said Oberzohn, pointing to a brand-new trunk at the foot of one of the beds. "Food you will have in plenty—bread and milk newly every day. By night you shall keep the curtain over the ventilator." On the wall was a small black curtain about ten inches square.

===Chinese===
「这口箱子里有各色衣裳，皆是高价购得。」欧伯佐恩说着，指了指一张床脚那口崭新的衣箱。「食物你们有的是——面包和牛奶，每天新鲜送到。入夜之后，要把通风口上的帘子拉严。」墙上挂着一方黑帘，约十英寸见方。

===Original===
Monty took her into the next apartment and showed her the wash-place. There was even a bath, a compulsory fixture under the English Factory Act in a store of this description, where, in the old days, men had to handle certain insanitary products of the Coast.

===Chinese===
蒙蒂带她进隔壁一间，指给她看盥洗的地方。那里居然还有一只浴缸——按英国《工厂法》，这样一座货栈里必备的设置；想当年，工人们就得在这儿经手「海岸」（西非沿岸旧称）运来的几样不洁土产。

===Original===
"But how do we get out, Monty? Where do we get exercise?"

"You'll come out tomorrow night: I'll see to that," he said, dropping his voice. "Now listen, Joan: you've got to be a sensible girl and help me. There's money in this—bigger money than you have ever dreamed of. And when we've got this unpleasant business over, I'm taking you away for a trip round the world."

===Chinese===
「可我们怎么出去呢，蒙蒂？上哪儿活动身子？」

「明晚就放你们出来：这个我自有安排。」他压低嗓音说。「现在听着，琼：你要做个懂事的姑娘，帮我的忙。这里头有钱——比你想过的哪一笔都大。等这桩讨厌的勾当一了，我带你出门，环游世界去。」

===Original===
It was the old promise, given before, never fulfilled, always hoped for. But this time it did not wholly remove her uneasiness.

"But what are you going to do with the girl?" she asked.
