这一回，欧伯佐恩到来的动静再不会叫人弄错了。活板门砰的一声掀起，米拉贝尔心口怦怦急跳，听着脚步声顺着石梯下来。是两个人，走得沉重。不知怎的，还没见着他们扛的人，她已知道那是琼。琼穿着晚礼服，脸白得像粉笔，双眼紧闭；眼看他们把她放上床，姑娘还当她已经死了。

===Original===

"You have given her too much, Gurther," said Oberzohn.

Gurther? She had not recognized him. It was almost impossible to believe that this was the dapper young man who had danced with her at the Arts Ball.

"I had to guess in the dark, Herr Doktor," said Gurther.

===Chinese===

「你给她下得太多了，格瑟。」欧伯佐恩说。

格瑟？她方才竟没认出他来。这就是在艺术家舞会上同她跳过舞的那个衣冠楚楚的年轻人——简直叫人无法相信。

「黑灯瞎火，我只好估摸着下，博士先生。」格瑟说。

===Original===

They were talking in German, and Mirabelle's acquaintance with that language was very slight. She saw Gurther produce a small flat case from his pocket, take out a little phial, and shake into the palm of his hand a small brown capsule. This he dissolved in a tiny tube which, with the water he used, was also extracted from the case. Half filling a minute syringe, he sent the needle into Joan's arm. A pause, and then: "Soon she will wake, with your kind permission, Herr Doktor," said Gurther.

===Chinese===

两人说的是德语，而米拉贝尔对那种语言的学问浅得很。她看见格瑟从口袋里掏出一只扁扁的小匣，取出一小瓶药，往手心里倒出一粒棕色的小胶囊。他把胶囊溶进一支细小的管子——他所用的水，也是从那只匣子里取出来的。往一支极小的注射器里灌了半管，他把针头扎进了琼的胳膊。顿了一顿，随后道：「很快她就会醒来——若蒙您恩准，博士先生。」格瑟说。

===Original===

Mirabelle was not looking at him, but she knew that his hot eyes were fixed on her, that all the time except the second he was operating, he was looking at her; and now she knew that this was the man to be feared. A cold hand seemed to grip at her heart.

===Chinese===

米拉贝尔没有朝他看，却知道他那双火辣辣的眼睛正死死盯在她身上；除了动针的那一小会儿，他自始至终都在看她。此刻她明白了：真正要怕的正是这个人。一只冰冷的手仿佛攥住了她的心。

===Original===

"That will do, Gurther." Oberzohn's voice was sharp. He, too, had interpreted the stare. "You need not wait."

===Chinese===

「够了，格瑟。」欧伯佐恩的嗓音尖利起来。他也读出了那一眼的意味。「你不必在这儿候着。」

===Original===

Gurther obediently stalked from the room, and the doctor followed. Almost before the trap had fastened down she was by the girl's side, with a basin of water and a wet towel. The second the water touched her face, Joan opened her eyes and gazed wildly up at the vaulted ceiling, then rolling over from the bed to her knees, she struggled to her feet, swayed and would have fallen, had not Mirabelle steadied her.

===Chinese===

格瑟服服帖帖地大步出了屋，博士跟在后面。活板门几乎才落定，她已端着一盆水、一条湿毛巾到了姑娘身边。水一碰到琼的脸，琼便睁开眼，狂乱地仰望着那拱顶，随即从床上翻身跪倒，挣扎着爬起身，摇晃了一下，若不是米拉贝尔扶住，几乎跌倒。

===Original===

"They've got him! They've got my boy… killed him like a dog!"

"What—Mr.—Mr. Newton?" gasped Mirabelle, horrified.

"Killed him—Monty—Monty!"

===Chinese===

「他们逮住他了！他们逮住我的小伙子了……把他像条狗一样宰了！」

「什么——牛——牛顿先生？」米拉贝尔倒抽一口冷气，惊骇万分。

「宰了他——蒙蒂——蒙蒂！」

===Original===

And then she began to scream and run up and down the room like a thing demented. Mirabelle, sick at heart, almost physically sick at the sight, caught her and tried to calm her, but she was distracted, half mad. The drug and its antidote seemed to have combined to take away the last vestige of restraint. It was not until she fell, exhausted, that Mirabelle was able to drag her again to the bed and lay her upon it.

===Chinese===

接着她便尖叫起来，在屋里跑来跑去，像疯了的一般。米拉贝尔心里发苦，眼看这光景几乎真要呕出来，一把抱住她，想让她安静，可她早已神魂全散、半近疯狂。麻醉药和解药像是合起伙来，把她最后一丝自持都夺了个干净。直到她力竭倒地，米拉贝尔才得以把她重新拖上床去，放平在床上。

===Original===

Montague Newton was dead! Who had killed him? Who were the "they"? Then she thought of Gurther in his strange attire; white dress-front crumpled, even his beard disarranged in the struggle he had had with the overwrought woman.

===Chinese===

蒙塔古·牛顿死了！是谁杀了他？那个「他们」又是些什么人？随即她想起了格瑟那身古怪的装束：白礼服的前襟皱作一团，连他那部胡子，也在同那个情绪失控的女人扭打时弄乱了。

===Original===

In sheer desperation she ran up the steps and tried the trap, but it was fast. She must get away from here—must get away at once. Joan was moaning pitiably, and the girl sat by her side, striving to calm her. She seemed to have passed into a state of semiconsciousness; except for her sobs, she made no sound and uttered no intelligible word. Half an hour passed—the longest and most dreadful half-hour in Mirabelle Leicester's life. And then she heard a sound. It had penetrated even to the brain of this half-mad girl, for she opened her eyes wide, and, gripping Mirabelle, drew herself up.

===Chinese===

绝望之极，她奔上台阶去扳那活板门，门却闩得死死的。她必须离开这里——必须立刻离开。琼在可怜巴巴地呻吟，姑娘坐在她身边，竭力安抚。琼像是已坠入半昏迷的境地；除了呜咽，不作一声，也吐不出一个成句的字眼。半个钟头过去了——这是米拉贝尔·莱斯特一生中最漫长、最可怕的半个钟头。随后她听见了一个声响。那声响竟穿透了这半疯姑娘的脑子，只见她睁大双眼，一把攥住米拉贝尔，撑起了身子。

===Original===

"He's coming," she said, white to the lips, "coming… the Killer is coming!"

"For God's sake don't talk like that!" said Mirabelle, beside herself with fear.

===Chinese===

「他来了。」她说，连嘴唇都白了，「来了……杀人凶手来了！」

「看在上帝分上，别这么说话！」米拉贝尔说，怕得魂不附体。

===Original===

There it was, in the outer room; a stealthy shuffle of feet. She stared at the closed door, and the strain of the suspense almost made her faint. And then she saw the steel door move, slowly, and first a hand came through, the edge of a face… Gurther was leering at her. His beard was gone, and his wig; he was collarless, and had over his white shirt the stained jacket that was his everyday wear.

===Chinese===

来了，就在外间：一阵蹑手蹑脚的趿行声。她死死盯着那扇关着的门，悬念绷得她几乎要晕过去。随后她看见钢门动了，慢慢地，先探进来一只手，又露出小半张脸……格瑟正斜着眼不怀好意地瞅着她。他的胡子没了，假发也没了；他没戴领子，白衬衫外头罩着那件沾满污渍的日常外套。

===Original===

"I want you." He was talking to Mirabelle. Her tongue clave to the roof of her mouth, but she did not speak.

"My pretty little lady—" he began, and then, with a shriek, Joan leapt at him.

===Chinese===

「我要你。」他是对米拉贝尔说的。她的舌头死死粘住了上膛，可她一声也不出。

「我的漂亮小姑娘——」他刚开了个头，琼便尖叫一声朝他扑了过去。

===Original===

"Murderer, murderer…! Beast!" she cried, striking wildly at his face. With a curse, he tried to throw her off, but she was clinging to him; a bestial lunatic thing, hardly human.

===Chinese===

「杀人凶手，杀人凶手……！畜生！」她喊道，没头没脑地朝他脸上乱打。他咒骂一声想把她甩脱，她却死死缠住他；一头野兽般癫狂的东西，几乎不复是人。

===Original===

He flung her aside at last, and then he put up his hand to guard his face as she leapt at him again. This time she went under his arm and was through the door in a flash. He heard the swift patter of her feet on the stairs, and turned in pursuit. The trap was open. He stumbled and tripped in the dark across the floor of the gaunt factory. Just as she reached the open, he grabbed at her and missed. Like a deer she sped, but he was fleeter-footed behind her; and suddenly his hand closed about her throat.

===Chinese===

他终于把她甩脱到一旁；待她再度扑来，他举起手护住脸。这一回她从他臂下钻过，一眨眼便穿门而出。他听见她的脚在楼梯上急促地啪嗒作响，转身便追。活板门开着。他在黑暗里跌跌撞撞，一路磕磕绊绊穿过那座阴森空荡的厂房。她刚跑近出口，他一把抓去，抓了个空。她逃得像头母鹿，可他脚程更疾，紧追在她身后；突然，他的手扼住了她的咽喉。

===Original===

"You had better go out, my friend," he said, and tightened his grip.

===Chinese===

「你最好还是出去，我的朋友。」他说着，收紧了手掌。

===Original===

As she twisted to avoid him, he put out his foot. There was a grating snap, something gripped his legs, and the excruciating pain of it was agonizing. He loosened his hold of her throat, but held her arm tightly. With all his strength he threw her against the wall and she fell in a heap. Then, leaning down, he forced apart the cruel jagged teeth of the mantrap on to which he had put his foot, and drew his leg clear. He was bleeding; his trouser leg was torn to ribbons. He stopped only long enough to drag the girl to her feet, and, throwing her across his shoulder as though she were a sack, he went back into the factory, down the stairs, and threw her on to the bed with such violence that the spring supports broke. It had a strange effect upon the dazed woman, but this he did not see, for he had turned to Mirabelle.

===Chinese===

她扭身躲他，他伸脚去绊。只听咔啦一声脆响，什么东西钳住了他的腿，那剧痛真是痛彻骨髓。他松开扼着她咽喉的手，却把她那条胳膊攥得死紧。他使出全身的气力把她朝墙上掼去，她瘫作一堆倒下。随后他俯下身，掰开捕人铁夹那残忍的锯齿钢牙——他一脚踏进的正是这东西——把腿抽了出来。他流血了；一条裤腿撕成了碎条。他只停了把姑娘拽起来的那点工夫，便像扛一袋货似的把她甩上肩头，回到厂房里，下了楼梯，把她往床上狠狠一掼，掼得连床的弹簧支架都断了。这一下在那神志昏沉的女人身上生出一种奇异的效果，可他没有看见，因为他已经转向米拉贝尔。

===Original===

"My little lady, I want you!" he breathed.

Blood was trickling down from his wounded calf, but he did not feel the pain any more; felt nothing, save the desire to hurt those who hunted him; wanted nothing but the materialization of crude and horrid dreams.

===Chinese===

「我的小姑娘，我要你！」他喘着气说。

血正从他受伤的小腿上涔涔流下，可他再也不觉得痛了；什么知觉都没有了，只剩下伤害那些追猎他的人的欲望；什么都不要了，只求把那粗野可怖的乱梦化作现实。

===Original===

She stood, frozen, paralysed, incapable of movement. And then his hand came under her chin and he lifted her face; and she saw the bright, hungry eyes devouring her, saw the thin lips come closer and closer, could not move; had lost all sentient impressions, and could only stare into the eyes of this man-snake, hypnotized by the horror of the moment.

===Chinese===

她呆立着，冻住了，瘫了，动弹不得。随后他的手托上她的下巴，抬起她的脸；她看见那双亮晶晶、饿狠狠的眼睛在吞噬她，看见那两片薄唇越逼越近，却动弹不得；一切知觉的印象都已散尽，只能直直望进这条人形毒蛇的眼睛，被眼前这一刻的恐怖慑住了魂。

===Original===

And then a raging fury descended upon him. Narrow fingers tore at his face, almost blinding him. He turned with a howl of rage, but the white-faced Joan had flown to the furnace and taken up a short iron bar that had been used to rake the burning coals together. She struck at him and missed. He dodged past her and she flung the bar at him, and again missed him. The iron struck the green box, behind the furnace, there was a sound of smashing glass. He did not notice this, intent only upon the girl, and Mirabelle closed her eyes and heard only the blow as he struck her.

===Chinese===

接着，一腔暴怒朝他当头罩下。瘦窄的手指抓向他的脸，几乎抓瞎了他的眼。他狂吼一声转过身，可那脸色惨白的琼早已飞奔到炉子跟前，抄起一根短铁钎——那原是用来拢煤的。她朝他打去，打空了。他从她身边闪过，她把铁钎朝他掷去，又没掷中。铁钎砸在炉子后头那只绿呢箱子上，响起一片玻璃碎裂之声。他没有留意这个，一门心思只在姑娘身上；米拉贝尔闭上眼睛，只听见他打她的那一记声响。

===Original===

When she looked again, Joan was lying on the bed and he was tying one of her hands to the bed-rail with a strap which he had taken from his waist. Then Mirabelle saw a sight that released her pent speech. He heard her scream and grinned round at her… saw where she was looking and looked too.

===Chinese===

她再看时，琼已躺在床上，他正用一条从腰间解下的皮带，把她的一只手往床栏上捆。随后米拉贝尔看见了一幅光景，那光景一下子冲开了她紧闭的喉咙。他听得她的尖叫，回头朝她咧嘴一笑……顺着她目光所注，自己也望了过去。

===Original===

Something was coming from the broken green box! A black, spade-shaped head, with bright, hard eyes that seemed to survey the scene in a malignant stare. And then, inch by inch, a thick shining thing, like a rubber rope, wriggled slowly to the floor, coiled about upon itself, and raised its flat head. "Oh, God, look!"

===Chinese===

有什么东西正从砸破的绿呢箱子里往外爬！一颗黑色的、铲形的头，一双又亮又硬的眼睛，仿佛正用恶毒的一瞥检视着眼前这一幕。随后，一寸又一寸，一条粗大发亮的东西，像一根橡皮绳，慢慢蠕动到地板上，盘作一团，昂起了它那扁平的头。「哦，上帝，快看！」

===Original===

He turned about at the sight, that immovable grin of his upon his face, and said something in a guttural tongue. The snake was motionless, its baleful gaze first upon the sinking girl, then upon the man.

===Chinese===

见了这光景他转过身来，脸上仍挂着那抹纹丝不动的狞笑，用一种喉音浓重的语言说了句什么。那蛇一动不动，恶毒的目光先落在那个渐渐瘫软的姑娘身上，又落到那男人身上。

===Original===

Gurther's surprise was tragic; it was as though he had been confronted with some apparition from another world. And then his hand went to his hip pocket; there was a flash of light and a deafening explosion that stunned her. The pistol dropped from his hand and fell with a clatter to the floor, and she saw his arm was stiffly extended, and protruding from the cuff of his coat a black tail that wound round and round his wrist. It had struck up his sleeve. The cloth about his biceps was bumping up and down erratically.

===Chinese===

格瑟的惊愕近乎悲怆；那模样，仿佛他当面撞上了另一个世界来的鬼影。随后他的手伸向裤后袋；一道火光，一声震聋耳朵的爆响，把她惊呆了。手枪从他手里脱落，哐啷一声掉在地板上；她看见他的手臂僵直地伸着，外套袖口里探出一条黑尾巴，一圈又一圈地缠在他手腕上。它方才朝上蹿进了他的袖管。他上臂一带的布料正没个章法地一鼓一鼓地动着。

===Original===

He stood straightly erect, grinning, the arm still outflung, his astonished eyes upon the coil about his wrist. And then, slowly his other hand came round, gripped the tail and pulled it savagely forth. The snake turned with an angry hiss and tried to bite back at him; but raising his hand, he brought the head crashing down against the furnace. There was a convulsive wriggle as the reptile fell among the ashes.

===Chinese===

他直挺挺地立着，咧着嘴笑，那条胳膊仍然直伸着，惊愕的两眼盯着腕上盘着的那一圈。随后，他的另一只手慢慢地绕了过来，攫住蛇尾，狠狠地往外一拽。那蛇愤怒地嘶了一声回头反咬；他却把手往上一扬，将蛇头朝炉身上猛地一磕。那爬虫跌进灰烬里，痉挛般地扭动了一下。

===Original===

"*Gott in himmel!*" whispered Gurther, and his free hand went up to his arm and felt gingerly. "He is dead, gracious lady. Perhaps there is another?"

===Chinese===

「*Gott in himmel*！（德语：上帝在天）」格瑟低声说，那只闲着的手抬起来，小心翼翼地摸向自己的胳膊。「它死了，雍容的小姐。说不定还有一条？」

===Original===

He went, swaying as he walked, to the green box, and put in his hand without hesitation. There was another—a bigger snake, roused from its sleep and angry. He bit twice at the man's wrist, but Gurther laughed, a gurgling laugh of pure enjoyment. For already he was a dead man; that he knew. And it had come to him, at the moment and second of his dissolution, when the dread gates of judgment were already ajar, that he should go to his Maker with this clean space in the smudge of his life.

===Chinese===

他走了过去，一路摇摇晃晃，走到绿呢箱子跟前，毫不迟疑地把手伸了进去。里头果真还有一条——更大的一条蛇，被从睡梦里搅醒，怒气冲冲。它朝那男人的手腕连咬了两口，格瑟却笑了，咯咯地笑，笑得纯粹心花怒放。因为他已经是个死人了；这一点他自己清楚。而恰恰在他行将消散的这一刻、这一秒，当那可畏的审判之门已然半开之际，一个念头降临到他心头：他该带着他一生污迹之中这一小块干净的地方，去见他的造物主。

===Original===

"Go, little one," he said, grinning into the spade-face. "You have no more poison; that is finished!"

===Chinese===

「去吧，小家伙。」他说着，朝那张铲形脸咧嘴一笑。「你再也没有毒了；那玩意儿完蛋了！」

===Original===

He put the writhing head under his heel, and Mirabelle shut her eyes and put her hands to her ears. When she looked again, the man was standing by the door, clinging to the post and slipping with every frantic effort to keep himself erect. He grinned at her again; this man of murder, who had made his last kill.

===Chinese===

他把那颗犹自扭动的蛇头踩进脚跟底下；米拉贝尔闭上眼睛，双手捂住了耳朵。待她再看时，那人已站在门边，死死攀着门柱，每一次发狂似的挣扎着挺直身子，都在朝下溜。他又朝她咧嘴一笑；这个满手血腥的人，杀完了他最后一回。

===Original===

"Pardon, gracious lady," he said thickly, and went down on his knees, his head against the door, his body swaying slowly from side to side, and finally tumbled over.

===Chinese===

「告罪了，雍容的小姐。」他含混不清地说，双膝跪了下去，头抵着门，身子缓缓地左右摇晃，终于一头栽倒在地。

===Original===

She heard Oberzohn's harsh voice from the floor above. He was calling Gurther, and presently he appeared in the doorway, and there was a pistol in his hand.

"So!" he said, looking down at the dying man.

And then he saw the snake, and his face wrinkled. He looked from Mirabelle to the girl on the bed, went over and examined her, but did not attempt to release the strap. It was Mirabelle who did that; Mirabelle who sponged the bruised face and loosened the dress.

===Chinese===

她听见头顶那层楼上传来欧伯佐恩粗厉的嗓音。他在喊格瑟；不多时，他自己出现在门口，手里握着一支手枪。

「原来如此！」他低头望着那垂死的人说。

随后他看见了那条蛇，脸皱作一团。他的目光从米拉贝尔移到床上的姑娘身上，走过去查验了一番，却不动手去解那条皮带。解皮带的，是米拉贝尔；为那张青肿的脸擦拭、把衣裳松开的，也是米拉贝尔。

===Original===

So doing, she felt a hand on her shoulder.

"Come," said Oberzohn.

"I'm staying here with Joan, until—"

"You come at once, or I will give you to my pretty little friends." He pointed to the two snakes on the floor who still moved spasmodically.

===Chinese===

正忙着这些，她觉出一只手落在了自己肩上。

「来。」欧伯佐恩说。

「我要留在这儿陪着琼，等到——」

「你立刻过来，不然我就把你交给我的漂亮小朋友们。」他指着地板上那两条还在痉挛抽动的蛇。

===Original===

She had to step past Gurther, but that seemed easier than passing those wriggling, shining black ropes; and, her hand in his, she stumbled up the dark steps and eventually into the clean, sweet air of the night.

===Chinese===

她不得不从格瑟身边跨过去，可这比起走过那两条蠕动发亮的黑绳来，倒显得容易些；她把一只手交在他手里，跌跌撞撞地登上黑黢黢的台阶，终于走进了夜里那洁净、清甜的空气里。

===Original===

He was dressed for a journey; she had noticed that when he appeared. A heavy cloth cap was on his curious-shaped head, and he looked less repulsive with so much of his forehead hidden. Though the night was warm, he wore an overcoat.

===Chinese===

他一身出远门的装束；他露面时她就注意到了。那颗形状古怪的脑袋上扣着一顶厚实的布帽，前额遮去了大半，他那副嘴脸看上去倒不那么可憎了。夜里虽暖，他却裹着一件大衣。

===Original===

They were passing between the wall and the factory when he stopped and put his hand before her mouth. He had heard voices, low voices on the other side of the wall, and presently the scrape of something. Without removing his hand from her face, he half dragged, half pushed her until they were clear of the factory.

===Chinese===

两人正打高墙与厂房之间经过，他忽然停住，一只手捂上了她的嘴。他听见了人声——墙那头低低的人声，不多时又有什么东西刮擦了一响。他没有把那只手从她脸上挪开，半拖半推地带着她走，直到脱开了厂房。

===Original===

She thought they were going back to the house, which was in darkness, but instead, he led her straight along the wall, and presently she saw the bulk of the barge.

===Chinese===

她以为他们要回那宅子去——宅子黑着灯——他却领着她径直沿着墙走，不多时，她便望见了那艘驳船黑黢黢的庞然身影。

===Original===

"Stay, and do not speak," he said, and began to turn a rusty wheel. With a squeak and a groan the water-gates opened inwards. What did he intend doing? There was no sign of a boat, only this old dilapidated barge. She was presently to know.

"Come," he said again.

She was on the deck of the barge, moving forward to its bow, which pointed towards the open gate and the canal beyond.

===Chinese===

「站住，不许出声。」他说着，转动起一只锈迹斑斑的轮盘。吱呀一声、一声呻吟，水门朝里开了。他打算干什么？一点船的影子也没有，只有这艘破败不堪的老驳船。她很快就会知道的。

「来。」他又说了一遍。

她上了驳船的甲板，朝船头走去；船头正对着敞开的水门和门外的运河。

===Original===

She heard him puff and groan as he strained at a rope he had found, and then, looking down, she saw the front of the barge open, like the two water-gates of a lock. Displaying remarkable agility, he lowered himself over the edge; he seemed to be standing on something solid, for again he ordered her to join him.

"I will not go," she said breathlessly, and turning, would have fled, but his hand caught her dress and dragged at her.

===Chinese===

她听见他连喘带哼，正拽着一根寻出来的绳子使劲；随后她朝下一望，看见驳船的前部张了开来，就像船闸的两扇闸门。他显出惊人的敏捷，翻身跨过船缘下去了；他像是站在什么结实的东西上，因为他又吩咐她下去同他会合。

「我不下去。」她气喘吁吁地说，转身便想逃，他的手却抓住了她的衣裙，把她往回拽。

===Original===

"I will drown you here, woman," he said, and she knew that the threat would have a sequel.

===Chinese===

「我就在这儿淹死你，女人。」他说；她知道，这威胁是会有下文的。

===Original===

Tremblingly she lowered herself over the edge until her foot touched something hard and yet yielding. He was pushing at the barge with all his might, and the platform beneath her grew in space. First the sharp nose and then the covered half-deck of the fastest motorboat that Mr. Oberzohn's money could buy, or the ingenuity of builders could devise. The old barge was a boathouse, and this means of escape had always been to his hand. It was for this reason that he lived in a seemingly inaccessible spot.

===Chinese===

她哆哆嗦嗦地从船缘翻下去，直到脚尖触到一样又硬而又有弹性的东西。他正拼尽全力推那艘驳船，她脚底下的平台越露越大。先探出那尖锐的艇首，接着是带篷的半截甲板——那是欧伯佐恩先生的钱所能买到、造船匠的心机所能想出的最快的摩托艇。那艘旧驳船原是一间船屋，这条脱身之路从来就在他手边。他之所以住在这处看似无路可通的所在，缘由正在于此。

===Original===

The men who had been on the canal bank were gone. The propellers revolving slowly, the boat stole down the dark waters, after a short time slipped under a bridge over which streetcars were passing, and headed for Deptford and the river.

===Chinese===

原先守在运河岸上的那些人已经走了。螺旋桨缓缓旋转，小艇悄悄溜下黑沉沉的水面，不多时从一座桥下钻过——桥上正有电车驶过——朝德普特福德和大河驶去。

===Original===

Dr. Oberzohn took off his overcoat and laid it tenderly inside the shelter of the open cabin, tenderly because every pocket was packed tight with money.

===Chinese===

欧伯佐恩博士脱下大衣，轻轻柔柔地安放在敞舱的棚罩里；这份轻柔，是因为每一只口袋都塞满了钞票，塞得紧绷绷的。

===Original===

To Mirabelle Leicester, crouching in the darkness of that sheltered space, the time that passed had no dimension. Once an authoritative voice hailed them from the bank. It was a policeman; she saw him after the boat had passed. A gas-lamp showed the glitter of his metal buttons. But soon he was far behind.

===Chinese===

对蜷伏在那棚罩阴影里的米拉贝尔·莱斯特来说，逝去的时间没有了长短。有一回，岸上一个威严的声音朝他们吆喝。是个警察；小艇驶过之后她才看见他。一盏煤气灯映出他金属纽扣的闪光。可不多时，他已远远落在了后头。

===Original===

Deptford was near when they reached a barrier which neither ingenuity nor money could pass; a ragged nightbird peered down curiously at the motorboat.

"You can't get through here, guv'nor," he said simply. "The lock doesn't open until high tide."

===Chinese===

快到德普特福德时，他们碰上了一道心机与金钱都闯不过去的关卡；一个衣衫褴褛的夜游神好奇地朝下面打量这条摩托艇。

「打这儿过不去，老板。」他平平淡淡地说。「船闸要等满潮才开。」

===Original===

"When is this high tide?" asked Oberzohn breathlessly.

"Six o'clock tomorrow morning," said the voice.

For a long time he saw, stricken to inactivity by the news, and then he sent his engines into reverse and began circling round.

===Chinese===

「什么时候满潮？」欧伯佐恩上气不接下气地问。

「明天早晨六点。」那声音说。

这消息把他打懵了，好半天他僵着不动，随后他让发动机倒转，开始兜起圈来。

===Original===

"There is one refuge for us, young miss," he said. "Soon we shall see it. Now I will tell you something. I desire so much to live. Do you also?"

She did not answer.

"If you cry out, if you will make noises, I will kill you—that is all," he said; and the very simplicity of his words, the lack of all emphasis behind the deadly earnestness, told her that he would keep his word.

===Chinese===

「咱们还有一处藏身之所，年轻的小姐。」他说。「很快就能见着了。现在我要告诉你一件事。我非常想活下去。你也想么？」

她没有回答。

「你若喊叫，你若弄出声响，我就杀了你——就是这样。」他说；这话直白到极处，那致命的认真背后又不见一点着重的语气，反倒叫她明白：他必定说到做到。

## XXII The Search / 二十二 搜寻

===Original===

“ ’Ware mantraps,” said Gonsalez.

The white beam of his lamp had detected the ugly thing. He struck at it with his stick, and with a vicious snap it closed.
===Chinese===

「当心捕人铁夹。」冈萨雷斯说。

他的手电射出的一道白光已经照见了那件丑家伙。他拿手杖朝它敲去，只听一声恶狠狠的脆响，铁夹合上了。

===Original===

“Here’s one that’s been sprung,” he said, and examined the teeth. “And, what’s more, it has made a catch! There’s blood here.”
===Chinese===

「这儿有一只是触发过的。」他说着，察看那铁齿。「而且，它还捕着了东西！这儿有血。」

===Original===

Manfred and Digby were searching the ground cautiously. Then Manfred heard the quick intake of his breath, and he stooped again, picked up a strip of braided cloth.

“A man’s,” he said, and his relief betrayed his fear. “Somebody in evening dress, and quite recent.” He looked at his finger. “The blood is still wet.”
===Chinese===

曼弗雷德和迪格比正小心翼翼地搜索地面。忽然曼弗雷德听见一声急促的吸气，便又俯下身去，拾起一条编带的布片。

「男人的。」他说，那一口如释重负，反倒泄露了心底的惧意。「穿晚礼服的人，而且就在不久之前。」他看了看自己的手指。「血还是湿的。」

===Original===

Digby showed him the ventilator grating through which he had smelt the incense, and when Leon stooped, the faint aroma still remained.
===Chinese===

迪格比领他看了那个他闻到过熏香气味的通风口格栅；莱昂俯身凑近，那缕淡淡的香气还在。

===Original===

“We will try the factory first. If that draws blank, we’ll ask Dr. Oberzohn’s guidance, and if it is not willingly given I shall persuade him.” And in the reflected light of the lamp George Manfred saw the hard Leon he knew of old. “This time I shall not promise: my threat will be infinitely milder than my performance.”
===Chinese===

「咱们先搜厂房。要是扑了个空，就去请教欧伯佐恩博士；他若不肯痛快指教，我便去说服他。」借着灯光的反照，乔治·曼弗雷德看见了那个他早已熟识的心肠刚硬的莱昂。「这一回我不许诺了：我的威胁，将远比我的行动温和。」

===Original===

They came to the dark entry of the factory, and Manfred splashed his light inside.

“You’ll have to walk warily here,” he said.
===Chinese===

他们走到厂房黑洞洞的入口，曼弗雷德把手电的光泼进里面。

「这儿可得步步留神。」他说。

===Original===

Progress was slow, for they did not know that a definite path existed between the jagged ends of broken iron and debris. Once or twice Leon stopped to stamp on the floor; it gave back a hollow sound.
===Chinese===

行进得很慢，因为在断铁的尖茬与瓦砾之间本有一条现成的路径，他们却并不知晓。莱昂停了一两回，顿脚踩踩地板；地板回以空洞的声响。

===Original===

The search was long and painfully slow: a quarter of an hour passed before Leon’s lamp focused the upturned flagstone and the yawning entrance of the vault. He was the first to descend, and, as he reached the floor, he saw, silhouetted in the light that flowed from the inner room, a man, as he thought, crouching in the doorway, and covered him.
===Chinese===

搜索漫长而磨人地缓慢：过了一刻钟，莱昂的灯光才照定那块翻起的石板和地窖张着大口的入口。他头一个下去；脚才落地，就看见门口蹲踞着一个人影——他只当是个活人——映在内室流泻出来的光里成了一道剪影，他当即举枪对准。

===Original===

“Put up your hands!” he said.

The figure made no response, and Manfred ran to the shape. The face was in the shadow, but he brought his own lamp down and recognized the set grin of the dead man.

Gurther!

So thus he had died, in a last effort to climb out for help.
===Chinese===

「举起手来！」他说。

那人影毫无回应，曼弗雷德朝那轮廓奔了过去。脸还埋在暗处，他把自己的灯往下一照，认出了死人脸上那僵凝的狞笑。

格瑟！

他原来是这么死的——拼尽最后一点力气，想爬出去求救。

===Original===

“The Snake,” said Manfred briefly. “There are no marks on his face, so far as I can see.”

“Do you notice his wrist, George?”

Then, looking past the figure, Gonsalez saw the girl lying on the bed, and recognized Joan before he saw her face. Halfway across the room he slipped on something. Instinctively he knew it was a snake and leapt around, his pistol balanced.
===Chinese===

「毒蛇。」曼弗雷德简短地说。「依我看，他脸上没有伤痕。」

「你留意到他的手腕没有，乔治？」

这时，目光越过那具身影，冈萨雷斯望见了床上躺着的姑娘，还没看清脸，已认出那是琼。走到屋子中途，他的脚在什么东西上一滑。他本能地知道那是条蛇，霍地旋身，手枪端得平正。

===Original===

“Merciful heaven! Look at this!”

He stared from the one reptile to the other.

“Dead!” he said. “That explains Gurther.”
===Chinese===

「仁慈的老天！瞧这个！」

他两眼从这条爬虫望到那条爬虫。

「死了！」他说。「格瑟的死因，这就说通了。」

===Original===

Quickly he unstrapped Joan’s wrists and lifted up her head, listening, his ear pressed to the faintly fluttering heart. The basin and the sponge told its own story. Where was Mirabelle?

There was another room, and a row of big cupboards, but the girl was in no place that he searched.
===Chinese===

他飞快解开琼手腕上的皮带，托起她的头，耳朵贴在那微微扑动的心口上听。脸盆和海绵，已把经过自陈分明。米拉贝尔在哪儿？

还有一间屋，还有一排大柜子，可他要找的姑娘，凡他搜过的地方都不在。

===Original===

“She’s gone, of course,” said Manfred quietly. “Otherwise, the trap would not have been open. We’d better get this poor girl out of the way and search the grounds. Digby, go to﻿—”

He stopped. If Oberzohn were in the house, they must not take the risk of alarming him.
===Chinese===

「她自然是被带走了。」曼弗雷德平静地说。「不然活板门不会开着。咱们最好先把这个可怜的姑娘弄出去，再搜四下的场地。迪格比，你去——」

他住了口。倘若欧伯佐恩就在宅子里，便千万冒不得惊动他的险。

===Original===

But the girl’s needs were urgent. Manfred picked her up and carried her out into the open, and, with Leon guiding them, they came, after a trek which almost ended in a broken neck for Leon, to within a few yards of the house.
===Chinese===

可这姑娘的急难刻不容缓。曼弗雷德抱起她，抱到露天之下；莱昂在前引路，一路跋涉——这一路险些叫莱昂折了脖颈——终于来到离宅子不过几码的地方。

===Original===

“I presume,” said Gonsalez, “that the hole into which I nearly dived was dug for a purpose, and I shouldn’t be surprised to learn it was intended that the late Mr. Gurther should find a permanent home there. Shall I take her?”

“No, no,” said Manfred, “go on into the lane. Poiccart should be there with the car by now.”
===Chinese===

「我揣想，」冈萨雷斯说，「我险些一头栽进去的那个窟窿，掘它是派用场的；若说本是要让已故的格瑟先生在那底下觅个永久的安身之所，我也毫不惊讶。要我来抱她么？」

「不，不用。」曼弗雷德说，「你只管到巷子里去。这会儿波伊卡特该带着车在那儿了。」

===Original===

“Poiccart knows more about growing onions than driving motorcars.” The gibe was mechanical; the man’s heart and mind were on Mirabelle Leicester.

They had to make a circuit of the stiff copper-wire fence which surrounded the house, and eventually reached Hangman’s Lane just as the headlamps of the Spanz came into view.
===Chinese===

「波伊卡特种洋葱懂得多，开汽车懂得少。」这句挖苦脱口而出，全无心思；他的心他的神，都在米拉贝尔·莱斯特身上。

他们只得绕着宅子四周那道坚硬的铜丝围栏兜了一个圈，终于抵达绞刑吏巷，恰在此时，「斯潘兹」的车头灯驶入了视野。

===Original===

“I will take her to the hospital and get in touch with the police,” said Manfred. “I suppose there isn’t a nearby telephone?”

“I shall probably telephone from the house,” said Leon gravely.
===Chinese===

「我带她上医院，再同警方联络。」曼弗雷德说。「近处该没有电话吧？」

「我多半就从这宅子里打电话。」莱昂神色沉重地说。

===Original===

From where he stood he could not tell whether the door was open or closed. There was no transom above the door, so that it was impossible to tell whether there were lights in the passage or not. The house was in complete darkness.

He was so depressed that he did not even give instructions to Poiccart, who was frankly embarrassed by the duty which had been imposed upon him, and gladly surrendered the wheel to George.

They lifted the girl into the tonneau, and, backing into the gate, went cautiously up the lane﻿—Leon did not wait to see their departure, but returned to the front of the house.
===Chinese===

从他站的地方，看不出大门是开着还是关着。门上方没有气窗，也无从知道门道里点没点灯。整座宅子黑得彻彻底底。

他消沉到这地步，连对波伊卡特也吩咐不出一句；波伊卡特被硬派了这桩差事，正自满脸为难，乐得把方向盘让给乔治。

他们把姑娘抬进后座车厢，车子倒进大门调了个头，小心翼翼地沿巷子驶去——莱昂没等看他们动身，便折回宅子正面去了。

===Original===

The place was in darkness. He opened the wire gate and went silently up the steps. He had not reached the top before he saw that the door was wide open. Was it a trap? His lamp showed him the switch: he turned on the light and closed the door behind him, and, bending his head, listened.

The first door on the right was Oberzohn’s room. The door was ajar, but the lamps were burning inside. He pushed it open with the toe of his boot, but the room was empty.
===Chinese===

那地方一片漆黑。他推开铁丝网门，悄没声儿地登上台阶。还没到顶，他已看见大门四敞大开。是个圈套么？手电给他照见了开关：他开了灯，反手带上大门，低下头谛听。

右边头一扇门是欧伯佐恩的房间。门虚掩着，屋里的灯却还亮着。他用靴尖把门顶开，屋里空空如也。

===Original===

The next two doors he tried on that floor were locked. He went carefully down to the kitchens and searched them both. They were tenantless. He knew there was a servant or two on the premises, but one thing he did not know, and this he discovered in the course of his tour, was that Oberzohn had no bedroom. One of the two rooms above had evidently been occupied by the servants. The door was open, the room was empty and in some confusion; a coarse nightdress had been hastily discarded and left on the tumbled bedclothes. Oberzohn had sent his servants away in a hurry﻿—why?
===Chinese===

这一层上他接下去试的两扇门都锁着。他小心地下到厨房，两间都搜了，都无人居住。他知道宅里有一两个仆人，可有一件事他不知道——也是这一趟巡视才发现的——欧伯佐恩竟没有卧室。楼上两间屋里，显然有一间是仆人住过的。门开着，屋里空了，略有些凌乱；一件粗布睡衣脱得仓促，撂在皱作一团的被褥上。欧伯佐恩急匆匆地把仆人打发了——为什么？

===Original===

There was a half-smoked cigarette on the edge of a deal washstand. The ash lay on the floor. In a bureau every drawer was open and empty, except one, a half-drawer filled with odd scraps of cloth. Probably the cook or the maid smoked. He found a packet of cigarettes under one pillow to confirm this view, and guessed they had gone to bed leisurely with no idea that they would be turned into the night.
===Chinese===

松木盥洗台的沿上搁着一支吸了半截的烟。烟灰落在地板上。一张书桌的抽屉全开着、全空着，只有一格半抽屉里塞满零碎布头。多半是厨娘或女仆抽烟。他在一只枕头底下又寻到一包香烟，印证了这一看法，便猜她们上床时从容得很，全没想到半夜会被撵出门去。

===Original===

He learned later that Oberzohn had bundled off his servants at ten minutes’ notice, paying them six months’ salary as some salve for the indignity.
===Chinese===

他后来才得知，欧伯佐恩给仆人们十分钟限期卷铺盖，付了他们六个月的工钱，算是给这番羞辱敷的一点药膏。

===Original===

Pfeiffer’s room was locked; but now, satisfied that the house was empty, he broke the flimsy catch, made a search but found nothing. Gurther’s apartment was in indescribable disorder. He had evidently changed in a hurry. His powder puffs and beards, crepe hair and spirit bottles, littered the dressing-table. He remembered, with a pang of contrition, that he had promised to telephone the police, but when he tried to get the exchange he found the line was dead: a strange circumstance, till he discovered that late that evening Meadows had decided to cut the house from all telephonic communication, and had given orders accordingly.
===Chinese===

法伊弗的房间锁着；不过此时他已确信宅子是空的，便折断那单薄的小锁舌，搜了一遍，一无所获。格瑟的房间乱得难以形容。他显然换装换得仓促。粉扑、假胡子、绉纱假发和一瓶瓶酒精，狼藉地摊在梳妆台上。他心头猛地一沉，悔痛地想起自己答应过给警方打电话；可他去要交换台时，却发现线路死了：这事蹊跷——直到后来他才查明，当晚深夜米多斯已决定切断这座宅子的一切电话联络，并已照此下了命令。

===Original===

It was a queerly built house: he had never realized its remarkable character until he had examined it at these close quarters. The walls were of immense thickness: that fact was brought home to him when he had opened the window of the maid’s room to see if Digby was in sight. The stairs were of concrete, the shutters which covered the windows of Oberzohn’s study were steel-faced. He decided, pending the arrival of the police, to make an examination of the two locked rooms. The first of these he had no difficulty in opening. It was a large room on the actual ground level, and was reached by going down six steps. A rough bench ran round three sides of this bare apartment, except where its continuity broke to allow entrance to a further room. The door was of steel and was fastened.
===Chinese===

这宅子造得古怪：直到这回近身细察，他才识出它的非同寻常。墙壁厚得吓人：他打开女仆房的窗子张望迪格比在不在视野里，那一开窗便叫他切实领教了这一点。楼梯是混凝土的，罩住欧伯佐恩书房窗户的百叶窗包着钢板。趁警察未到，他决定查一查那两间上锁的屋子。头一间他不费事便打开了。那是一间恰与地面齐平的大屋，下去六级台阶才到。这间光秃秃的屋子，三面沿墙转着一条粗木板长凳，只有一处中断，好让人走进更里面的屋子。那扇门是钢的，闩得死死的。

===Original===

The room was dusty but not untidy. Everything was in order. The various apparatus was separated by a clear space. In one corner he saw a gas engine and dynamo covered with dust. There was nothing to be gained here. The machine which interested him most was one he knew all about, only he had not guessed the graphite moulds. The contents of a small blue bottle, tightly corked, and seemingly filled with discoloured swabs of cotton-wool, however, revived his interest. With a glance round the laboratory, he went out and tried the second of the locked doors.
===Chinese===

屋里积灰，却不凌乱。样样东西各就各位。各式器械之间都隔着一块空地。一角里他看见一台蒙尘的煤气发动机和发电机。这里探不出什么名堂。最叫他上心的那台机器，他本是了如指掌的，只是不曾猜到那些石墨模具。然而一只塞得严严实实的小蓝瓶，又勾起了他的兴致——瓶里瞧着像塞满了变色的药棉团。他把这间实验室环顾一遍，走出去试那第二扇锁着的门。

===Original===

This room, however, was well protected, both in the matter of stoutness of door and complication of locks. Leon tried all his keys, and then used his final argument. This he carried in a small leather pouch in his hip pocket; three steel pieces that screwed together and ended in a bright claw. Hammering the end of the jemmy with his fist, he forced the claw between door and lintel, and in less than a minute the lock had broken, and he was in the presence of the strangest company that had ever been housed.
===Chinese===

这一间却防护得严实：门板之厚，锁具之繁，皆是上乘。莱昂把随身的钥匙试了个遍，随后祭出他最后的论据。这物件装在他裤后袋的一只小皮袋里：三截钢杆，可旋接成一体，末端是一只亮闪闪的爪钩。他用拳头捶着撬棍的尾端，把爪尖楔进门与门楣之间，不出一分钟，锁断了，他便来到了一群空前古怪的「住户」跟前。

===Original===

Four electric radiators were burning. The room was hot and heavy, and the taint of it caught his throat, as it had caught the throat of the Danish servant. He put on all the lights﻿—and they were many﻿—and then began his tour.
===Chinese===

四台电暖炉烧得正旺。屋里又热又浊，那股恶气直呛他的喉咙，一如它当年呛过那丹麦女仆的喉咙。他把灯全打开——灯着实不少——随后开始巡视。

===Original===

There were two lines of shelves, wide apart, and each supporting a number of boxes, some of which were wrapped in baize, some of which, however, were open to view. All had glass fronts, all had steel tops with tiny air-holes, and in each there coiled, in its bed of wool or straw, according to its requirements, one or two snakes. There were cobras, puff-adders, two rattlesnakes, seemingly dead, but, as he guessed, asleep; there was a South American fer-de-lance, that most unpleasant representative of his species; there were little coral snakes, and, in one long box, a whole nest of queer little things that looked like tiny yellow lobsters, but which he knew as scorpions.
===Chinese===

两排架子，隔得老宽，每排架上搁着一批箱子：有的裹着呢布，有的却敞在眼前。箱箱前脸是玻璃，箱箱顶上是钻着细气孔的钢板；每一箱里，各按习性，在羊毛或干草铺就的窝中盘着一条或两条蛇。有眼镜蛇，有鼓腹蝰，有两条响尾蛇——看着像死的，照他猜只是睡着；有一条南美矛头蛇，蛇族里最叫人不痛快的一位代表；有小小的珊瑚蛇；还有一只长箱里，满满一窝古怪的小东西，模样像一只只小小的黄色龙虾，他却认得，那是蝎子。

===Original===

He was lifting a baize cover when: “Don’t move, my friend! I think I can promise you more intimate knowledge of our little family.”

Leon turned slowly, his hands extended. Death was behind him, remorseless, unhesitating. To drop his hand to his pocket would have been the end for him﻿—he had that peculiar instinct which senses sincerity, and when Dr. Oberzohn gave him his instructions he had no doubt whatever that his threat was backed by the will to execute.
===Chinese===

他正掀起一块呢布罩，身后响起：「别动，我的朋友！我想我可以向你担保：你会有机会同我们这个小小的家族，结下更亲密的交情。」

莱昂缓缓转身，双手摊开。死神就在他背后：不留情面，不容迟疑。把手垂向口袋，便是他的末路——他生就一种能嗅出诚意的特异直觉，而当欧伯佐恩博士向他发话时，他毫不怀疑，这威胁背后正有着非执行不可的意志。

===Original===

Oberzohn stood there, a little behind him, white-faced, open-eyed with fear, Mirabelle Leicester.

Digby﻿—where was he? He had left him in the grounds.

The doctor was examining the broken door and grunted his annoyance.
===Chinese===

欧伯佐恩立在那里，稍稍在他身后；还有那脸色惨白、吓得睁大眼睛的米拉贝尔·莱斯特。

迪格比——他在哪儿？他分明把他留在场地上了。

博士正察看那扇砸坏的门，恼怒地哼了一声。

===Original===

“I fear my plan will not be good,” he said, “which was to lock you in this room and break all those glasses, so that you might become better acquainted with the Quiet People. That is not to be. Instead, march!”

What did he intend? Leon strolled out nonchalantly, but Oberzohn kept his distance, his eyes glued upon those sensitive hands that could move so quickly and jerk and fire a gun in one motion.
===Chinese===

「我怕我的计划要落空了。」他说，「原想把你锁进这间屋，把那些玻璃全打碎，好教你同『安静的人们』多多亲近。如今办不成了。那么——开步走！」

他打的什么主意？莱昂若无其事地踱出去，欧伯佐恩却始终跟他保持距离，两眼死死盯住那双灵敏的手——那双手动得飞快，能在一抖之间便把枪打响。

===Original===

“Stop!”

Leon halted, facing the open front door and the steps.
===Chinese===

「站住！」

莱昂收步站定，正对着敞开的大门和门前的台阶。

===Original===

“You will remember my sainted brother, Señor Gonsalez, and of the great loss which the world suffered when he was so vilely murdered?”

Leon stood without a quiver. Presently the man would shoot. At any second a bullet might come crashing on its fatal errand. This was a queer way to finish so full a life. He knew it was coming, had only one regret; that this shaken girl should be called upon to witness such a brutal thing. He wanted to say goodbye to her, but was afraid of frightening her.
===Chinese===

「你总还记得我那位圣徒般的兄长吧，冈萨雷斯先生？也记得他遭人那样卑劣谋害时，这世界蒙受的重大损失吧？」

莱昂站着，纹丝不动。转眼间那人就要开枪了。随时会有一颗子弹呼啸而至，去办它那致命的差使。这样充实的一生，竟用这样古怪的方式收梢。他知道那一刻正在逼近，心里只有一桩遗憾：这个魂魄俱乱的姑娘，竟要被叫来亲眼看这样一桩残酷的事。他想同她道别，又怕惊了她。

===Original===

“You remember that so sainted brother?” Oberzohn’s voice was raucous with fury. Ahead of him the light fell upon a face.

“Digby! Stay where you are!” shouted Leon.
===Chinese===

「你记得那位圣徒般的兄长吧？」欧伯佐恩的嗓音嘶哑，浸透了狂怒。在他前方，灯光正落在一张脸上。

「迪格比！站在原地别动！」莱昂高喊。

===Original===

The sound of the explosion made him jump. He saw the brickwork above the doorway splinter, heard a little scuffle, and turned, gun in hand. Oberzohn had pulled the girl in front of him so that she afforded a complete cover: under her arm he held his pistol.
===Chinese===

枪声震得他一跳。他看见门洞上方的砖石迸裂，听见一阵短促的扭打，便握着枪转过身去。欧伯佐恩已把姑娘拽到身前，拿她作了一副严严实实的遮挡：手枪就夹在她的胳膊底下。

===Original===

“Run!” she screamed.

He hesitated a second. Again the pistol exploded and a bullet ricochetted from the door. Leon could not fire. Oberzohn so crouched that nothing but a trick shot could miss the girl and hit him. And then, as the doctor shook free the hand that gripped his wrist, he leapt down the steps and into the darkness. Another second and the door slammed. He heard the thrust of the bolts and a clang as the great iron bar fell into its place. Somehow he had a feeling as of a citadel door being closed against him.
===Chinese===

「快跑！」她尖声喊。

他迟疑了一秒。枪又响了一声，一颗子弹从门上弹飞。莱昂开不得枪。欧伯佐恩蜷得那样低，除非打出绝活的巧枪，才能绕过姑娘打中他。就在这时，趁着博士甩脱那只攥住他手腕的手，他跃下台阶，遁入黑暗。又过一秒，大门砰然关死。他听见门闩一根根捅进槽里，又听见那根大铁闩落位时的一声铿然。不知怎的，他心头生出一种感觉，仿佛一座堡垒的大门，正对着他轰然关闭。

---

===Original===

Dr. Oberzohn had returned unobserved, though the night was clear. Passing through the open water-gate he had tied up to the little quay and landed his unwilling passenger. Digby, according to instructions, had been making a careful circuit of the property, and at the moment was as far away from the barge as it was humanly possible to be. Unchallenged, the doctor had worked his way back to the house. The light in the hall warned him that somebody was there. How many? He could not guess.
===Chinese===

欧伯佐恩博士回来了，竟无一人察觉，虽说夜色清朗。他驶进敞开的水门，把艇拴上小码头，让那位百般不愿的乘客登了岸。迪格比奉命正沿产业外围细细兜圈，此一刻，人在人力所能及的离驳船最远之处。博士一路无人盘问，摸回了宅子。门厅里的灯光警示他：里头有人。几个？他猜不着。

===Original===

“Take off your shoes,” he growled in Mirabelle’s ear, and she obeyed.

Whatever happened, he must not lose touch of her, or give her an opportunity of escape. Still grasping her arm with one hand and his long Mauser pistol in the other, he went softly up the steps, got into the hall and listened, locating the intruder instantly.
===Chinese===

「把鞋脱了。」他对着米拉贝尔的耳朵低吼，她照办了。

无论生出什么变故，他决不能同她脱开身，也不能给她半点脱身的机会。他一手仍旧攥着她的胳膊，另一手握着那支长长的毛瑟枪，轻手轻脚登上台阶，进了门厅，侧耳一听，立时锁定了那个闯入者的方位。

===Original===

It all happened so quickly that Mirabelle could remember nothing except the desperate lunge she made to knock up the pistol that had covered the spine of Leon Gonsalez. She stood dumbly by, watching this horrible old man fasten the heavy door, and obediently preceded him from room to room. She saw the long cases in the hot room and shrank back. And then began a complete tour of the house. There were still shutters to be fastened, peepholes to be opened up. He screwed up the shutters of the servants’ room, and then, with a hammer, broke the thumb-piece short.
===Chinese===

这一切都来得太快，米拉贝尔事后什么都记不得了，只记得自己拼死扑了那一把，要把对准莱昂·冈萨雷斯后心的那支枪磕飞。她哑然立在一旁，看着这可怕的老头闩牢那扇沉重的门，又乖乖地在头前引路，随他一间屋一间屋地走。她望见暖屋里的那些长箱子，吓得直缩。随后，全宅大巡视开始了。还有百叶窗要闩死，窥孔要捅开。他把仆人房间的百叶窗拧死，又拿一把锤子，把旋柄生生敲断。

===Original===

“You will stay here,” he said. “I do not know what they will do. Perhaps they will shoot. I also am a shooter!”

Not satisfied with the lock that fastened her door, he went into his workshop, found a staple, hook and padlock, and spent the greater part of an hour fixing this additional security. At last he had finished, and could put the situation in front of four very interested men.
===Chinese===

「你就待在这儿。」他说。「我不知道他们会做什么。也许他们会放枪。我也是个放枪的！」

他不放心闩她房门的那把锁，便进了工作间，寻出一副锁鼻、搭钩和挂锁，花了大半个钟头安上这道额外的保险。总算弄完了，他可以把眼下的局面，摆到四个听得入神的人面前。

===Original===

He unlocked the door of the concrete annex and called the crestfallen gunmen forth, and in a very few words explained the situation and their danger.
===Chinese===

他开了水泥库房的门，把那几个垂头丧气的枪手唤出来，三言两语，讲明了局势和他们的险境。

===Original===

“For every one of you the English police hold warrants,” he said. “I do not bluff, I know. This afternoon I was visited by the police. I tell you I do not bluff you﻿—me they cannot touch, because they know nothing, can prove nothing. At most I shall go to prison for a few years, but with you it is different.”
===Chinese===

「英国警方手里，为你们每人都捏着拘票。」他说。「我不是虚声恫吓，这我知道。今天下午警察登门造访过我。我告诉你，我不是吓唬你们——我这个人他们碰不着，因为他们一无所知，一无所证。至多我坐上几年牢，你们可就不同了。」

===Original===

“Are they waiting outside?” asked one suspiciously. “Because, if they are, we’d better move quick.”

“You do not move, quick or slow,” said Oberzohn. “To go out from here means certain imprisonment for you all. To stay, if you follow my plan, means that every one of you may go free and with money.”
===Chinese===

「他们在外头候着么？」一个狐疑地问。「要是候着，咱们最好快挪。」

「你们不许动，快也不许，慢也不许。」欧伯佐恩说。「从这里出去，意味着你们个个难逃牢狱。留下来，只要依我的计划，意味着你们人人都可能脱身，还能带着钱。」

===Original===

“What’s the idea?” asked Cuccini. “Are you going to fight them?”

“Sure I am going to fight them,” nodded Oberzohn. “That is my scheme. I have the young miss upstairs; they will not wish to do her any harm. I intend to defend this house.”

“Do you mean you’re going to hold it?” asked one of the staggered men.

“I will hold it until they are tired, and make terms.”
===Chinese===

「打的什么算盘？」库奇尼问。「你要跟他们干一仗？」

「仗，我自然是要打的。」欧伯佐恩点头道。「这就是我的方略。楼上那位年轻的小姐在我手里；他们不会愿意伤她分毫。我打算守住这座宅子。」

「你是说，你要死守着它？」一个惊得发怔的人问。

「我要守到他们倦了，来同我讲条件。」

===Original===

Cuccini was biting his nails nervously.

“Might as well be hung for a sheep as a lamb, boss,” he growled. “I’ve got an idea you’ve roped us into this.”

“You may rope yourself out of it!” snapped Oberzohn. “There is the door﻿—go if you wish. There are police there; make terms with them. A few days ago you were in trouble, my friend. Who saved you? The doctor Oberzohn. There is life imprisonment for every one of you, and I can hold this house myself. Stay with me, and I will give you a fortune greater than any you have dreamt about. And, more than this, at the end you shall be free.”
===Chinese===

库奇尼紧张地啃着指甲。

「偷羊偷羔，横竖都是上绞架，老大。」他咕哝道。「我算看出来了，你是把咱们全圈进这局里了。」

「那你就自己从套索里钻出去！」欧伯佐恩厉声道。「门在那儿——愿意就走。外头有警察；同他们讲条件去。几天之前你还身陷难处，我的朋友。是谁救的你？欧伯佐恩博士。你们人人面前都摆着无期徒刑，而这座宅子，我一个人也守得住。跟着我，我给你们的钱财，比你们做梦梦见的哪一笔都大。还不止此——到了头，你们还能自由。」

===Original===

“Where’s Gurther?”

“He has been killed﻿—by accident.” Oberzohn’s face was working furiously. “By accident he died,” he said, and told the truth unconvincingly. “There is nothing now to do but to make a decision.”
===Chinese===

「格瑟呢？」

「他死了——死于意外。」欧伯佐恩的脸剧烈地抽动着。「死于意外，他死了。」他说，说的是实话，却说得不能服人。「眼下别无他法，只有拿定主意。」

===Original===

Cuccini and his friends consulted in a whisper.

“What do we get for our share?” he asked, and Oberzohn mentioned a sum which staggered them.

“I speak the truth,” he said. “In two days I shall have a goldmine worth millions.”
===Chinese===

库奇尼和他的伙伴们交头接耳地商量起来。

「咱们那一份，能到手多少？」他问；欧伯佐恩报出一个数目，惊得众人咋舌。

「我说的是实话。」他说。「两天之内，我名下就会有一座价值数百万的金矿。」

===Original===

The habit of frankness was on him, and he told them the story of the golden hill without reservations. His agents at Lisbon had already obtained from the Ministry an option upon the land and its mineral rights. As the clock struck twelve on June 14, the goldfield of Biskara automatically passed into his possession.

“On one side you have certain imprisonment, on the other you have great monies and happiness.”
===Chinese===

他这会儿坦诚成了癖，便把那座金山的故事一五一十讲给他们听，毫无保留。他设在里斯本的代理人，已从该部弄到那片土地连同矿权的期权。6 月 14 日时钟敲响十二下，比斯卡拉金矿便自动归入他的名下。

「一边是笃定的牢狱，一边是大笔的钱财与好日子。」

===Original===

“How long will we have to stay here?” asked Cuccini.

“I have food for a month, even milk. They will not cut the water because of the girl. For the same reason they will not blow in the door.”

Again they had a hasty consultation and made their decision.
===Chinese===

「咱们得在这儿待多久？」库奇尼问。

「我备下了一个月的口粮，连牛奶都有。看在姑娘分上，他们不会断水。也是为着这同一个缘故，他们不会轰开这扇门。」

他们又匆匆计议了一回，拿定了主意。

===Original===

“All right, boss, we’ll stay. But we want that share-out put into writing.”

“To my study,” said Oberzohn promptly, “march!”
===Chinese===

「行，老大，我们留下。可那笔分账，得写成白纸黑字。」

「到我的书房去。」欧伯佐恩应声道，「开步走！」

===Original===

He was halfway through writing the document when there came a thunderous knock on the door and he got up, signalling for silence. Tiptoeing along the passage, he came to the door.

“Yes﻿—who is that?” he asked.

“Open, in the name of the law!” said a voice, and he recognized Meadows. “I have a warrant for your arrest, and if necessary the door will be broken in.”

“So!” said Oberzohn, dropped the muzzle of his pistol until it rested on the edge of the little letter-slit and fired twice.
===Chinese===

文书写到一半，门上响起一阵雷霆般的擂击，他站起身，示意众人噤声。他踮着脚沿门道走到门前。

「谁——外头是谁？」他问。

「开门，以法律之名！」一个声音说，他听出是米多斯。「我手里有逮捕你的拘票；必要的话，这扇门是要砸开的。」

「好哇！」欧伯佐恩说。他把手枪口沉下去，架在那道小投信口的边沿上，开了两枪。

## XXXIII The Siege / 三十三 围攻

===Original===

But Meadows had already been warned to keep clear of the letter-box, and the bullets eventually reached one of the railway viaducts, to the embarrassment of a road ganger who happened to be almost in the line of fire.

Meadows slipped down the steps to cover. Inside the wire fence a dozen policemen were waiting. "Sergeant, go back to the station in the police car and bring arms," he said. "This is going to be a long job."

===Chinese===

但米多斯早已得了警告，不靠近那只信箱，子弹飞到末了，落到了一座铁路高架桥上，害得一个恰好几乎正处在弹道上的养路工头好不狼狈。

米多斯溜下台阶，躲进掩体。铁丝网内候着十来个警察。「巡佐，乘警车回分局，把枪械取来。」他说。「这会是场持久战。」

===Original===

Gonsalez had made a very careful reconnaissance of the ground, and from the first had recognized the difficulties which lay ahead of the attacking party. The wall rose sheer without any break; such windows as were within reach were heavily shuttered; and even the higher windows, he guessed, had been covered. The important problem in his mind was to locate the room in which the girl was imprisoned, and, making a mental review of the house, he decided that she was either in the servants' apartment or in that which had held Gurther. By the light of the lantern he made a rapid sketch plan of the floors he had visited.

===Chinese===

冈萨雷斯早已把这一带地形侦察得极为仔细，从一开始便看清了进攻一方面前的重重困难。那道墙笔直而起，没有一处豁口；凡够得着的窗子，一律钉着厚重的窗板；便是更高处的窗，他料想也已被封了起来。他心头最要紧的问题，是查明姑娘囚在哪间屋里；他在脑中把这宅子从头到尾检视一遍，断定她不是在仆人的住处，便是在原先囚过格瑟的那一间。他就着提灯的光，把走过的几层楼面飞快画成一张简图。

===Original===

Meadows had gone away to telephone to police headquarters. He had decided to reestablish telephone connection with the doctor, and when this was done, he called the house and Oberzohn's voice answered him.

The colloquy was short and unsatisfactory. The terms which the doctor offered were such as no self-respecting government could accept. Immunity for himself and his companions (he insisted so strongly upon this latter offer that Meadows guessed, accurately, that the gang were standing around the instrument).

===Chinese===

米多斯到别处给警察总部打电话去了。他已决定恢复与博士的电话联络；线一接通，他便往宅里挂了电话，接话的是欧伯佐恩的声音。

这番交涉短促而不痛快。博士开出的条件，是任何尚有自尊的政府都断难接受的：给他本人及其同伙一体豁免（后一条他坚持得那样起劲，米多斯准确地猜到，那伙人正围着话机站成一圈）。

===Original===

"I don't want your men at all. So far as I am concerned, they can go free," said Meadows. "Ask one of them to speak on the phone."

===Chinese===

「你手下的人，我根本不要。就我而论，他们尽管自去。」米多斯说。「叫他们中随便哪个来接一接电话。」

===Original===

"Oh, indeed, no," said Oberzohn. "It is ridiculous to ask me that."

He hung up at this point and explained to the listening men that the police had offered him freedom if he would surrender the gang.

===Chinese===

「哦，那可不行。」欧伯佐恩说。「要我答应这个，未免荒唐。」

说到这里他把电话挂了，向竖着耳朵的众人解释：警方开出条件，只要他肯交出这伙人，便放他自由。

===Original===

"As I already told you," he said in conclusion, "that is not the way of Dr. Oberzohn. I will gain nothing at the expense of my friends."

===Chinese===

「正如我方才告诉诸位的，」他末了说道，「那不是欧伯佐恩博士的为人。我绝不在牺牲朋友之中，为自己谋得分毫。」

===Original===

A little later, when Cuccini crept into the room to call police headquarters and confirm this story of the doctor, he found that not only had the wire been cut, but a yard of the flex had been removed. Dr. Oberzohn was taking no risks.

===Chinese===

过了不大一会儿，库奇尼蹑手蹑脚溜进房来，想往警察总部挂个电话，验证博士这套说辞，却发现不但线被剪断，连一码长的皮线也叫人拆走了。欧伯佐恩博士从不冒一丝风险。

===Original===

The night passed without any further incident. Police reserves were pouring into the neighbourhood; the grounds had been isolated, and even the traffic of barges up and down the canal prohibited. The late editions of the morning newspapers had a heavily headlined paragraph about the siege of a house in the New Cross area, and when the first reporters arrived a fringe of sightseers had already gathered at every police barrier. Later, special editions, with fuller details, began to roll out of Fleet Street; the crowd grew in density, and a high official from Scotland Yard, arriving soon after nine, ordered a further area to be cleared, and with some difficulty the solid wedge of humanity at the end of Hangman's Lane was slowly pushed back until the house was invisible to them. Even here, a passageway was kept for police cars and only holders of passes were allowed to come within the prohibited area.

===Chinese===

这一夜再没有别的动静。警察后备队源源开进左近一带；园地已被封锁隔离，连运河上上下下的驳船交通也一概禁止。晨报的晚版登出一段标题浓黑硕大的报道，说的是新克罗斯地区一座宅子遭围攻；待第一批记者赶到时，每道警察警戒线外都已围上了一圈看热闹的人。随后，载着更详尽细节的号外，一份份从舰队街（Fleet Street）滚滚涌出；人群越聚越密，苏格兰场的一位高级官员九点刚过便到场，下令再清出一片地方；绞刑吏巷尽头那一大块密实的人楔，费了好一番气力才被慢慢推后，直推到望不见那宅子为止。便在这条线内，也给警车留着一条通道，唯有持通行证者方准进入禁区。

===Original===

The three men, with the police chief, had taken up their headquarters in the factory, from which the body of Gurther had been removed in the night. The Deputy Commissioner, who came on the spot at nine, and examined the dead snakes, was something of a herpetologist, and pronounced them to be veritable fers-de-lance, a view from which Poiccart differed.

===Chinese===

三义士同警察总监已把指挥部安进了厂房——格瑟的尸身夜里已从这里移走。九点到场的副警务总监查验了那几条死蛇；此人算得上半个爬虫学家，一口断定它们是如假包换的矛头蛇。这个论断，波伊卡特不敢苟同。

===Original===

"They are a species of African tree snakes that the natives call mamba. There are two, a black and a green. Both of these are the black type."

===Chinese===

「那是非洲树蛇的一种，土人管它叫曼巴。共分两样，一黑一绿。这两条都是黑的那一样。」

===Original===

"The Zoo mamba?" said the official, remembering the sensational disappearance of a deadly snake which had preceded the first of the snake mysteries.

===Chinese===

「动物园那条曼巴？」那位官员说，想起了毒蛇连环奇案开锣之前轰动一时的毒蛇失踪案。

===Original===

"You will probably find the bones of the Zoo mamba in some mole run in Regent's Park—he must have been frozen to death the night of his escape," said Poiccart. "It was absolutely impossible that at that temperature he could live. I have made a very careful inspection of the land, and adjacent to the Zoological Gardens is a big stretch of earth which is honeycombed by moles. No, this was imported, and the rest of his menagerie was imported."

===Chinese===

「动物园那条曼巴的尸骨，多半能在摄政公园哪条鼹鼠洞道里寻见——它出逃的那夜，准是冻死了。」波伊卡特说。「那样的气温，它绝无活路。那片地我做过极仔细的踏勘：动物园紧旁有一大片土地，叫鼹鼠钻得千疮百孔。不，这一条是外洋进口的；他那座兽园里其余的活计，也无一不是进口的。」

===Original===

The police chief shook his head.

"Still, I'm not convinced that a snake could have been responsible for these deaths," he said, and went over the ground so often covered.

The three listened in polite silence, and offered no suggestion.

===Chinese===

警察总监摇了摇头。

「话虽如此，我还是不信这些死亡会是蛇下的手。」他说，又把那套翻来覆去讲了不知多少遍的道理重讲了一遍。

三位客客气气地静听着，一句口也不插。

===Original===

The morning brought news of Washington's arrival in Lisbon. He had left the train at Irun, Leon's agent in Madrid having secured a relay of aeroplanes, and the journey from Irun to Lisbon had been completed in a few hours. He was now on his way back.

===Chinese===

早晨传来消息，说华盛顿已抵达里斯本。他在伊伦便下了火车——莱昂驻马德里的代理人备妥了一程接一程的飞机——伊伦到里斯本这段路程，几个钟头便告完成。他此刻已在归途之上。

===Original===

"If he makes the connections he will be here tonight," he told Manfred. "I rather think he will be a very useful recruit to our forces."

"You're thinking of the snakes in the house?"

Leon nodded.

"I know Oberzohn," he said simply, and George Manfred thought of the girl, and knew the unspoken fears of his friend were justified.

===Chinese===

「各程若都衔接得上，他今夜便能到。」他告诉曼弗雷德。「我看，他多半会是咱们麾下一员大有用处的新力。」

「你想着的是宅子里那些蛇？」

莱昂点了点头。

「我了解欧伯佐恩。」他平平地说。乔治·曼弗雷德想起了那姑娘，知道朋友这份未曾出口的忧惧，绝非多虑。

===Original===

The night had not been an idle one for Oberzohn and his companions. With the first light of dawn they had mounted to the roof, and, under his direction, the gunmen had dismantled the four sheds which stood at each corner of the parapet. Unused to the handling of such heavy metal, the remnants of the Old Guard gazed in awe upon the tarnished jackets of the Maxim guns that were revealed.

===Chinese===

这一夜，欧伯佐恩和他的同伙也不曾虚度。破晓头一线光，他们便上了屋顶；在他的指点之下，枪手们把女儿墙四角上那四座小棚一一拆开。「老近卫军」的残部摆弄不来这般沉重的铁家伙，眼见露出来的马克沁机枪那锈黯失色的冷却水套，不由得肃然起了敬畏。

===Original===

Oberzohn understood the mechanism of the machines so thoroughly that in half an hour he had taught his crew the method of handling and sighting. In the larger shed was a collapsible tripod, which was put together, and on this he mounted a small but powerful searchlight and connected it up with one of the plugs in the roof.

===Chinese===

欧伯佐恩对这些机械的构造烂熟于胸，不过半个钟头，便把操枪与瞄准的法门教给了他这一班人马。较大的那座棚里有一副折叠三脚架，众人把它支了起来，他把一具小巧而强力十足的探照灯装了上去，接通屋顶上的一个插座。

===Original===

He pointed to them the three approaches to the house: the open railway arches and the long lane, at the end of which the crowd at that moment was beginning to gather.

"From only these places can the ground be approached," he said, "and my little quick-firers cover them!"

===Chinese===

他把通往宅子的三条来路一一指给他们看：几孔豁然的铁路桥洞，还有那条长巷——此时巷口那头，人群正开始聚集。

「只有打这几处，地面才能逼近，」他说，「而我这些小小的速射枪，正把它们一一罩住！」

===Original===

Just before eleven there came down Hangman's Lane, drawn by a motor tractor, a long tree-trunk, suspended about the middle by chains, and Oberzohn, examining it carefully through his field-glasses, realized that no door in the world could stand against the attack of that battering-ram. He took up one of the dozen rifles that lay on the floor, sighted it carefully, resting his elbow on the parapet, and fired.

===Chinese===

十一点将近，绞刑吏巷里开来一辆机动拖拉机，拖着一根长树干，中段用铁链兜住吊起。欧伯佐恩用双筒望远镜细细察看，顿时明白：天底下没有一扇门，经得起那根攻城槌的一击。他从摊在地上的十来支步枪里拈起一支，肘子搁在女儿墙上，仔细瞄准，开了火。

===Original===

He saw the helmet of a policeman shoot away from the head of the astonished man, and fired again. This time he was more successful, for a policeman who was directing the course of the tractor crumpled up and fell in a heap.

===Chinese===

他看见一顶警盔从那惊愕汉子的脑袋上飞了出去，便又开一枪。这一回收获更佳：一个正指挥拖拉机走向的警察软瘫下去，倒作一堆。

===Original===

A shrill whistle blew; the policemen ran to cover, leaving the machine unattended. Again he fired, this time at the driver of the tractor. He saw the man scramble down from his seat and run for the shelter of the fence.

===Chinese===

一声尖哨吹响；警察们奔进掩体，把那台机器撂下不管。他再开一枪，这回打的是拖拉机手。只见那人从座位上翻爬下来，奔向围栏的掩蔽处去了。

===Original===

A quarter of an hour passed without any sign of activity on the part of his enemies, and then eight men, armed with rifles, came racing across the ground towards the wire barrier. Oberzohn dropped his rifle, and, taking a grip of the first machine-gun in his hand, sighted it quickly. The staccato patter of the Maxim awakened the echoes. One man dropped; the line wavered. Again the shrill whistle, and they broke for cover, dragging their wounded companion with them.

===Chinese===

一刻钟过去，敌方毫无动静可寻；随后，八个带枪的人疾奔过空地，直扑铁丝网。欧伯佐恩撂下步枪，一手攥住头一挺机枪，飞快瞄定。马克沁哒哒的短点射惊醒了四野的回声。一人栽倒；队形一阵摇晃。尖哨再度响起，他们顿作鸟兽散，拖着挂彩的同伴躲进了掩体。

===Original===

"I was afraid of that," said Leon, biting his knuckles—sure evidence of his perturbation.

===Chinese===

「我就怕来这个。」莱昂咬着指节说——那是他心绪不宁的铁证。

===Original===

He had put a ladder against the wall of the factory, and now he climbed up on to the shaky roof and focused his glasses.

===Chinese===

他早在厂房墙根支了一架梯子，此刻爬上那颤巍巍的屋顶，对好了望远镜的焦距。

===Original===

"There's another Maxim on this side," he shouted down. And then, as he saw a man's head moving above the parapet, he jerked up his pistol and fired. He saw the stone splinters fly up and knew that it was not bad practice at four hundred yards. The shot had a double effect; it made the defenders cautious and aroused in them the necessary quantity of resentment.

===Chinese===

「这一边还有一挺马克沁。」他朝下喊道。这时他瞥见女儿墙上方有个人头晃动，猛地抬手枪便是一发。他看见石屑迸飞，知道这四百码的枪法不算离谱。这一枪收了双重之效：一面叫守方添了戒心，一面又恰到好处地煽起了他们的火气。

===Original===

He was hardly down before there was a splutter from the roof, and the whine and snap of machine-gun bullets; one slate tile shivered and its splinters leapt high in the air and dropped beside his hand.

===Chinese===

他还没来得及下去，屋顶上便噗地喷出一串火，机枪子弹呼啸着、噼啪炸响；一块石板瓦应声碎裂，瓦屑高高蹦起，落在他手边。

===Original===

The presence of the girl was the only complication. Without her, the end of Oberzohn and his companions was inevitable. Nobody realized this better than the doctor, eating a huge ham sandwich in the shelter of the parapet—an unusual luxury, for he ate few solids.

===Chinese===

姑娘还在敌手之中，这是唯一的变数。没有她，欧伯佐恩及其同伙的结局早已注定。这层道理，谁也不比博士本人更了然——他正躲在女儿墙的掩蔽里，大嚼一只硕大的火腿三明治；这是一桩难得的奢侈，因为他平素很少吃固体食物。

===Original===

"This will be very shocking for our friends of Curzon Street," he said. "At this moment they bite their hands in despair." (He was nearly right here.)

===Chinese===

「这对咱们柯曾街的那几位朋友，必将大大地刺激。」他说。「此时此刻，他们正咬着手，绝望得很呢。」（他这话已近乎猜中。）

===Original===

He peeped over the parapet. There was no policeman in sight. Even the trains that had roared at regular intervals along the viaduct had ceased to run, traffic being diverted to another route.

===Chinese===

他从女儿墙上探头窥望。视野里一个警察也无。连先前每隔一阵便沿高架桥轰鸣而过的火车也停了驶——车流已改走另一条线。

===Original===

At half-past twelve, looking through a peephole, he saw a long yellow line of men coming down Hangman's Lane, keeping to the shelter of the fence.

"Soldiers," he said, and for a second his voice quavered.

===Chinese===

十二点半，他借着一孔瞭望孔望出去，看见一条长长的、黄颜色的人线正沿绞刑吏巷而下，一路贴着围栏的掩蔽。

「兵。」他说；那一瞬，嗓音竟有些发颤。

===Original===

Soldiers they were. Presently they began to trickle into the grounds, one by one, each man finding his own cover. Simultaneously there came a flash and a crack from the nearest viaduct. A bullet smacked against the parapet and the sound of the ricochet was like the hum of a bee.

===Chinese===

来的真是兵。不多时，他们开始一个接一个渗入园地，各人自觅各自的掩蔽。与此同时，最近那座高架桥上火光一闪、枪声一响。一颗子弹啪地打在女儿墙上，跳弹的余音嗡的一声，恰似一只蜜蜂。

===Original===

Another menace had appeared simultaneously; a great, lumbering, awkward vehicle, that kept to the middle of the lane and turned its ungainly nose into the field. It was a tank, and Oberzohn knew that only the girl's safety stood between him and the dangling noose.

===Chinese===

与此同时，又一重威胁现了身：一辆庞大、笨重、跌跌撞撞的车辆，一路占着巷子正中，把那副蠢钝的鼻头拐进了田里。那是一辆坦克。欧伯佐恩心知肚明：横在他与那根悬荡的绞索之间的，如今只剩姑娘的平安这一样东西了。

===Original===

He went down to see her, unlocked the door, and found her, to his amazement, fast asleep. She got up at the sound of the key in the lock, and accepted the bread and meat and water he brought her without a word.

===Chinese===

他下去看她，开了锁，却愕然发现她睡得正酣。钥匙在锁孔里一响，她便起身；他送来的面包、肉和水，她一言不发地尽数收下。

===Original===

"What time is it?"

Oberzohn stared at her.

"That you should ask the time at such a moment!" he said.

===Chinese===

「几点钟了？」

欧伯佐恩直瞪着她。

「都到了这样的时刻，你竟然还问钟点！」他说。

===Original===

The room was in darkness but for the light he had switched on.

"It is noon, and our friends have brought soldiers. Ach! how important a woman you are, that the whole army should come out for you!"

===Chinese===

屋里一片漆黑，只有他捻亮的那盏灯亮着。

「现在是正午，咱们的朋友把兵搬来了。啊！你这个女人好大的分量，竟劳动整支大军出马来迎你！」

===Original===

Sarcasm was wasted on Mirabelle.

"What is going to happen—now?"

===Chinese===

挖苦话落在米拉贝尔身上，纯属虚掷。

「现在——会发生什么事？」

===Original===

"I do not know." He shrugged his shoulders. "They have brought a diabolical instrument into the grounds. They may use it, to give them cover, so that the door may be blown in. At that moment I place you in the snake-room. This I shall tell our friends very quickly."

===Chinese===

「我不得而知。」他耸了耸肩。「他们把一件邪门的器械开进了园地。他们或许要拿它做掩蔽，好教正门被炸开。到那个时辰，我便把你安置进蛇室。这话，我自会飞快地告诉咱们的朋友。」

===Original===

She gazed at him in horror.

"You wouldn't do anything so wicked, Mr. Oberzohn!"

===Chinese===

她惊恐地凝视着他。

「你总不至于干出这样歹毒的事来吧，欧伯佐恩先生！」

===Original===

Up and down went the skin of his forehead.

"That I shall tell them and that I shall do," he said, and locked her in with this comfortless assurance.

===Chinese===

他额头上的皮皱上去，又落下来。

「这话我要告诉他们，这事我也要照办。」他说，随即把她锁在屋里，留下的只是这句毫无暖意的许诺。

===Original===

He went into his study and, fastening the door, took two strands of wire from his pocket and repaired the broken telephone connections.

===Chinese===

他走进书房，闩上门，从口袋里摸出两股电线，把剪断的电话线接续完好。

===Original===

"I wish to speak to Meadows," he said to the man who answered him—a police officer who had been stationed at the exchange to answer any call from this connection.

"I will put you through to him," was the reply.

===Chinese===

「我要同米多斯讲话。」他对接话的人说——那是一名派驻交换台前、专接这条线路来电的警官。

「这就替您接过去。」对方答道。

===Original===

For a moment the doctor was surprised that Meadows was not at the exchange. He did not know then that a field telephone line had been organized, and that the factory headquarters of the directing staff was in communication with the world.

===Chinese===

米多斯竟不在交换台前，博士一时略感诧异。他那时还不知道，一条战地电话线早已架设停当，设在厂房里的指挥班子的总部，正同外面的世界声气相通。

===Original===

It was not Meadows, but another man who answered him, and by his tone of authority Oberzohn guessed that some higher police official than Meadows was on the spot.

"I am the doctor Oberzohn," he barked. "You have brought a tank machine to attack me. If this approaches beyond the wire fence, I shall place the woman Leicester in the home of the snakes, and there I will bind her and release my little friends to avenge me."

===Chinese===

接他电话的不是米多斯，是另一个男人；听那发号施令的口气，欧伯佐恩猜到场面上来了位比米多斯官阶更高的警官。

「我是欧伯佐恩博士。」他厉声道。「你们开来一台坦克机器攻我。它若越过铁丝网一步，我便把莱斯特那个女人放进蛇的家园，在那里把她捆起，放出我的小朋友们替我复仇。」

===Original===

"Look here—" began the officer, but Oberzohn hung up on him.

===Chinese===

「你听着——」警官刚开口，欧伯佐恩已把电话挂断了。

===Original===

He went out and locked the door, putting the key in his pocket. His one doubt was of the loyalty of his companions. But here, strangely enough, he underrated their faith in him. The very mildness of the attack, the seeming reluctance of the soldiers to fire, had raised their hopes and spirits; and when, a quarter of an hour later, they saw the tank turn about and go out into Hangman's Lane, they were almost jubilant.

===Chinese===

他出门落锁，钥匙揣进衣袋。他唯一拿不准的，是同伙们的忠心。可恰恰在这一节上，说来奇怪，他低估了他们对他的信服。攻势偏偏这样温和，士兵们开枪又显得那样迟疑，早已把他们的指望与士气抬升起来；一刻钟后，眼见坦克掉转车头、退出绞刑吏巷而去，他们几乎要欢腾起来了。

===Original===

"You're sure that he will carry out his threat?" asked the police chief.

"Certain," said Leon emphatically. "There is nothing on earth that will stop Oberzohn. You will force the house to find a man who has died by his own hand, and—" He shuddered at the thought. "The only thing to be done is to wait for the night. If Washington arrives on time, I think we can save Miss Leicester."

===Chinese===

「他真会兑现那威胁，说到做到？」警察总监问。

「笃定。」莱昂斩钉截铁。「世上没有任何东西拦得住欧伯佐恩。你们把宅子攻下来，只会寻见一个死在自己手里的人，而且——」想到此处，他不寒而栗。「眼下唯一可做的，便是等天黑。华盛顿若能准时赶到，我想咱们救得下莱斯特小姐。」

===Original===

From the roof Dr. Oberzohn saw that the soldiers were digging a line of trenches, and sent a spatter of machine-gun bullets in their direction. They stopped their work for a moment to look round, and then went on digging, as though nothing had happened.

===Chinese===

欧伯佐恩博士从屋顶上看见士兵们正在掘一道堑壕，便朝那头扫去一梭机枪弹。他们停了一停，四下张望，随即接着掘了下去，仿佛什么事也不曾发生。

===Original===

The supply of ammunition was not inexhaustible, and he determined to reserve any further fire until the attack grew more active. Looking over the top of the parapet to examine the ground immediately below, something hot and vicious snicked his ear. He saw the brickwork of the chimney behind him crumble and scatter, and, putting up his hand, felt blood.

===Chinese===

弹药并非取之不竭，他决意省下火力，待攻势转紧时再打。他从女儿墙顶上探身，查看正贴墙脚下的那片地面，一个火辣辣、恶狠狠的东西嗖地擦过他的耳朵。他看见身后烟囱的砖体迸裂四散，抬手一摸，摸到了血。

===Original===

"You'd better keep down, Oberzohn," said Cuccini, crouching in the shelter of the parapet. "They nearly got you then. They're firing from that railway embankment. Have you had a talk with the boss of these birds?"

===Chinese===

「你最好猫低些，欧伯佐恩。」库奇尼蹲在女儿墙的掩蔽里说。「方才差一点儿就把你办了。他们是从那道铁路路堤上放的枪。这帮家伙的总头儿，你同他搭上话没有？」

===Original===

"They are weakening," said Oberzohn promptly. "Always they are asking me if I will surrender the men; always I reply, 'Never will I do anything so dishonourable.'"

Cuccini grunted, having his own views of the doctor's altruism.

===Chinese===

「他们气力不济了。」欧伯佐恩应声道。「他们总是问我，肯不肯把这些人交出去；我总是回答：『如此不名誉之事，我断然不为。』」

库奇尼咕哝了一声——对博士这份舍己为人，他自有一套看法。

===Original===

Late in the afternoon, a flight of aeroplanes appeared in the west: five machines flying in V formation. None of the men on the roof recognized the danger, standing rather in the attitude and spirit of sightseers. The machines were flying low; with the naked eye Cuccini could read their numbers long before they came within a hundred yards of the house. Suddenly the roof began to spout little fountains of asphalt. Oberzohn screamed a warning and darted to the stairway, and three men followed him out. Cuccini lay spreadeagled where he fell, two machine-gun bullets through his head.

===Chinese===

下晌时分，西边天际现出一队飞机：五架，列成 V 字队形。屋顶上的人没有一个识出危险来，反倒个个摆出一副看热闹的身段与心肠。飞机飞得极低；离宅子还有一百码开外，库奇尼凭肉眼便认出了机身上的编号。骤然间，屋顶噗噗地喷起一股股小小的沥青泉。欧伯佐恩尖叫示警，一头扎进楼梯口，三个人跟着他逃了出来。库奇尼摊成一个大字，倒在他倒下的地方，两颗机枪子弹洞穿了他的头。

===Original===

The fighting machines mounted, turned and came back. Standing on the floor below, Oberzohn heard the roar of their engines as they passed, and went incautiously to the roof, to discover that the guns of flying machines fire equally well from the tail. He was nearer to death then than he had ever been. One bullet hit the tip of his finger and sliced it off neatly. With a scream of pain he half fell, half staggered to safety, spluttering strange oaths in German.

===Chinese===

战斗机群爬升、转弯，又折了回来。欧伯佐恩立在下面一层，听它们掠过头顶时发动机的轰鸣，又冒失失地走上屋顶——这才领教，飞机上的机枪打起机尾来，也一样又狠又准。那一刻，他离死亡比生平任何时候都近。一颗子弹打中他的指尖，齐齐削去一片。他痛得一声尖叫，半跌半撞地逃进安全地带，嘴里用德语迸出一串古怪的咒誓。

===Original===

The aeroplanes did not return. He waited until their noise had died away before he again ventured to the roof, to find the sky clear. Cuccini was dead, and it was characteristic of his three friends that they should make a thorough search of his pockets before they heaved the body over the parapet.

===Chinese===

飞机没有再回来。他直等到那片轰鸣消散殆尽，才再次冒险上得屋顶，只见天空一片净空。库奇尼死了。他那三位朋友的做派也恰如其人：先把死者的口袋细细搜刮一遍，才把尸身从女儿墙上掀了出去。

===Original===

Oberzohn left the three on the roof, with strict instructions that they were to dive to cover at the first glint of white wings, and went down into his study. The death of Cuccini was in some ways a blessing. The man was full of suspicion; his heart was not in the fight, and the aeroplane gunner had merely anticipated the doctor's own plan.

===Chinese===

欧伯佐恩把那三人留在屋顶，严令他们一见白色机翼的闪光便立刻扑倒掩蔽，自己下楼进了书房。库奇尼之死，从某几方面看，倒是一桩幸事。这人满腹狐疑；打起来心思又不在上头；飞机上那位机枪手，不过是替博士把他自己肚里的计划提前办了。

===Original===

Cuccini was a Latin, who spoke English well and wrote it badly. He had a characteristic hand, which it amused Oberzohn to copy, for the doctor was skilful with his pen. All through the next three hours he wrote, breaking off his labours at intervals to visit the guard on the roof. At last he had finished, and Cuccini's sprawling signature was affixed to the bottom of the third page. Oberzohn called down one of the men.

===Chinese===

库奇尼是个拉丁人，英语说得好，写得糟。他那一笔字自具面目，欧伯佐恩模仿起来颇得其乐——博士这支笔本就来得娴熟。此后整整三个钟头，他一路写下去，时时辍笔，上屋顶视察一回岗哨。终于大功告成，第三页页尾落上了库奇尼那手铺张潦草的签名。欧伯佐恩唤下一个人来。

===Original===

"This is the statement of Cuccini which he left. Will you put your name to his signature?"

"What is it?" asked the man surlily.

"It is a letter which the good Cuccini made—what generosity! In this he says that he alone was to blame for bringing you here, and nobody else. Also that he kept you by threats."

===Chinese===

「这是库奇尼留下的自白。你可愿连着他的签名，也署上你的名字？」

「这是什么东西？」那人没好气地问。

「这是好心的库奇尼立下的一封信——何等慷慨！信里说，把你们引到此处，罪责全在他一人，更无别人。还说他全凭威胁把你们扣在此地。」

===Original===

"And you?" asked the man.

"Also me," said Oberzohn, unabashed. "What does it matter? Cuccini is dead. May he not in his death save us all? Come, come, my good friend, you are a fool if you do not sign. After that, send down our friends that they may also sign."

===Chinese===

「那么你呢？」那人问。

「也有我。」欧伯佐恩面不改色。「这又有何打紧？库奇尼死了。他死都死了，难道不能救咱们大家一命？来来，我的好朋友，你不签字，便是个傻瓜。签完之后，把咱们的朋友也都唤下来，教他们一一签字。」

===Original===

A reluctant signature was fixed, and the other men came one by one, and one by one signed their names, content to stand by the graft which the doctor indicated, exculpating themselves from all responsibility in the defence.

===Chinese===

一份勉强的签名落了纸；其余的人一个接一个上来，又一个接一个签下名字，都乐得认下博士炮制的这套伪证，把各自在顽抗中的干系撇了个一干二净。

===Original===

Dusk fell and night came blackly, with clouds sweeping up from the west and a chill rain falling. Gonsalez, moodily apart from his companions, watched the dark bulk of the house fade into the background with an ever-increasing misery. What these men did after did not matter—to them. A policeman had been killed, and they stood equally guilty of murder in the eyes of the law. They could now pile horror upon horror, for the worst had happened. His only hope was that they did not know the inevitability of their punishment.

===Chinese===

暮色四合，黑夜沉沉压下，云头自西边席卷而来，冷雨纷纷。冈萨雷斯闷闷地离群独处，望着宅子那团黑黢黢的庞然巨影一点点隐入夜幕，心头凄苦愈积愈深。这伙人此后再做什么都已无关紧要——对他们而言。一名警察已然毙命，在法律眼里，他们人人同犯谋杀。如今他们尽可以恐怖上加恐怖，因为最坏的已然发生。他唯一指望的，是他们并不知道自己的刑罚已在劫难逃。

===Original===

No orders for attack had been given. The soldiers were standing by, and even the attack by the aeroplanes had been due to a misapprehension of orders. He had seen Cuccini's body fall, and as soon as night came he determined to approach the house to discover if there was any other way in than the entrance by the front door.

===Chinese===

攻击令并未下达。士兵们枕戈待命，连飞机那一场来袭，也是出于命令的误会。他亲眼看见库奇尼的尸身坠落；天一擦黑，他便打定主意摸近宅子，探一探除正门那条进路之外，可还有别的门道入内。

===Original===

The aeroplanes had done something more than sweep the roof with their guns. Late in the evening there arrived by special messenger telescopic photographs of the building, which the military commander and the police chief examined with interest.

===Chinese===

飞机干下的，还不止用机枪扫荡屋顶。入夜之后，一名专差送来了这栋建筑的长焦远摄照片，军方指挥官与警察总监都饶有兴味地查验了一番。

===Original===

Leon was watching the house when he saw a white beam of light shoot out and begin a circular sweep of the grounds. He expected this; the meaning of the connections in the wall was clear. He knew, too, how long that experiment would last. A quarter of an hour after the searchlight began its erratic survey of the ground, the lamp went out, the police having disconnected the current. But it was only for a little while, and in less than an hour the light was showing again.

===Chinese===

莱昂正监视着宅子，忽见一道白光射出，开始绕着园地兜圈扫视。这是他早料到的；墙里那些接线的用意，早已一目了然。这番试验能撑多久，他心里同样有数。探照灯在地面上一阵没头没脑地巡视，一刻钟后灯便熄了——警方掐断了电流。可这也不过断得一时；不出一个钟头，那光又亮了起来。

===Original===

"He has power in the house—a dynamo and a gas engine," explained Gonsalez.

===Chinese===

「宅子里自有电源——一台发电机，一台煤气发动机。」冈萨雷斯解释道。

===Original===

Poiccart had been to town and had returned with a long and heavy steel cylinder, which Leon and Manfred carried between them into the open and left. They were sniped vigorously from the roof, and although the firing was rather wild, the officer in charge of the operations forbade any further movement in daylight.

===Chinese===

波伊卡特进了一趟城，回来时带回一只长大沉重的钢瓶；莱昂与曼弗雷德两人合力，把它抬到露天处放下。屋顶上的冷枪朝他们打得很凶；枪法虽颇散漫，主持全局的那位军官还是下令：白天再不许有任何行动。

===Original===

At midnight came the blessed Washington. They had been waiting for him with eagerness, for he, of all men, knew something that they did not know. Briefly, Leon described the snake-room and its contents. He was not absolutely certain of some of the species, but his description was near enough to give the snake expert an idea of the species.

===Chinese===

午夜时分，福星华盛顿到了。众人翘盼他已久，因为普天之下，唯有他知道一件他们不知道的事。莱昂扼要地把蛇室及其内存描述了一番。有几种他拿得不十分准，但这番描述已足够教这位蛇类行家听出个门道。

===Original===

"Yes, sir, they're all deadly," said Washington, shaking his head. "I guess there isn't a thing there, bar the scorps, who wouldn't put a grown man to sleep in five minutes—ten minutes at the most."

===Chinese===

「没错，先生，样样要命。」华盛顿摇着头说。「依我估摸，那里头没有一样东西——除了蝎子——不能在五分钟里送一条大汉子长眠不醒，至多十分钟。」

===Original===

They showed him the remains of the dead snake and he instantly recognized the kind, as the zoological expert had done in the afternoon.

"That's mamba. He's nearly the deadliest of all. You didn't see a fellow with a long bill-shaped head? You did? Well, that's fer-de-lance, and he's almost as bad. The little red fellows were corals.…"

===Chinese===

他们把那条死蛇的残骸指给他看，他一眼便认出了种类，一如下午那位动物学专家之所为。

「那是曼巴。它差不多是全天底下最毒的了。你没瞧见一个长着鸟喙般尖长脑袋的家伙？瞧见了？喏，那是矛头蛇，毒性也几乎不相上下。那些小红家伙，是珊瑚蛇……」

===Original===

Leon questioned him more closely.

"No, sir, they don't leap—that's not their way. A tree snake will hang on to something overhead and get you as you pass, and they'll swing from the floor, but their head's got to touch the floor first. The poor little fellow that killed Gurther was scared, and when they're scared they'll lash up at you—I've known a man to be bitten in the throat by a snake that whipped up from the ground. But usually they're satisfied to get your leg."

===Chinese===

莱昂又细细追问。

「不，先生，它们不蹦——那不是它们的路数。树蛇会攀在头顶的什么东西上，等你打底下经过时下手；它们也能从地上甩起来咬人，可蛇头总得先沾着地。咬死格瑟的那条可怜的小家伙是受了惊；蛇一受惊，就会朝你猛甩上来——我认得一个人，叫一条从地皮上抽起来的蛇咬中了喉咙。不过寻常时候，它们咬着你一条腿，也就心满意足了。」

===Original===

Leon told him his plan.

"I'll come along with you," said Washington without hesitation.

===Chinese===

莱昂把自己的计划讲给他听。

「我跟你们一道去。」华盛顿毫不迟疑地说。

===Original===

But this offer neither of the three would accept. Leon had only wanted the expert's opinion. There were scores of scientists in London, curators of museums and keepers of snakes, who could have told him everything there was to be known about the habits of the reptile in captivity. He needed somebody who had met the snake in his native environment.

===Chinese===

可这三个当中，没有一个肯应承。莱昂要的只是行家的一番见解。伦敦有的是科学家——博物馆的馆长、养蛇的管事——凡关在笼中的爬虫有甚习性，他们无所不晓。他需要的，却是一个在蛇的老家同蛇打过照面的人。

===Original===

An hour before daylight showed in the sky, there was a council of war, Leon put his scheme before the authorities, and the plan was approved. He did not wait for the necessary orders to be given, but, with Poiccart and Manfred, went to the place where they had left the cylinder, and, lifting it, made their slow way towards the house. In addition, Leon carried a light ladder and a small bag full of tools.

===Chinese===

天光透亮之前一个钟头，开了一场军事会议；莱昂把方案陈于当局面前，计划获得核准。他没有坐等必要的命令正式颁下，便同波伊卡特、曼弗雷德走到存放钢瓶的地方，抬将起来，一步一步慢慢朝宅子进发。此外，莱昂还扛着一架轻便长梯，提着一只装满工具的小包。

===Original===

The rays of the searchlight were moving erratically, and for a long time did not come in their direction. Suddenly they found themselves in a circle of dazzling light and fell flat on their faces. The machine-gun spat viciously, the earth was churned up under the torrent of bullets, but none of the men was hit; and, more important, the cylinder was not touched.

===Chinese===

探照灯的光柱没头没脑地游移，好一阵子都不朝他们这边照来。骤然间，他们发觉自己已被罩进一圈炫目的光里，齐齐扑倒在地。机枪恶狠狠地喷吐，大地在弹雨之下翻腾，可三个人毫发无伤；更要紧的是，那钢瓶也分毫无损。

===Original===

Then suddenly, from every part of the ground, firing started. The target was the searchlight, and the shooting had not gone on for more than a minute before the light went out, so jerkily that it was obvious that one bullet at least had got home.

===Chinese===

跟着，园地四面八方骤然枪声大作。目标正是那盏探照灯；打了不满一分钟，灯便熄了，熄得那样猝然，显然至少有一颗子弹打中了要害。

===Original===

"Now," said Manfred, and, lifting up the cylinder, they ran.

Poiccart put his hand on the fence wire and was hurled back. The top wire was alive, but evidently the doctor's dynamo was not capable of generating a current that would be fatal. Leon produced an insulated wire cutter and snipped off a six-foot length, earthing the broken ends of the wire. They were now under the shadow of the wall of the house, and out of danger so far as bullets were concerned.

===Chinese===

「就是现在。」曼弗雷德说；三人抬起钢瓶便跑。

波伊卡特一搭上围栏铁丝，整个人被震了回来。最上那根铁丝带着电，好在博士那台发电机显然发不出致命的电流。莱昂掏出一柄绝缘剪线钳，铰下六英尺长的一段，把断线两头一一接地。此刻他们已贴进宅墙的阴影里，就子弹而论，已出了险地。

===Original===

Leon planted his ladder against the window under which they stopped, and in a second had broken the glass, turned the catch and sent up the sash. From his bag he produced a small diamond drill and began to work through the thick steel plate. It was a terribly arduous job, and after ten minutes' labour he handed over the work to Manfred, who mounted in his place.

===Chinese===

莱昂把梯子架上他们停身的窗下，一眨眼便敲碎玻璃，拨开窗闩，推上窗扇。他从包里取出一支小小的金刚钻，对着那块厚钢板钻将起来。这活计苦不堪言；十分钟的劳作之后，他把位置让给曼弗雷德，曼弗雷德登上去接着钻。

---

===Original===

Whatever damage had been done to the searchlight had now been repaired, and its beam had concentrated on the spot where they had been last seen. This time no fusillade greeted its appearance, and Oberzohn was surprised and troubled by the inaction.

===Chinese===

探照灯先前受的损伤此时已修复如初，光柱死死钉在他们最后现身的那处地方。这一回，迎接灯亮的却没有一阵排枪；对面的按兵不动，叫欧伯佐恩又惊又疑。

===Original===

The light came into the sky, the walls grew grey and all objects sharply visible, when he saw the tank move out of the lane where it had been standing all the previous day, turn into the field, and slowly move towards the house. He set his teeth in a grin and, darting down the stairs, flung himself against the door of the girl's room, and his agitation was such that for a time he could not find the keyhole of the two locks that held the door secure.

===Chinese===

天光透上天际，墙垣转成灰色，万物的轮廓尖利分明；恰在此时，他望见坦克从巷子里开了出来——它在那一带整整停了一天——拐进田里，缓缓朝宅子逼来。他咬牙一咧嘴，飞奔下楼，扑向姑娘的房门；慌乱之下，那把双锁锁牢的门，他一时竟寻不着锁眼。

===Original===

It opened with a crash, and he almost fell into the room in his eagerness. Mirabelle Leicester was standing by the bed, her face white as death. Yet her voice was steady, almost unconcerned, when she asked: "What do you want?"

===Chinese===

门砰然而开，他急切之下几乎跌进屋去。米拉贝尔·莱斯特立在床边，脸白得像死。可她问「你要什么」时，声音却平稳，几乎是淡然的。

===Original===

"You!" he hissed. "You, my fine little lady—you are for the snakes!"

===Chinese===

「你！」他嘶声道。「你呀，我的标致小姐——你是留给蛇们的！」

===Original===

He flung himself upon her, though she offered no resistance, threw her back on the bed and snapped a pair of rusty handcuffs on her wrists. Pulling her to her feet, he dragged her from the room and down the stairs. He had some difficulty in opening the door of the snake-room, for he had wedged it close. The door was pushed open at last: the radiators were no longer burning. He could not afford the power. But the room was stiflingly hot, and when he turned on the lights, and she saw the long line of boxes, her knees gave way under her, and she would have fallen had he not put his arm about her waist. Dragging a heavy chair to the centre of the room, he pushed her down into it.

===Chinese===

他朝她扑将过去——她未作半分抵抗——把她掼回床上，一副生锈的手铐咔哒锁上她的双腕。他拽她起身，拖出房门，拽下楼去。蛇室的门他先前楔得死紧，开起来颇费了一番手脚。门终于推开：暖气片早已不烧了——那份电他供不起。可屋里仍闷热灼人；灯一亮，她望见长长一列箱子，双膝一软，若不是他一条胳膊兜住她的腰，几乎栽倒。他把一把沉重的椅子拖到屋子正中，把她按了下去。

===Original===

"Here you wait, my friend!" he yelled. "You shall wait… but not long!"

===Chinese===

「你在这儿等着，我的朋友！」他吼道。「你就等着吧……不用多久！」

===Original===

On the wall there were three long straps which were used for fastening the boxes when it was necessary to travel with them. In a second one thong was about her and buckled tight to the back of the chair. The second he put under the seat and fastened across her knees.

===Chinese===

墙上原有三条长皮带，是携箱出行时捆扎箱子所用。转眼间，一条已拦腰捆住她，紧紧扣死在椅背上。第二条他从椅座底下穿过，横过她的膝盖扎牢。

===Original===

"Goodbye, gracious lady!"

===Chinese===

「别了，雍容的小姐！」

===Original===

The rumble of the tank came to him in that room. But he had work to do. There was no time to open the boxes. The glass fronts might easily be broken. He ran along the line, hitting the glass with the barrel of his Mauser. The girl, staring in horror, saw a green head come into view through one opening; saw a sinuous shape slide gently to the floor.
