And then he turned out the lights, the door was slammed, and she was left alone in the room of terror.

===Chinese===

坦克的轰隆声传进这间屋来。可他还有事要办。开箱是来不及了；那些玻璃前脸，敲碎便是。他顺着那列箱子一路奔过去，用毛瑟枪的枪管逐一敲碎玻璃。姑娘惊恐万状地瞪着，看见一颗青绿色的头从一处破洞里探出；又看见一条蜿蜒的身形轻轻滑落到地板上。随后他熄了灯，门砰然关死，她独自一人，被留在了那间恐怖的屋子里。

===Original===

Oberzohn was no sooner in the passage than the first bomb exploded at the door. Splinters of wood flew past him, as he turned and raced up the stairs, feeling in his pocket as he went for the precious document which might yet clear him.

===Chinese===

欧伯佐恩前脚刚进过道，第一颗炸弹便在正门上炸响。木屑从他身边纷飞而过；他转身飞奔上楼，一路在口袋里摸索，去掏那份说不定还能为他开脱的紧要文书。

===Original===

*Boom!*

===Chinese===

*轰！*

===Original===

He had not locked the door of the snake-room; Leon had broken the hasp. Let them go in, if they wished. The front door was not down yet. From the landing above he listened over the balustrade. And then a greater explosion than ever shook the house, and after an interval of silence he heard somebody running along the passage and shake at the snake-room door.

===Chinese===

蛇室的门他没有上锁；莱昂早把搭扣砸坏了。他们要进，只管进。正门一时还轰不塌。他在楼上平台贴着栏杆侧耳细听。随后，一声前所未有的大爆炸撼动了整座宅子；静默了一阵，他听见有人奔过过道，摇撼起蛇室的门来。

===Original===

Too late now! He grinned his joy, went up the last flight to the roof, to find his three men in a state of mutiny, the quelling of which was not left to him. The glitter of a bayonet came through the door opening, a khaki figure slipped on to the roof, finger on trigger.

===Chinese===

如今迟了！他咧开嘴，笑出一腔快意，登上最后一段楼梯上到屋顶，却见他那三个人正闹着哗变——而弹压这场哗变的差事，已轮不到他来动手。一道刺刀的寒光自门洞探入，一个卡其色身影闪上屋顶，手指扣在扳机上。

===Original===

"Hands up, you!" he said, in a raucous Cockney voice.

Four pairs of hands went upward.

===Chinese===

「举起手来，你这家伙！」他用一副沙哑的伦敦土腔喝道。

四双手齐齐举向了天。

===Original===

Manfred followed the second soldier and caught the doctor by the arm.

"I want you, my friend," he said, and Oberzohn went obediently down the stairs.

===Chinese===

曼弗雷德跟着第二名士兵上来，一把攥住博士的臂膀。

「我要你，我的朋友。」他说；欧伯佐恩便服服帖帖地随他下了楼。

===Original===

They had to pass Gurther's room: the door was open, and Manfred pushed his prisoner inside, as Poiccart and Leon ran up the stairs.

===Chinese===

半路要经过格瑟那间屋：门开着，曼弗雷德把俘虏推了进去，恰逢波伊卡特与莱昂奔上楼来。

===Original===

"The girl's all right. The gas killed the snakes the moment they touched the floor, and Brother Washington is dealing with the live ones," said Leon rapidly.

===Chinese===

「姑娘没事。瓦斯一沾地板就把蛇熏死了，华盛顿老兄正在收拾活着的那些。」莱昂急急地说。

===Original===

He shut the door quickly. The doctor was alone for the first time in his life with the three men he hated and feared.

===Chinese===

他迅速关上了房门。博士平生头一遭，独自与他既恨又怕的那三个人关在了一处。

===Original===

"Oberzohn, this is the end," said Manfred.

That queer grimace that passed for a smile flitted across the puckered face of the doctor.

===Chinese===

「欧伯佐恩，到此为止了。」曼弗雷德说。

博士那张皱缩的脸上，掠过一丝权当微笑的古怪怪相。

===Original===

"I think not, my friends," he said. "Here is a statement by Cuccini. I am but the innocent victim, as you will see. Cuccini has confessed to all and has implicated his friends. I would not resist—why should I? I am an honest, respectable man, and a citizen of a great and friendly country. Behold!"

===Chinese===

「我以为不然，我的朋友们。」他说。「这里有一份库奇尼的自白。诸位自会看到，我不过是个无辜的受害者。库奇尼已尽数招认，并把他的朋友们一一攀咬在内。我不曾抗拒——何必抗拒？我是个诚实、体面的人，是一个伟大而友好的国家的公民。看哪！」

===Original===

He showed the paper. Manfred took it from his hand but did not read it.

===Chinese===

他亮出那张纸。曼弗雷德从他手里接过，却看也不看。

===Original===

"Also, whatever happens, your lady loses her beautiful hill of gold." He found joy in this reflection. "For tomorrow is the last day—"

===Chinese===

「再者，无论今日如何，你那位小姐那座美丽的金山，是保不住了。」他从这念头里咂摸出快意。「因为明天便是最后一天——」

===Original===

"Stand over there, Oberzohn," said Manfred, and pushed him against the wall. "You are judged. Though your confession may cheat the law, you will not cheat us."

===Chinese===

「站到那边去，欧伯佐恩。」曼弗雷德说着，把他推到墙边。「你已受审。你的自白或可骗过法律，却骗不过我们。」

===Original===

And then the doctor saw something and he screamed his fear. Leon Gonsalez was fixing a cigarette to the long black holder he had found in Gurther's room.

===Chinese===

便在这时，博士看见了什么，尖声叫出了他的恐惧。莱昂·冈萨雷斯正把一支香烟装上一支长长的黑色烟嘴——那烟嘴，是他从格瑟房中找出来的。

===Original===

"You hold it thus," said Leon, "do you not?" He dipped the cigarette down and pressed the small spring that was concealed in the black ebonite. "The holder is an insulated chamber that holds two small icy splinters—I found the mould in your laboratory, Herr Doktor. They drop into the cigarette, which is a metal one, and then…"

===Chinese===

「是这样拿的，」莱昂说，「对么？」他把烟头朝下一沉，压下藏在黑色硬橡胶里的那枚小弹簧。「这烟嘴是个隔热的小腔，内里盛着两枚细小的冰镖——模子嘛，我在你的实验室里寻见了，博士先生。它们坠进烟卷里——那是一支金属烟卷——然后……」

===Original===

He lifted it to his lips and blew. None saw the two tiny icicles fly. Only Oberzohn put his hand to his cheek with a strangled scream, glared for a second, and then went down like a heap of rags.

===Chinese===

他把它举到唇边，吹了一口气。谁也不曾看见那两枚小小的冰镖飞出。唯有欧伯佐恩抬手捂住面颊，迸出一声噎在喉咙里的尖叫，瞪视了一秒，随即像一堆破布似的瘫倒了。

===Original===

Leon met Inspector Meadows on his way up.

"I'm afraid our friend has gone," he said. "He has cheated the hangman of ten pounds."

"Dead?" said Meadows. "Suicide?"

===Chinese===

莱昂上楼时，迎面遇见米多斯探长。

「咱们这位朋友，怕是已经去了。」他说。「他赖了刽子手十英镑。」

「死了？」米多斯说。「自杀？」

===Original===

"It looks like a snakebite to me," said Leon carelessly, as he went down to find Mirabelle Leicester, half laughing, half crying, whilst an earnest Elijah Washington was explaining to her the admirable domestic qualities of snakes.

===Chinese===

「依我看，倒像是一口蛇咬。」莱昂漫不经心地说，径自下楼去寻米拉贝尔·莱斯特——她又是半个笑，又是半个哭；一旁那位一脸恳切的伊莱贾·华盛顿，正向她细数蛇类种种可钦可敬的居家美德。

===Original===

"There's five thousand dollars' worth dead," he said, in despair, "but there's enough left to start a circus!"

===Chinese===

「死了的这批，值五千美元，」他绝望地说，「可剩下的，也够开一座马戏团了！」

## XXXIV The Death Tube / 三十四 死亡吹管

===Original===
Later, Manfred explained to an interested police chief.
===Chinese===
事后，曼弗雷德向一位听得津津有味的警界首长作了一番解说。

===Original===
“Oberzohn secured the poison by taking a snake and extracting his venom—a simple process: you have but to make him angry, and he will bite on anything. The doctor discovered a way of blending these venoms to bring out the most deadly qualities of them all—it sounds fantastic, and, from the scientist’s point of view, unlikely. But it is nevertheless the fact. The venom was slightly diluted with water and enough to kill a dozen people was poured into a tiny mould and frozen.”
===Chinese===
「欧伯佐恩弄到毒药的法子，是捉一条蛇、榨取它的毒液——工序很简单：只消把它惹恼，它什么都肯咬。博士还琢磨出一种调配诸种蛇毒的门道，能把种种毒液里最致命的性能尽数激发出来——听着玄乎其玄，以科学家的眼光看也难以置信。然而事实便是如此。毒液用清水略微稀释，分量足以毒杀十来个人的，便注入一只小小的模子，冻了起来。」

===Original===
“Frozen?” said the chief, in astonishment.

Manfred nodded.
===Chinese===
「冻起来？」首长愕然道。

曼弗雷德点了点头。

===Original===
“There is no doubt about it,” he said. “Snake venom does not lose its potency by being frozen, and this method of moulding their darts was a very sane one, from their point of view. It was only necessary for a microscopic portion of the splinter to pierce the flesh. Sufficient instantly melted to cause death, and if the victim rubbed the place where he had been struck, it was more certain that he would rub some of the venom, which had melted on his cheek, into the wound. Usually they died instantly. The cigarette holders that were carried by Gurther and the other assassin, Pfeiffer, were blowpipes, the cigarette a hollow metal fake. By the time they blew their little ice darts, it was in a half-molten condition and carried sufficient liquid poison to kill, even if the skin was only punctured. And, of course, all that did not enter the skin melted before there could be any examination by the police. That is why you never found darts such as the bushmen use, slithers of bamboo, thorns from trees. Oberzohn had the simplest method of dealing with all opposition: he sent out his snake-men to intercept them, and only once did they fail—when they aimed at Leon and caught that snake-proof man, Elijah Washington!”
===Chinese===
「这一点毫无疑义。」他说。「蛇毒一经冰冻，毒性分毫不失；以他们的眼光看，这浇铸毒镖的法子也着实精明妥当。只消碎镖上极微小的一小片刺破皮肉便已足够——当场化开的分量便足以致命；倘若中镖的人去揉那被击中的地方，那就更是十拿九稳：他会把已在脸颊上化开的一些毒液揉进伤口。通常挨上一下的人当场便毙命。格瑟和另一名刺客法伊弗随身带的烟嘴，就是吹管；那支烟卷则是空心的金属假货。待他们把小小的冰镖吹出去时，镖身已呈半融状态，所携的液态毒药足以杀人，哪怕只扎破了点儿皮也尽够了。当然，凡不曾进入皮肉的毒液，不等警方来得及查验，早化得干干净净。这就是你们始终找不到毒镖的缘故——布须曼人用的那种，什么竹篾片啦、树上的硬刺啦。欧伯佐恩对付一切跟他作对的人，有个再简单不过的法子：派出他的蛇人半路拦截；他们只失手过一回——那一回瞄准的是莱昂，却打中了那个蛇毒不侵的伊莱贾·华盛顿！」

===Original===
“What about Miss Leicester’s claim to the goldfields of Biskara?”

Manfred smiled.
===Chinese===
「那么莱斯特小姐对比斯卡拉金矿的权益主张呢？」

曼弗雷德微微一笑。

===Original===
“The renewal has already been applied for and granted. Leon found at Heavytree Farm some blank sheets of notepaper signed with the girl’s name. He stole one during the aunt’s absence and filled up the blank with a formal request for renewal. I have just had a wire to say that the lease is extended.”
===Chinese===
「续展的手续早已申请，也已核准。莱昂在赫维特里农场寻到几张空白信纸，上头签着那姑娘的名字。他趁姨妈不在，偷走一张，在那片空白上填了一份正式的续展申请书。我刚接到一封电报，说租契已经展期。」

===Original===
He and Poiccart had to walk the best part of the way to New Cross before they could find a taxicab. Leon had gone on with the girl. Poiccart was worried about something, and did not speak his mind until the providential cab appeared on the scene and they were trundling along the New Cross Road.
===Chinese===
他和波伊卡特只得朝新克罗斯走了大半程，才寻到一辆出租汽车。莱昂已带着那姑娘先走了。波伊卡特心里搁着一桩事，却迟迟不露口风，直到那辆犹如天降的出租汽车出现、两人一路颠簸行在新克罗斯路上，才把心事吐露出来。

===Original===
“My dear George, I am a little troubled about Leon,” he said at last. “It seems almost impossible to believe, but—”

“But what?” asked Manfred good-humouredly, and knowing what was coming.
===Chinese===
「亲爱的乔治，我为莱昂有点儿犯愁。」他终于开口道。「这事几乎叫人无法相信，可是——」

「可是什么呢？」曼弗雷德和颜悦色地问，心里早知道接下来是什么。

===Original===
“You don’t believe,” said Poiccart in a hushed voice, as though he were discussing the advent of some world cataclysm—“you don’t believe that Leon is in love, do you?”

Manfred considered for a moment.

“Such things happen, even to just men,” he said, and Poiccart shook his head sadly.
===Chinese===
「你不会相信的，」波伊卡特压着嗓音说，仿佛在议论某场倾覆世界的大劫难将至——「你总不会相信，莱昂是坠入情网了吧，会么？」

曼弗雷德沉吟了片刻。

「这种事是会有的，义士也在所难免。」他说；波伊卡特黯然摇了摇头。

===Original===
“I have never contemplated such an unhappy contingency,” he said, and Manfred was laughing to himself all the way back to town.
===Chinese===
「这样一桩不幸的意外变故，我平生连想都不曾设想过。」他说；而曼弗雷德一路回到城里，都在暗自窃笑。
